    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn collect_pull_returns_entries_after_since_seq() {
        let pool = test_memory_pool().await.expect("pool");
        run_migrations(&pool).await.expect("migrate");
        ensure_sync_tables(&pool).await.expect("schema");
        let device = ensure_local_device(&pool, "pull-device")
            .await
            .expect("device");
        crate::repo::append_outbox(
            &pool,
            &device,
            "app_kv",
            "pull-a",
            "INSERT",
            r#"{"key":"pull-a","value":"1"}"#,
        )
        .await
        .expect("o1");
        let e2 = crate::repo::append_outbox(
            &pool,
            &device,
            "app_kv",
            "pull-b",
            "INSERT",
            r#"{"key":"pull-b","value":"2"}"#,
        )
        .await
        .expect("o2");
        let pulled = SyncEngine::collect_pull(&pool, &device, 1)
            .await
            .expect("pull");
        assert_eq!(pulled.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(pulled[0].seq, e2.seq);
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn collect_pull_for_replica_ignores_caller_device_id() {
        let pool = test_memory_pool().await.expect("pool");
        run_migrations(&pool).await.expect("migrate");
        ensure_sync_tables(&pool).await.expect("schema");
        save_deployment(
            &pool,
            &crate::deployment::SyncDeploymentConfig {
                schema_version: 1,
                mode: crate::deployment::DeploymentMode::PracticeDesktop,
                role: crate::deployment::DeviceRole::Master,
                device_label: "Main".into(),
                ..Default::default()
            },
        )
        .await
        .expect("deploy");
        let local = ensure_local_device(&pool, "Main").await.expect("device");
        crate::repo::append_outbox(
            &pool,
            &local,
            "app_kv",
            "from-master",
            "INSERT",
            r#"{"key":"from-master","value":"1"}"#,
        )
        .await
        .expect("outbox");
        let pulled = SyncEngine::collect_pull_for_replica(&pool, "unknown-replica-guess", 0, 0)
            .await
            .expect("pull");
        assert!(
            pulled.entries.iter().any(|e| e.entity_id == "from-master"),
            "master must serve its own outbox, not the replica-supplied device id"
        );
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn master_forwards_its_row_not_a_stale_replica_payload() {
        let pool = test_memory_pool().await.expect("pool");
        run_migrations(&pool).await.expect("migrate");
        ensure_sync_tables(&pool).await.expect("schema");
        save_deployment(
            &pool,
            &crate::deployment::SyncDeploymentConfig {
                schema_version: 1,
                mode: crate::deployment::DeploymentMode::ServerlessPeer,
                role: crate::deployment::DeviceRole::Master,
                device_label: "Main".into(),
                ..Default::default()
            },
        )
        .await
        .expect("deploy");
        let local = ensure_local_device(&pool, "Main").await.expect("device");
        let id = "00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000d1";
        sqlx::query(
            "INSERT INTO patient (id, name, date_of_birth, sex, insurance_number, status, updated_at)
             VALUES (?1, 'Master Newer', '1990-01-01', 'MALE', 'V-STALE', 'ACTIVE', '2099-03-01 00:00:00')",
        )
        .bind(id)
        .execute(&pool)
        .await
        .expect("seed");
        let entry = crate::repo::OutboxEntry {
            id: "stale".into(),
            device_id: "replica-a".into(),
            seq: 4,
            entity_table: "patient".into(),
            entity_id: id.into(),
            op: "UPDATE".into(),
            payload_json: format!(
                r#"{{"id":"{id}","name":"Stale Replica","updated_at":"2099-01-01T00:00:00Z"}}"#
            ),
            created_at: "2099-01-01T00:00:00Z".into(),
        };
        SyncEngine::ingest_push(&pool, "replica-a", std::slice::from_ref(&entry))
            .await
            .expect("ingest");
        let name: String = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT name FROM patient WHERE id = ?1")
            .bind(id)
            .fetch_one(&pool)
            .await
            .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(name, "Master Newer");
        let forwarded = crate::repo::list_entries_since(&pool, &local, 0)
            .await
            .expect("outbox");
        let row = forwarded
            .iter()
            .find(|e| e.entity_id == id)
            .expect("stale push must still be relayed from the master's current row");
        assert!(row.payload_json.contains("Master Newer"));
        assert!(!row.payload_json.contains("Stale Replica"));
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn replica_does_not_relay_a_peer_push() {
        let pool = test_memory_pool().await.expect("pool");
        run_migrations(&pool).await.expect("migrate");
        ensure_sync_tables(&pool).await.expect("schema");
        save_deployment(
            &pool,
            &crate::deployment::SyncDeploymentConfig {
                schema_version: 1,
                mode: crate::deployment::DeploymentMode::ServerlessPeer,
                role: crate::deployment::DeviceRole::Replica,
                device_label: "Replica".into(),
                ..Default::default()
            },
        )
        .await
        .expect("deploy");
        let local = ensure_local_device(&pool, "Replica").await.expect("device");
        let id = "00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000d2";
        let entry = crate::repo::OutboxEntry {
            id: "peer".into(),
            device_id: "replica-a".into(),
            seq: 1,
            entity_table: "patient".into(),
            entity_id: id.into(),
            op: "INSERT".into(),
            payload_json: format!(
                r#"{{"id":"{id}","name":"Peer","date_of_birth":"1990-01-01","sex":"MALE","insurance_number":"V-PEER","status":"ACTIVE","updated_at":"2099-01-01T00:00:00Z"}}"#
            ),
            created_at: "2099-01-01T00:00:00Z".into(),
        };
        SyncEngine::ingest_push(&pool, "replica-a", std::slice::from_ref(&entry))
            .await
            .expect("ingest");
        let n: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM sync_outbox WHERE device_id = ?1")
            .bind(&local)
            .fetch_one(&pool)
            .await
            .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(n, 0);
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn align_moves_pending_outbox_onto_the_activation_token_device() {
        use ed25519_dalek::SigningKey;
        use rand::rngs::OsRng;

        let pool = test_memory_pool().await.expect("pool");
        run_migrations(&pool).await.expect("migrate");
        ensure_sync_tables(&pool).await.expect("schema");
        let signing_key = SigningKey::generate(&mut OsRng);
        let previous_secret = std::env::var("MEDOC_CLUSTER_DEVICE_SECRET").ok();
        std::env::set_var(
            "MEDOC_CLUSTER_DEVICE_SECRET",
            "1111111111111111111111111111111111111111111111111111111111111111",
        );
        let fingerprint = crate::cluster::crypto::DeviceIdentity::try_load()
            .expect("identity")
            .expect("loaded")
            .fingerprint;
        let foreign = crate::pairing::mint_activation_token(
            &signing_key,
            &crate::pairing::ActivationTokenPayload {
                version: 2,
                device_id: "not-this-install".into(),
                slave_label: "Replica".into(),
                master_device_id: "master".into(),
                allowed_actions: vec!["sync.push".into()],
                issued_at: "2026-11-06T00:00:00Z".into(),
                nonce: "n".into(),
            },
        )
        .expect("mint");
        medoc_core::infrastructure::database::repos::admin::app_kv::set(
            &pool,
            crate::deployment::APP_KV_DEVICE_ID_KEY,
            "local-wrong",
        )
        .await
        .expect("kv");
        ensure_local_device(&pool, "Replica").await.expect("device");
        crate::repo::append_outbox(
            &pool,
            "local-wrong",
            "app_kv",
            "pending-key",
            "INSERT",
            r#"{"key":"pending-key","value":"1"}"#,
        )
        .await
        .expect("outbox");
        let mut cfg = crate::deployment::SyncDeploymentConfig {
            schema_version: 1,
            mode: crate::deployment::DeploymentMode::ServerlessPeer,
            role: crate::deployment::DeviceRole::Replica,
            device_label: "Replica".into(),
            activation_token: foreign,
            master_pubkey: crate::master_keys::pubkey_b64(&signing_key),
            ..Default::default()
        };
        let kept = align_local_id_to_activation(&pool, &cfg)
            .await
            .expect("foreign token");
        assert_eq!(kept, "local-wrong");
        sqlx::query(
            "INSERT INTO pairing_request (id, device_id, slave_pubkey, fingerprint)
             VALUES ('join-paired', 'paired-fp', '', 'paired-fp')",
        )
        .execute(&pool)
        .await
        .expect("pairing row");
        let paired = crate::pairing::mint_activation_token(
            &signing_key,
            &crate::pairing::ActivationTokenPayload {
                version: 2,
                device_id: "paired-fp".into(),
                slave_label: "Replica".into(),
                master_device_id: "master".into(),
                allowed_actions: vec!["sync.push".into()],
                issued_at: "2026-11-06T00:00:00Z".into(),
                nonce: "paired".into(),
            },
        )
        .expect("mint paired token");
        cfg.activation_token = paired;
        let adopted = align_local_id_to_activation(&pool, &cfg)
            .await
            .expect("joined token");
        assert_eq!(adopted, "paired-fp");
        let token = crate::pairing::mint_activation_token(
            &signing_key,
            &crate::pairing::ActivationTokenPayload {
                version: 2,
                device_id: fingerprint.clone(),
                slave_label: "Replica".into(),
                master_device_id: "master".into(),
                allowed_actions: vec!["sync.push".into()],
                issued_at: "2026-11-06T00:00:00Z".into(),
                nonce: "n2".into(),
            },
        )
        .expect("mint fingerprint token");
        cfg.activation_token = token;
        let id = align_local_id_to_activation(&pool, &cfg)
            .await
            .expect("align");
        assert_eq!(id, fingerprint);
        let pending = crate::repo::list_pending_outbox(&pool, &fingerprint, 500)
            .await
            .expect("pending");
        assert_eq!(pending.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(pending[0].device_id, fingerprint);
        let stored = medoc_core::infrastructure::database::repos::admin::app_kv::get(
            &pool,
            crate::deployment::APP_KV_DEVICE_ID_KEY,
        )
        .await
        .expect("read")
        .expect("device id");
        assert_eq!(stored, fingerprint);
        if let Some(previous) = previous_secret {
            std::env::set_var("MEDOC_CLUSTER_DEVICE_SECRET", previous);
        } else {
            std::env::remove_var("MEDOC_CLUSTER_DEVICE_SECRET");
        }
        let wrong: i64 =
            sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM sync_outbox WHERE device_id = 'local-wrong'")
                .fetch_one(&pool)
                .await
                .expect("count");
        assert_eq!(wrong, 0);
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn initial_copy_skips_push_and_second_pull_advances_through_seq() {
        let master = test_memory_pool().await.expect("pool");
        run_migrations(&master).await.expect("migrate");
        ensure_sync_tables(&master).await.expect("schema");
        save_deployment(
            &master,
            &crate::deployment::SyncDeploymentConfig {
                schema_version: 1,
                mode: crate::deployment::DeploymentMode::PracticeDesktop,
                role: crate::deployment::DeviceRole::Master,
                device_label: "Main".into(),
                ..Default::default()
            },
        )
        .await
        .expect("deploy");
        ensure_local_device(&master, "Main").await.expect("device");
        for (id, name, insurance) in [
            (
                "00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000c1",
                "Copy One",
                "V-COPY-1",
            ),
            (
                "00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000c2",
                "Copy Two",
                "V-COPY-2",
            ),
        ] {
            sqlx::query(
                "INSERT INTO patient (id, name, date_of_birth, sex, insurance_number, status, updated_at)
                 VALUES (?1, ?2, '1990-01-01', 'FEMALE', ?3, 'ACTIVE', '2099-03-01 00:00:00')",
            )
            .bind(id)
            .bind(name)
            .bind(insurance)
            .execute(&master)
            .await
            .expect("patient");
        }
        let first = SyncEngine::collect_pull_for_replica(&master, "new-replica-copy", 0, 0)
            .await
            .expect("first pull");
        let span = first.initial_copy.expect("snapshot span");
        assert!(span.end_seq > 1, "snapshot must cover more than one row");
        assert_eq!(span.through_seq, 0);
        assert_eq!(span.total, span.end_seq);
        assert!(!first.entries.is_empty());

        let second = SyncEngine::collect_pull_for_replica(&master, "new-replica-copy", 1, 0)
            .await
            .expect("second pull");
        let next = second.initial_copy.expect("copy still open");
        assert!(next.through_seq > span.through_seq);
        assert_eq!(next.through_seq, 1);
        assert_eq!(next.end_seq, span.end_seq);

        let replica = test_memory_pool().await.expect("pool");
        run_migrations(&replica).await.expect("migrate");
        ensure_sync_tables(&replica).await.expect("schema");
        save_deployment(
            &replica,
            &crate::deployment::SyncDeploymentConfig {
                schema_version: 1,
                mode: crate::deployment::DeploymentMode::ServerlessPeer,
                role: crate::deployment::DeviceRole::Replica,
                device_label: "Replica".into(),
                ..Default::default()
            },
        )
        .await
        .expect("replica deploy");
        sqlx::query(
            "INSERT INTO patient (id, name, date_of_birth, sex, insurance_number, status, updated_at)
             VALUES ('00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000c3', 'Local Seed', '1990-01-01', 'MALE', 'V-SEED', 'ACTIVE', '2099-03-01 00:00:00')",
        )
        .execute(&replica)
        .await
        .expect("seed patient");
        sqlx::query("INSERT INTO app_kv (key, value) VALUES ('ui.keep', 'yes')")
            .execute(&replica)
            .await
            .expect("kv");
        let report = SyncEngine::run_replica_sync(&replica).await.expect("sync");
        assert!(report.push.is_none());
        let err = report.error.expect("pull has no master url");
        assert!(
            !err.contains("push:"),
            "a new replica must not push before the snapshot is applied: {err}"
        );
        let kept: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM patient")
            .fetch_one(&replica)
            .await
            .expect("count");
        assert_eq!(kept, 1, "a failed pull must not wipe local rows");

        super::super::initial_copy::clear_practice_rows(&replica)
            .await
            .expect("clear");
        let patients: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM patient")
            .fetch_one(&replica)
            .await
            .expect("count");
        assert_eq!(patients, 0);
        let kv: Option<String> =
            sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT value FROM app_kv WHERE key = 'ui.keep'")
                .fetch_optional(&replica)
                .await
                .expect("kv");
        assert_eq!(kv.as_deref(), Some("yes"));

        let local = ensure_local_device(&master, "Main").await.expect("device");
        crate::repo::append_outbox(
            &master,
            &local,
            "patient",
            "00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000c9",
            "INSERT",
            r#"{"id":"00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000c9","name":"Live","updated_at":"2099-04-01T00:00:00Z"}"#,
        )
        .await
        .expect("live row");
        let live = SyncEngine::collect_pull_for_replica(&master, "new-replica-copy", 1, 0)
            .await
            .expect("live pull");
        assert!(
            live.live
                .iter()
                .any(|entry| entry.entity_id == "00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000c9"),
            "a change made after the snapshot must travel with the copy"
        );
        assert!(
            live.entries.iter().all(|entry| entry.seq <= next.end_seq),
            "the live row must not skip the snapshot cursor"
        );

        medoc_core::infrastructure::database::repos::admin::app_kv::set(
            &replica,
            "sync.initial_copy.cleared.v1",
            "1",
        )
        .await
        .expect("cleared");
        let skip = super::super::initial_copy::should_skip_push(&replica)
            .await
            .expect("skip");
        assert!(
            !skip,
            "an edit made on this device after the wipe has to be sent"
        );
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn packet_exchange_applies_each_devices_queue_on_the_other() {
        let main = test_memory_pool().await.expect("main");
        let replica = test_memory_pool().await.expect("replica");
        run_migrations(&main).await.expect("migrate main");
        run_migrations(&replica).await.expect("migrate replica");
        ensure_sync_tables(&main).await.expect("schema main");
        ensure_sync_tables(&replica).await.expect("schema replica");
        save_deployment(
            &main,
            &crate::deployment::SyncDeploymentConfig {
                schema_version: 1,
                mode: crate::deployment::DeploymentMode::PracticeDesktop,
                role: crate::deployment::DeviceRole::Master,
                device_label: "Main".into(),
                ..Default::default()
            },
        )
        .await
        .expect("main deploy");
        save_deployment(
            &replica,
            &crate::deployment::SyncDeploymentConfig {
                schema_version: 1,
                mode: crate::deployment::DeploymentMode::ServerlessPeer,
                role: crate::deployment::DeviceRole::Replica,
                device_label: "Replica".into(),
                ..Default::default()
            },
        )
        .await
        .expect("replica deploy");
        let main_id = ensure_local_device(&main, "Main").await.expect("main id");
        let replica_id = ensure_local_device(&replica, "Replica")
            .await
            .expect("replica id");

        let main_packet = SyncEngine::enqueue_update_packet(
            &main,
            "app_kv",
            "practice.packet.main",
            "INSERT",
            r#"{"key":"practice.packet.main","value":"from-main"}"#,
        )
        .await
        .expect("main packet");
        let replica_packet = SyncEngine::enqueue_update_packet(
            &replica,
            "app_kv",
            "practice.packet.replica",
            "INSERT",
            r#"{"key":"practice.packet.replica","value":"from-replica"}"#,
        )
        .await
        .expect("replica packet");
        let queued: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar(
            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM sync_packet_queue WHERE queue_name = 'parameters'",
        )
        .fetch_one(&main)
        .await
        .expect("queue count");
        assert_eq!(queued, 1);

        let replica_out = crate::repo::list_exchange_packets(&replica, &replica_id, &main_id, &[], 50)
            .await
            .expect("replica list");
        assert_eq!(replica_out.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(replica_out[0].id, replica_packet.id);

        let on_main = SyncEngine::serve_exchange(&main, &replica_id, &replica_out, 0, &[])
            .await
            .expect("main serve");
        assert_eq!(on_main.accepted, 1);
        let replica_value: String =
            sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT value FROM app_kv WHERE key = 'practice.packet.replica'")
                .fetch_one(&main)
                .await
                .expect("main stored replica value");
        assert_eq!(replica_value, "from-replica");
        assert!(
            on_main
                .packets
                .iter()
                .any(|packet| packet.entity_id == "practice.packet.main"),
            "main queue must be offered back"
        );

        let main_out: Vec<_> = on_main
            .packets
            .into_iter()
            .filter(|packet| packet.id == main_packet.id)
            .collect();
        let on_replica = SyncEngine::serve_exchange(&replica, &main_id, &main_out, 0, &[])
            .await
            .expect("replica serve");
        assert_eq!(on_replica.accepted, 1);
        let main_value: String =
            sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT value FROM app_kv WHERE key = 'practice.packet.main'")
                .fetch_one(&replica)
                .await
                .expect("replica stored main value");
        assert_eq!(main_value, "from-main");

        let acked = SyncEngine::serve_exchange(
            &main,
            &replica_id,
            &[],
            main_packet.seq,
            &[],
        )
        .await
        .expect("ack");
        assert!(
            acked
                .packets
                .iter()
                .all(|packet| packet.seq > main_packet.seq),
            "acknowledged main packets stay off the next page"
        );
    }
}

//! Sync engine report DTOs (serialised to Tauri IPC + LAN JSON).

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct SyncPushResult {
    pub accepted: u32,
    pub last_seq: i64,
}

/// Result of `POST /sync/exchange`. `packets` are the callee’s own queued
/// updates the caller has not acknowledged yet.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct SyncExchangePage {
    pub accepted: u32,
    pub last_seq: i64,
    pub packets: Vec<crate::repo::OutboxEntry>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct SyncPullResult {
    pub applied: u32,
    pub skipped: u32,
}

/// Span of the one-shot snapshot a new replica is copying.
/// Older clients ignore the field.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct InitialCopyProgress {
    pub total: i64,
    pub through_seq: i64,
    pub end_seq: i64,
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub device_label: Option<String>,
}

/// Pull page served to a replica. `initial_copy` is set only during a first copy.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct ReplicaPullPage {
    pub entries: Vec<crate::repo::OutboxEntry>,
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub initial_copy: Option<InitialCopyProgress>,
    /// Rows queued on the main device after the snapshot. Applied without
    /// moving the snapshot cursor, so a new calendar edit is not stuck
    /// behind the rest of the copy.
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Vec::is_empty")]
    pub live: Vec<crate::repo::OutboxEntry>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct SyncRunReport {
    pub push: Option<SyncPushResult>,
    pub pull: Option<SyncPullResult>,
    pub mesh: Option<MeshSyncReport>,
    pub error: Option<String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Default, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct MeshSyncReport {
    pub attempted: u32,
    pub succeeded: u32,
    pub errors: Vec<String>,
}

================================================================================
FILE: src/hlc.rs
================================================================================
//! Hybrid logical clock for sync conflict order.
//!
//! Wall time still breaks ties of real edits that are minutes apart. Once both
//! copies of a row carry a stamp, the comparison uses `(wall_ms, counter)` so
//! a replica whose clock is slow stays ordered after the master events it has
//! already seen.

use medoc_core::error::AppError;
use medoc_core::infrastructure::database::sync_registry::APP_KV_HLC_KEY;
use sqlx::SqlitePool;

pub use medoc_core::infrastructure::database::hlc::{format_hlc, observe, parse_hlc, tick, Hlc};

pub fn payload_hlc(obj: &serde_json::Map<String, serde_json::Value>) -> Option<Hlc> {
    obj.get("syncHlc")
        .and_then(|v| v.as_str())
        .and_then(parse_hlc)
}

pub async fn load_row(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    table: &str,
    entity_id: &str,
) -> Result<Option<Hlc>, AppError> {
    let row: Option<(i64, i64)> = sqlx::query_as(
        "SELECT wall_ms, counter FROM sync_row_hlc WHERE entity_table = ?1 AND entity_id = ?2",
    )
    .bind(table)
    .bind(entity_id)
    .fetch_optional(pool)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    Ok(row.map(|(wall_ms, counter)| Hlc { wall_ms, counter }))
}

pub async fn save_row(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    table: &str,
    entity_id: &str,
    hlc: Hlc,
) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    sqlx::query(
        "INSERT INTO sync_row_hlc (entity_table, entity_id, wall_ms, counter)
         VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4)
         ON CONFLICT(entity_table, entity_id) DO UPDATE SET
            wall_ms = excluded.wall_ms,
            counter = excluded.counter",
    )
    .bind(table)
    .bind(entity_id)
    .bind(hlc.wall_ms)
    .bind(hlc.counter)
    .execute(pool)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    Ok(())
}

pub async fn attach_row_hlc(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    table: &str,
    entity_id: &str,
    payload_json: &str,
) -> Result<String, AppError> {
    let Ok(mut value) = serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(payload_json) else {
        return Ok(payload_json.to_string());
    };
    let Some(obj) = value.as_object_mut() else {
        return Ok(payload_json.to_string());
    };
    if payload_hlc(obj).is_some() {
        return Ok(payload_json.to_string());
    }
    let Some(hlc) = load_row(pool, table, entity_id).await? else {
        return Ok(payload_json.to_string());
    };
    obj.insert(
        "syncHlc".to_string(),
        serde_json::Value::String(format_hlc(hlc)),
    );
    Ok(serde_json::to_string(&value).unwrap_or_else(|_| payload_json.to_string()))
}

pub async fn observe_clock(pool: &SqlitePool, remote: Hlc) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    let local = load_clock(pool).await?;
    let next = observe(local, remote, chrono::Utc::now().timestamp_millis());
    store_clock(pool, next).await
}

fn updated_at_millis(obj: &serde_json::Map<String, serde_json::Value>) -> Option<i64> {
    let raw = obj.get("updated_at")?.as_str()?.trim();
    if let Ok(parsed) = chrono::DateTime::parse_from_rfc3339(raw) {
        return Some(parsed.timestamp_millis());
    }
    let normalized = raw.replace(' ', "T");
    let with_zone = if normalized.ends_with('Z') {
        normalized
    } else {
        format!("{normalized}Z")
    };
    chrono::DateTime::parse_from_rfc3339(&with_zone)
        .ok()
        .map(|parsed| parsed.timestamp_millis())
}

pub async fn stamp_payload(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    table: &str,
    entity_id: &str,
    payload_json: &str,
) -> Result<String, AppError> {
    let Ok(mut value) = serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(payload_json) else {
        return Ok(payload_json.to_string());
    };
    let Some(obj) = value.as_object_mut() else {
        return Ok(payload_json.to_string());
    };
    let hlc = if let Some(existing) = payload_hlc(obj) {
        existing
    } else if let Some(saved) = load_row(pool, table, entity_id).await? {
        obj.insert(
            "syncHlc".to_string(),
            serde_json::Value::String(format_hlc(saved)),
        );
        saved
    } else if let Some(wall_ms) = updated_at_millis(obj) {
        let from_row = Hlc {
            wall_ms,
            counter: 0,
        };
        obj.insert(
            "syncHlc".to_string(),
            serde_json::Value::String(format_hlc(from_row)),
        );
        from_row
    } else {
        let next = tick(
            load_clock(pool).await?,
            chrono::Utc::now().timestamp_millis(),
        );
        store_clock(pool, next).await?;
        obj.insert(
            "syncHlc".to_string(),
            serde_json::Value::String(format_hlc(next)),
        );
        next
    };
    save_row(pool, table, entity_id, hlc).await?;
    Ok(serde_json::to_string(&value).unwrap_or_else(|_| payload_json.to_string()))
}

async fn load_clock(pool: &SqlitePool) -> Result<Hlc, AppError> {
    let raw = medoc_core::infrastructure::database::repos::admin::app_kv::get(pool, APP_KV_HLC_KEY)
        .await?;
    Ok(raw.as_deref().and_then(parse_hlc).unwrap_or(Hlc {
        wall_ms: 0,
        counter: 0,
    }))
}

async fn store_clock(pool: &SqlitePool, hlc: Hlc) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    medoc_core::infrastructure::database::repos::admin::app_kv::set(
        pool,
        APP_KV_HLC_KEY,
        &format_hlc(hlc),
    )
    .await
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn a_slow_physical_clock_stays_after_an_observed_master_stamp() {
        let master = Hlc {
            wall_ms: 1_000_000,
            counter: 4,
        };
        let replica_clock = observe(
            Hlc {
                wall_ms: 10,
                counter: 0,
            },
            master,
            10,
        );
        let edited = tick(replica_clock, 10);
        assert!(edited.wall_ms > master.wall_ms || edited.counter > master.counter);
        assert!(edited.wall_ms >= master.wall_ms);
    }

    #[test]
    fn parse_hlc_reads_wall_and_counter() {
        assert_eq!(
            parse_hlc("50:10"),
            Some(Hlc {
                wall_ms: 50,
                counter: 10,
            })
        );
    }
}

================================================================================
FILE: src/lib.rs
================================================================================
//! MeDoc serverless / peer replication.
//!
//! Devices keep a local SQLCipher database. Mutations are appended to an
//! **outbox** with a per-device sequence. A designated **master** device
//! arbitrates conflicts; **replicas** queue changes while offline and push
//! when the master is reachable (direct HTTPS on the LAN — no central server).

pub mod cluster;
pub mod deployment;
pub mod engine;
pub mod hlc;
pub mod master_keys;
pub mod merge;
pub mod net;
pub mod numbering;
pub mod pairing;
pub mod paths;
pub mod reconcile;
pub mod repo;
pub mod schema;
pub mod tls_pin;
pub mod watch;

pub use deployment::{DeploymentMode, DeviceRole, SyncDeploymentConfig};
pub use engine::{SyncEngine, SyncPullResult, SyncPushResult, SyncRunReport};
pub use merge::ConflictPolicy;
pub use pairing::{
    ActivationTokenPayload, PairingDecideResult, PairingDecision, PairingRequest,
    PairingRequestSubmit, ACTIVATION_TOKEN_PREFIX, DEFAULT_ALLOWED_ACTIONS,
};
pub use repo::{InitialCopyStatus, OutboxEntry, SyncPeer, SyncStatusSnapshot};

================================================================================
FILE: src/master_keys.rs
================================================================================
//! Cluster CA / master Ed25519 keypair for activation tokens, seat certificates, peer lists.
//!
//! The **private key never leaves this device**. Replicas only ever see the
//! 32-byte public key (via `GET /api/v1/pairing/master-info` or stored in
//! `SyncDeploymentConfig::master_pubkey`).
//!
//! Storage:
//! - Private key bytes: OS keychain via `medoc-core::secret_store` with
//!   account `pairing-master-signing-key`.
//! - Optional env override for tests: `MEDOC_PAIRING_MASTER_SECRET` (hex,
//!   64 chars). Mirrors how `MEDOC_LAN_JWT_SECRET` works.
//!
//! When the keychain is unavailable (CI, sandboxed tests, …) the env
//! override is the only escape hatch — there is no plaintext file
//! fallback.

use std::sync::Mutex;

use base64::{engine::general_purpose::STANDARD_NO_PAD, Engine};
use ed25519_dalek::{Signer, SigningKey, VerifyingKey};
use medoc_core::error::AppError;
use medoc_core::infrastructure::secret_store;

const SECRET_ACCOUNT: &str = "pairing-master-signing-key";

fn master_key_account() -> String {
    match std::env::var("MEDOC_INSTANCE") {
        Ok(n) if !n.is_empty() && n != "1" => format!("{SECRET_ACCOUNT}.{n}"),
        _ => SECRET_ACCOUNT.to_string(),
    }
}

static CACHED_SEED: Mutex<Option<[u8; 32]>> = Mutex::new(None);

fn signing_key_from_seed(seed: [u8; 32]) -> SigningKey {
    SigningKey::from_bytes(&seed)
}

/// Load (or create) the master signing key. Private bytes stay in this process.
pub fn load_or_create() -> Result<SigningKey, AppError> {
    if let Ok(env) = std::env::var("MEDOC_PAIRING_MASTER_SECRET") {
        return from_hex(env.trim());
    }
    if let Ok(guard) = CACHED_SEED.lock() {
        if let Some(seed) = *guard {
            return Ok(signing_key_from_seed(seed));
        }
    }
    let bytes = secret_store::load_or_create_bytes(&master_key_account(), None, 32)?;
    if bytes.len() != 32 {
        return Err(AppError::Internal(format!(
            "Master signing key must be 32 bytes (got {})",
            bytes.len()
        )));
    }
    let mut arr = [0u8; 32];
    arr.copy_from_slice(&bytes);
    if let Ok(mut guard) = CACHED_SEED.lock() {
        *guard = Some(arr);
    }
    Ok(signing_key_from_seed(arr))
}

/// Cluster license seed when it is a raw 32-byte ed25519 seed and differs from `current`.
///
/// Activation tokens are signed with the key stored on the license row. A later
/// keychain miss mints a second key; HTTP sync then rejects every existing token
/// with `Invalid signature`. Callers that sign or verify tokens must prefer the
/// license seed.
pub(crate) fn prefer_license_seed(current: &SigningKey, license_seed: Option<&[u8]>) -> SigningKey {
    let Some(raw) = license_seed else {
        return signing_key_from_seed(current.to_bytes());
    };
    let Ok(seed) = <[u8; 32]>::try_from(raw) else {
        return signing_key_from_seed(current.to_bytes());
    };
    let pinned = signing_key_from_seed(seed);
    if pinned.verifying_key().to_bytes() == current.verifying_key().to_bytes() {
        signing_key_from_seed(current.to_bytes())
    } else {
        pinned
    }
}

/// Signing key for activation tokens. Uses the cluster license seed when the
/// keychain key has drifted. The license row is left as the source of truth;
/// this process remembers it so later signs match the tokens already issued.
pub async fn align_to_license(pool: &sqlx::SqlitePool) -> Result<SigningKey, AppError> {
    if std::env::var("MEDOC_PAIRING_MASTER_SECRET").is_ok() {
        return load_or_create();
    }
    let current = load_or_create()?;
    let seed = match sqlx::query_scalar::<_, Vec<u8>>("SELECT signing_key_enc FROM license LIMIT 1")
        .fetch_optional(pool)
        .await
    {
        Ok(row) => row,
        Err(sqlx::Error::Database(db)) if db.message().contains("no such table") => None,
        Err(error) => return Err(AppError::Database(error)),
    };
    let chosen = prefer_license_seed(&current, seed.as_deref());
    if chosen.verifying_key().to_bytes() == current.verifying_key().to_bytes() {
        return Ok(current);
    }
    let raw = <[u8; 32]>::try_from(seed.unwrap_or_default()).map_err(|_| {
        AppError::Internal("license signing key drifted but seed was not 32 bytes".into())
    })?;
    tracing::warn!(
        target: "medoc::sync",
        event = "MASTER_SIGNING_KEY_ALIGNED_TO_LICENSE"
    );
    if let Ok(mut guard) = CACHED_SEED.lock() {
        *guard = Some(raw);
    }
    Ok(signing_key_from_seed(raw))
}

/// One-time import from owner activation manifest (32-byte ed25519 seed).
pub fn import_seed(seed: [u8; 32]) -> Result<SigningKey, AppError> {
    secret_store::store_bytes_replace(&master_key_account(), &seed)?;
    if let Ok(mut guard) = CACHED_SEED.lock() {
        *guard = Some(seed);
    }
    Ok(signing_key_from_seed(seed))
}

fn from_hex(hex: &str) -> Result<SigningKey, AppError> {
    if hex.len() != 64 {
        return Err(AppError::Validation(
            "MEDOC_PAIRING_MASTER_SECRET must be 64 hex chars (32 bytes)".into(),
        ));
    }
    let mut out = [0u8; 32];
    for (i, chunk) in hex.as_bytes().chunks(2).enumerate() {
        let pair = std::str::from_utf8(chunk)
            .map_err(|e| AppError::Validation(format!("master secret utf8: {e}")))?;
        out[i] = u8::from_str_radix(pair, 16)
            .map_err(|e| AppError::Validation(format!("master secret hex: {e}")))?;
    }
    Ok(SigningKey::from_bytes(&out))
}

/// Compact base64 (no-pad) representation of the master public key.
pub fn pubkey_b64(sk: &SigningKey) -> String {
    STANDARD_NO_PAD.encode(sk.verifying_key().to_bytes())
}

/// Parse a base64 (no-pad) 32-byte public key.
pub fn parse_pubkey_b64(b64: &str) -> Result<VerifyingKey, AppError> {
    let bytes = STANDARD_NO_PAD
        .decode(b64.trim())
        .map_err(|e| AppError::Validation(format!("Master pubkey not decodable: {e}")))?;
    if bytes.len() != 32 {
        return Err(AppError::Validation(format!(
            "Master pubkey length {} (expected 32)",
            bytes.len()
        )));
    }
    let mut arr = [0u8; 32];
    arr.copy_from_slice(&bytes);
    VerifyingKey::from_bytes(&arr)
        .map_err(|e| AppError::Validation(format!("Master pubkey invalid: {e}")))
}

/// Sign `body` (canonical JSON bytes) with the loaded master key.
pub fn sign(sk: &SigningKey, body: &[u8]) -> String {
    let sig = sk.sign(body);
    STANDARD_NO_PAD.encode(sig.to_bytes())
}

/// Verify a base64 (no-pad) signature against the master public key.
pub fn verify(pubkey: &VerifyingKey, body: &[u8], sig_b64: &str) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    let sig_bytes = STANDARD_NO_PAD
        .decode(sig_b64.trim())
        .map_err(|e| AppError::Validation(format!("Signature not decodable: {e}")))?;
    if sig_bytes.len() != 64 {
        return Err(AppError::Validation("Invalid signature length".into()));
    }
    let mut arr = [0u8; 64];
    arr.copy_from_slice(&sig_bytes);
    let sig = ed25519_dalek::Signature::from_bytes(&arr);
    // Strict verification rejects malleable / small-order signatures, so a
    // token has exactly one valid encoding (tokens are compared as strings).
    pubkey
        .verify_strict(body, &sig)
        .map_err(|_| AppError::Validation("Invalid signature".into()))
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::prefer_license_seed;
    use super::signing_key_from_seed;
    use crate::pairing::{mint_activation_token, verify_activation_token, ActivationTokenPayload};

    #[test]
    fn drifted_keychain_key_does_not_verify_a_license_signed_token() {
        let license = signing_key_from_seed([7u8; 32]);
        let drifted = signing_key_from_seed([9u8; 32]);
        let payload = ActivationTokenPayload {
            version: 2,
            device_id: "device-1".into(),
            slave_label: "Replica".into(),
            master_device_id: "master-1".into(),
            allowed_actions: vec!["sync.push".into(), "sync.pull".into()],
            issued_at: "2026-11-15T20:25:03Z".into(),
            nonce: "nonce-1".into(),
        };
        let token = mint_activation_token(&license, &payload).expect("mint");
        assert!(verify_activation_token(&token, &drifted.verifying_key()).is_err());
        let chosen = prefer_license_seed(&drifted, Some(&license.to_bytes()));
        assert!(verify_activation_token(&token, &chosen.verifying_key()).is_ok());
        assert_eq!(
            prefer_license_seed(&license, Some(&license.to_bytes()))
                .verifying_key()
                .to_bytes(),
            license.verifying_key().to_bytes()
        );
        assert_eq!(
            prefer_license_seed(&drifted, Some(&[1, 2, 3]))
                .verifying_key()
                .to_bytes(),
            drifted.verifying_key().to_bytes()
        );
    }
}
