//! L2/L4 mDNS discovery for `_medoc-cluster._tcp`.

use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::net::{IpAddr, Ipv4Addr, SocketAddr};
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};
use std::time::Duration;

use mdns_sd::{ServiceDaemon, ServiceEvent, ServiceInfo};
use medoc_core::error::AppError;

use super::listener::DEFAULT_CLUSTER_PORT;

pub const SERVICE_TYPE: &str = "_medoc-cluster._tcp.local.";
pub const SERVICE_NAME: &str = "MeDoc Cluster";

#[derive(Debug, Clone, serde::Serialize, serde::Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct AdminEndpoint {
    pub host: String,
    pub port: u16,
    pub instance_name: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub cluster_id: Option<String>,
}

pub struct MdnsResponder {
    _daemon: ServiceDaemon,
}

impl MdnsResponder {
    pub fn advertise(host_ip: &str, port: u16, cluster_id: &str) -> Result<Self, AppError> {
        let daemon = ServiceDaemon::new().map_err(map_mdns)?;
        let props = HashMap::from([("cluster_id".into(), cluster_id.into())]);
        let slug = cluster_id
            .chars()
            .filter(|c| c.is_ascii_alphanumeric())
            .take(12)
            .collect::<String>();
        let slug = if slug.is_empty() {
            "cluster".into()
        } else {
            slug
        };
        let hostname = format!("medoc-{slug}.local.");
        let service = ServiceInfo::new(
            SERVICE_TYPE,
            SERVICE_NAME,
            &hostname,
            host_ip,
            port,
            Some(props),
        )
        .map_err(map_mdns)?;
        daemon.register(service).map_err(map_mdns)?;
        Ok(Self { _daemon: daemon })
    }
}

pub fn scan_admins(timeout: Duration) -> Result<Vec<AdminEndpoint>, AppError> {
    let mut list = scan_mdns_admins(timeout)?;
    list.retain(|a| !a.host.trim().is_empty() && a.port != 0);
    for extra in probe_local_cluster_endpoints() {
        if !list
            .iter()
            .any(|a| a.host == extra.host && a.port == extra.port)
        {
            list.push(extra);
        }
    }
    Ok(list)
}

fn scan_mdns_admins(timeout: Duration) -> Result<Vec<AdminEndpoint>, AppError> {
    let daemon = ServiceDaemon::new().map_err(map_mdns)?;
    let receiver = daemon.browse(SERVICE_TYPE).map_err(map_mdns)?;
    let found: Arc<Mutex<Vec<AdminEndpoint>>> = Arc::new(Mutex::new(Vec::new()));
    let found2 = Arc::clone(&found);
    std::thread::spawn(move || {
        while let Ok(event) = receiver.recv() {
            if let ServiceEvent::ServiceResolved(info) = event {
                let host = pick_mdns_reachable_host(info.get_addresses().iter().copied());
                let port = info.get_port();
                let cluster_id = info
                    .get_properties()
                    .get("cluster_id")
                    .map(|version| version.val_str().to_string());
                if let Ok(mut list) = found2.lock() {
                    list.push(AdminEndpoint {
                        host,
                        port,
                        instance_name: info.get_fullname().to_string(),
                        cluster_id,
                    });
                }
            }
        }
    });
    std::thread::sleep(timeout);
    let list = found
        .lock()
        .map_err(|_| AppError::Internal("mdns lock".into()))?;
    Ok(list.clone())
}

/// Same-machine master (second MeDoc window) is often missed by mDNS. Probe TCP.
fn probe_local_cluster_endpoints() -> Vec<AdminEndpoint> {
    let mut hosts = vec!["127.0.0.1".to_string()];
    if let Some(ip) = medoc_core::discovery::pick_private_lan_ipv4() {
        let s = ip.to_string();
        if s != "127.0.0.1" {
            hosts.push(s);
        }
    }
    hosts
        .into_iter()
        .filter_map(|host| probe_cluster_tcp(&host, DEFAULT_CLUSTER_PORT))
        .collect()
}

fn probe_cluster_tcp(host: &str, port: u16) -> Option<AdminEndpoint> {
    let ip: IpAddr = host.parse().ok()?;
    let addr = SocketAddr::new(ip, port);
    std::net::TcpStream::connect_timeout(&addr, Duration::from_millis(400)).ok()?;
    Some(AdminEndpoint {
        host: host.to_string(),
        port,
        instance_name: format!("{SERVICE_NAME} ({host})"),
        cluster_id: None,
    })
}

fn pick_mdns_reachable_host(addrs: impl Iterator<Item = IpAddr>) -> String {
    let mut v4: Vec<Ipv4Addr> = Vec::new();
    let mut loopback: Vec<Ipv4Addr> = Vec::new();
    let mut v6_addrs: Vec<IpAddr> = Vec::new();
    for a in addrs {
        match a {
            IpAddr::V4(v) if v.is_loopback() => loopback.push(v),
            IpAddr::V4(v) if medoc_core::discovery::lan_ipv4_addr_score(v) >= 0 => v4.push(v),
            IpAddr::V6(ip6) if crate::net::is_private_lan_address(IpAddr::V6(ip6)) => {
                v6_addrs.push(a);
            }
            _ => {}
        }
    }
    v4.sort_by_key(|ip| std::cmp::Reverse(medoc_core::discovery::lan_ipv4_addr_score(*ip)));
    if let Some(ip) = v4.first() {
        return ip.to_string();
    }
    if let Some(ip) = loopback.first() {
        return ip.to_string();
    }
    v6_addrs.first().map(|a| a.to_string()).unwrap_or_default()
}

fn map_mdns(e: impl std::fmt::Display) -> AppError {
    AppError::Internal(format!("mdns: {e}"))
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn mdns_prefers_rfc1918_v4_over_link_local_v6() {
        let host = pick_mdns_reachable_host(
            [
                "fe80::1".parse().unwrap(),
                "192.168.1.40".parse().unwrap(),
                "172.17.0.2".parse().unwrap(),
            ]
            .into_iter(),
        );
        assert_eq!(host, "192.168.1.40");
    }

    #[test]
    fn mdns_falls_back_to_loopback_when_no_lan_v4() {
        let host = pick_mdns_reachable_host(["127.0.0.1".parse().unwrap()].into_iter());
        assert_eq!(host, "127.0.0.1");
    }
}

================================================================================
FILE: src/net/handshake.rs
================================================================================
//! Noise XX handshake over async TCP streams (length-prefixed handshake messages).

use medoc_core::error::AppError;
use snow::Keypair;
use tokio::io::{AsyncReadExt, AsyncWriteExt};
use tokio::net::TcpStream;

use super::transport::{generate_keypair, NoiseHandshake, NoiseTransport};

const HS_BUF: usize = 2048;
/// A peer that opens a TCP connection and then stays silent must not hold the
/// handler (and its thread) forever.
const HS_READ_TIMEOUT: std::time::Duration = std::time::Duration::from_secs(15);

async fn write_hs_message(stream: &mut TcpStream, data: &[u8]) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    let len = u16::try_from(data.len())
        .map_err(|_| AppError::Internal("handshake message too large".into()))?;
    stream
        .write_all(&len.to_be_bytes())
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::Internal(format!("handshake write len: {e}")))?;
    stream
        .write_all(data)
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::Internal(format!("handshake write: {e}")))?;
    Ok(())
}

async fn read_hs_message(stream: &mut TcpStream) -> Result<Vec<u8>, AppError> {
    let mut len_buf = [0u8; 2];
    tokio::time::timeout(HS_READ_TIMEOUT, stream.read_exact(&mut len_buf))
        .await
        .map_err(|_| AppError::Validation("handshake timed out".into()))?
        .map_err(|e| AppError::Internal(format!("handshake read len: {e}")))?;
    let len = usize::from(u16::from_be_bytes(len_buf));
    if len > HS_BUF {
        return Err(AppError::Validation("handshake message too large".into()));
    }
    let mut buf = vec![0u8; len];
    tokio::time::timeout(HS_READ_TIMEOUT, stream.read_exact(&mut buf))
        .await
        .map_err(|_| AppError::Validation("handshake timed out".into()))?
        .map_err(|e| AppError::Internal(format!("handshake read: {e}")))?;
    Ok(buf)
}

/// Run Noise XX as initiator on `stream` using a fresh local keypair.
pub async fn run_xx_initiator(
    stream: &mut TcpStream,
) -> Result<(NoiseTransport, Vec<u8>), AppError> {
    run_xx_initiator_with_keypair(stream, &generate_keypair()?).await
}

/// Run Noise XX as initiator with the given device keypair.
pub async fn run_xx_initiator_with_keypair(
    stream: &mut TcpStream,
    local: &Keypair,
) -> Result<(NoiseTransport, Vec<u8>), AppError> {
    let mut buf_out = vec![0u8; HS_BUF];
    let mut payload = vec![0u8; HS_BUF];

    let mut ini = NoiseHandshake::new_xx_initiator(local)?;

    let n1 = ini.write_message(&[], &mut buf_out)?;
    write_hs_message(stream, &buf_out[..n1]).await?;
    let msg2 = read_hs_message(stream).await?;
    let _ = ini.read_message(&msg2, &mut payload)?;

    let n3 = ini.write_message(&[], &mut buf_out)?;
    write_hs_message(stream, &buf_out[..n3]).await?;

    if !ini.is_complete() {
        return Err(AppError::Internal("Noise XX initiator incomplete".into()));
    }
    let transcript = ini.transcript().to_vec();
    let transport = ini.into_transport()?;
    Ok((transport, transcript))
}

/// Run Noise XX as responder on `stream`.
pub async fn run_xx_responder(
    stream: &mut TcpStream,
) -> Result<(NoiseTransport, Vec<u8>), AppError> {
    run_xx_responder_with_keypair(stream, &generate_keypair()?).await
}

/// Run Noise XX as responder with the given device keypair.
pub async fn run_xx_responder_with_keypair(
    stream: &mut TcpStream,
    local: &Keypair,
) -> Result<(NoiseTransport, Vec<u8>), AppError> {
    let mut buf_out = vec![0u8; HS_BUF];
    let mut payload = vec![0u8; HS_BUF];

    let mut res = NoiseHandshake::new_xx_responder(local)?;

    let msg1 = read_hs_message(stream).await?;
    let _ = res.read_message(&msg1, &mut payload)?;

    let n2 = res.write_message(&[], &mut buf_out)?;
    write_hs_message(stream, &buf_out[..n2]).await?;
    let msg3 = read_hs_message(stream).await?;
    let _ = res.read_message(&msg3, &mut payload)?;

    if !res.is_complete() {
        return Err(AppError::Internal("Noise XX responder incomplete".into()));
    }
    let transcript = res.transcript().to_vec();
    let transport = res.into_transport()?;
    Ok((transport, transcript))
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use tokio::net::TcpListener;

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn xx_handshake_over_tcp_loopback() {
        let listener = TcpListener::bind("127.0.0.1:0").await.expect("bind");
        let addr = listener.local_addr().expect("addr");
        let kp1 = generate_keypair().expect("kp1");
        let kp2 = generate_keypair().expect("kp2");

        let server = tokio::spawn(async move {
            let (mut stream, _) = listener.accept().await.expect("accept");
            run_xx_responder_with_keypair(&mut stream, &kp2)
                .await
                .expect("responder")
        });

        let mut client = TcpStream::connect(addr).await.expect("connect");
        let (_t1, tr1) = run_xx_initiator_with_keypair(&mut client, &kp1)
            .await
            .expect("initiator");
        let (_t2, tr2) = server.await.expect("server join");
        assert!(!tr1.is_empty());
        assert_eq!(tr1, tr2);
    }
}

================================================================================
FILE: src/net/join_client.rs
================================================================================
//! Joiner-side TCP client: Noise XX + JOIN_REQUEST / JOIN_ACCEPT.
//!
//! Every request carries an Ed25519 signature of the device key over the
//! Noise handshake transcript (see [`DeviceIdentity::sign_channel`]). It
//! proves possession of the key behind the fingerprint and binds the request
//! to this connection, so a LAN peer cannot impersonate a device by sending
//! its (public) fingerprint, and a relay/MITM is detected.

use std::future::Future;
use std::time::Duration;

use medoc_core::discovery::primary_local_ipv4;
use medoc_core::error::AppError;
use tokio::net::TcpStream;

use crate::cluster::crypto::{ChannelPurpose, DeviceIdentity};
use crate::cluster::SeatRole;

use super::channel::{recv_wire_message, send_wire_message};
use super::handshake::run_xx_initiator;
use super::transport::NoiseTransport;
use super::wire::{SeatRoleWire, WireMessage};

/// TCP connect timeout to the admin device.
const CONNECT_TIMEOUT: Duration = Duration::from_secs(10);
/// Upper bound for one complete request/response exchange.
const EXCHANGE_TIMEOUT: Duration = Duration::from_secs(60);

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct JoinOutcome {
    pub session_id: String,
    pub fingerprint: String,
    pub handshake_transcript: Vec<u8>,
}

/// LAN credentials returned after SAS so the replica can call the master's HTTPS server.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]
pub struct SasConfirmOutcome {
    pub activation_token: Option<String>,
    pub master_pubkey: Option<String>,
    pub master_device_id: Option<String>,
    pub master_cert_sha256: Option<String>,
    /// Main device's LAN HTTPS port, when it sent one.
    pub master_http_port: Option<u16>,
}

fn local_hostname() -> String {
    hostname::get()
        .ok()
        .and_then(|h| h.into_string().ok())
        .filter(|s| !s.trim().is_empty())
        .unwrap_or_else(|| "medoc-device".into())
}

async fn with_timeout<T>(
    what: &str,
    fut: impl Future<Output = Result<T, AppError>>,
) -> Result<T, AppError> {
    tokio::time::timeout(EXCHANGE_TIMEOUT, fut)
        .await
        .map_err(|_| AppError::Internal(format!("cluster {what}: timed out")))?
}

/// Connect + Noise XX. Returns the stream, transport and handshake transcript.
async fn open_channel(
    host: &str,
    port: u16,
    what: &str,
) -> Result<(TcpStream, NoiseTransport, Vec<u8>), AppError> {
    let addr = format!("{host}:{port}");
    let mut stream = tokio::time::timeout(CONNECT_TIMEOUT, TcpStream::connect(&addr))
        .await
        .map_err(|_| AppError::Internal(format!("cluster {what} connect {addr}: timed out")))?
        .map_err(|e| AppError::Internal(format!("cluster {what} connect {addr}: {e}")))?;
    let _ = stream.set_nodelay(true);
    let (transport, transcript) = run_xx_initiator(&mut stream).await?;
    Ok((stream, transport, transcript))
}

fn lan_outcome_from_accept(msg: WireMessage, what: &str) -> Result<SasConfirmOutcome, AppError> {
    match msg {
        WireMessage::JoinAccept {
            activation_token,
            master_pubkey,
            master_device_id,
            master_cert_sha256,
            master_http_port,
            ..
        } => Ok(SasConfirmOutcome {
            activation_token: activation_token.filter(|s| !s.trim().is_empty()),
            master_pubkey: master_pubkey.filter(|s| !s.trim().is_empty()),
            master_device_id: master_device_id.filter(|s| !s.trim().is_empty()),
            master_cert_sha256: master_cert_sha256
                .map(|pin| crate::tls_pin::normalize_sha256_pin(&pin))
                .filter(|pin| crate::tls_pin::is_sha256_pin(pin)),
            master_http_port: master_http_port.filter(|p| *p != 0),
        }),
        WireMessage::Error { code, message } => Err(AppError::Validation(format!(
            "cluster {what} rejected ({code}): {message}"
        ))),
        other => Err(AppError::Internal(format!(
            "unexpected cluster {what} response: {}",
            message_kind(&other)
        ))),
    }
}

/// Message type only — never log payloads (tokens, staff directory).
fn message_kind(msg: &WireMessage) -> &'static str {
    match msg {
        WireMessage::Discover => "DISCOVER",
        WireMessage::JoinRequest { .. } => "JOIN_REQUEST",
        WireMessage::JoinAccept { .. } => "JOIN_ACCEPT",
        WireMessage::SasConfirm { .. } => "SAS_CONFIRM",
        WireMessage::Provision { .. } => "PROVISION",
        WireMessage::MemberHello { .. } => "MEMBER_HELLO",
        WireMessage::DataRequest { .. } => "DATA_REQUEST",
        WireMessage::DataPush { .. } => "DATA_PUSH",
        WireMessage::Revoke { .. } => "REVOKE",
        WireMessage::ClusterReset { .. } => "CLUSTER_RESET",
        WireMessage::StaffDirectoryRequest { .. } => "STAFF_DIRECTORY_REQUEST",
        WireMessage::StaffDirectoryResponse { .. } => "STAFF_DIRECTORY_RESPONSE",
        WireMessage::ReplicaAuthRequest { .. } => "REPLICA_AUTH_REQUEST",
        WireMessage::ClusterStatusRequest { .. } => "CLUSTER_STATUS_REQUEST",
        WireMessage::ClusterStatusResponse { .. } => "CLUSTER_STATUS_RESPONSE",
        WireMessage::Error { .. } => "ERROR",
    }
}

/// Connect to an admin endpoint, perform Noise XX, send JOIN_REQUEST, read JOIN_ACCEPT.
pub async fn join_admin_endpoint(
    host: &str,
    port: u16,
    identity: &DeviceIdentity,
    requested_role: SeatRole,
) -> Result<JoinOutcome, AppError> {
    with_timeout("join", async {
        let (mut stream, mut transport, transcript) = open_channel(host, port, "join").await?;

        let ip = primary_local_ipv4().unwrap_or_else(|| host.to_string());
        let join_msg = WireMessage::JoinRequest {
            fingerprint: identity.fingerprint.clone(),
            pubkey: identity.pubkey_bytes.to_vec(),
            hostname: local_hostname(),
            os: std::env::consts::OS.into(),
            app_version: env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION").into(),
            ip,
            requested_role: SeatRoleWire::from(requested_role),
            signature: identity.sign_channel(ChannelPurpose::Join, &transcript),
        };
        send_wire_message(&mut stream, &mut transport, &join_msg).await?;

        match recv_wire_message(&mut stream, &mut transport).await? {
            WireMessage::JoinAccept { session_id, .. } if !session_id.trim().is_empty() => {
                Ok(JoinOutcome {
                    session_id,
                    fingerprint: identity.fingerprint.clone(),
                    handshake_transcript: transcript,
                })
            }
            WireMessage::Error { code, message } => Err(AppError::Validation(format!(
                "cluster join rejected ({code}): {message}"
            ))),
            other => Err(AppError::Internal(format!(
                "unexpected cluster response: {}",
                message_kind(&other)
            ))),
        }
    })
    .await
}

/// Fetch owner staff directory after cluster join (for local login on member devices).
pub async fn fetch_staff_directory(
    host: &str,
    port: u16,
    identity: &DeviceIdentity,
    session_id: &str,
) -> Result<String, AppError> {
    with_timeout("staff directory", async {
        let (mut stream, mut transport, transcript) =
            open_channel(host, port, "staff directory").await?;

        send_wire_message(
            &mut stream,
            &mut transport,
            &WireMessage::StaffDirectoryRequest {
                fingerprint: identity.fingerprint.clone(),
                session_id: session_id.trim().to_string(),
                pubkey: identity.pubkey_bytes.to_vec(),
                signature: identity.sign_channel(ChannelPurpose::StaffDirectory, &transcript),
            },
        )
        .await?;

        match recv_wire_message(&mut stream, &mut transport).await? {
            WireMessage::StaffDirectoryResponse { directory_json } => Ok(directory_json),
            WireMessage::Error { code, message } => Err(AppError::Validation(format!(
                "staff directory rejected ({code}): {message}"
            ))),
            other => Err(AppError::Internal(format!(
                "unexpected staff directory response: {}",
                message_kind(&other)
            ))),
        }
    })
    .await
}

/// Confirm the 4-digit SAS shown on the admin after it accepted the request.
/// The request is signed with this device's key, so only the device that
/// joined can redeem the SAS (the admin also checks it accepted the session).
///
/// `identity` is the key used for the join. Callers pass that key instead of
/// reading the keychain again: a second read can fail even when the key is
/// already loaded, which used to abort pairing with "no cluster identity".
pub async fn confirm_sas_on_admin(
    host: &str,
    port: u16,
    identity: &DeviceIdentity,
    session_id: &str,
    digits: &str,
) -> Result<SasConfirmOutcome, AppError> {
    with_timeout("SAS", async {
        let (mut stream, mut transport, transcript) = open_channel(host, port, "SAS").await?;

        send_wire_message(
            &mut stream,
            &mut transport,
            &WireMessage::SasConfirm {
                session_id: session_id.trim().to_string(),
                digits: digits.to_string(),
                pubkey: identity.pubkey_bytes.to_vec(),
                signature: identity.sign_channel(ChannelPurpose::SasConfirm, &transcript),
            },
        )
        .await?;

        let reply = recv_wire_message(&mut stream, &mut transport).await?;
        lan_outcome_from_accept(reply, "SAS")
    })
    .await
}

/// Re-fetch LAN activation token + master device id after SAS (or if SAS
/// never stored them). Reuses the join session already on the master.
pub async fn fetch_replica_auth(
    host: &str,
    port: u16,
    identity: &DeviceIdentity,
    fingerprint: &str,
    session_id: &str,
) -> Result<SasConfirmOutcome, AppError> {
    if identity.fingerprint != fingerprint.trim() {
        return Err(AppError::Validation(
            "replica auth: fingerprint is not this device's identity".into(),
        ));
    }
    with_timeout("replica auth", async {
        let (mut stream, mut transport, transcript) =
            open_channel(host, port, "replica auth").await?;

        send_wire_message(
            &mut stream,
            &mut transport,
            &WireMessage::ReplicaAuthRequest {
                fingerprint: identity.fingerprint.clone(),
                session_id: session_id.trim().to_string(),
                pubkey: identity.pubkey_bytes.to_vec(),
                signature: identity.sign_channel(ChannelPurpose::ReplicaAuth, &transcript),
            },
        )
        .await?;

        let reply = recv_wire_message(&mut stream, &mut transport).await?;
        lan_outcome_from_accept(reply, "replica auth")
    })
    .await
}

================================================================================
FILE: src/net/join_handler.rs
================================================================================
//! Admin-side handler for inbound cluster TCP connections (pairing + cluster status + reset).

use medoc_core::error::AppError;
use sqlx::SqlitePool;
use tokio::io::AsyncWriteExt;
use tokio::net::TcpStream;
use tokio::sync::watch;

use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicUsize, Ordering};
use std::time::Duration;

use crate::cluster::crypto::{
    fingerprint_matches_pubkey, verify_channel_signature, ChannelPurpose,
};
use crate::cluster::services::cluster_reset_service::{
    load_pending_reset, queue_verified_remote_reset,
};
use crate::cluster::services::create_join_request;
use crate::cluster::services::export_staff_directory_json;
use crate::cluster::services::issue_replica_lan_auth;
use crate::cluster::services::record_join_identity;
use crate::cluster::services::store_join_device_pubkey;
use crate::cluster::services::verify_inbound_sas;
use crate::cluster::SeatRole;

use super::channel::{recv_wire_message, send_wire_message};
use super::handshake::run_xx_responder;
use super::transport::NoiseTransport;
use super::wire::{SeatRoleWire, WireMessage};

/// Handle one inbound cluster connection on the admin or member device.
pub async fn handle_cluster_connection(stream: TcpStream, pool: SqlitePool) {
    let (_tx, cancel) = watch::channel(false);
    handle_cluster_connection_until(stream, pool, cancel).await;
}

/// One connection carries exactly one request; nothing legitimate takes this
/// long. Bounds slow / silent peers (each connection owns a thread).
const CLUSTER_CONN_TIMEOUT: Duration = Duration::from_secs(60);
/// Max. concurrent inbound cluster connections (each runs on its own thread
/// with an 8 MiB stack). Further connections are closed immediately.
const MAX_CONCURRENT_CONNECTIONS: usize = 32;
static ACTIVE_CONNECTIONS: AtomicUsize = AtomicUsize::new(0);

/// Decrements the active-connection counter when the handler ends.
struct ConnectionSlot;

impl ConnectionSlot {
    fn acquire() -> Option<Self> {
        let prev = ACTIVE_CONNECTIONS.fetch_add(1, Ordering::AcqRel);
        if prev >= MAX_CONCURRENT_CONNECTIONS {
            ACTIVE_CONNECTIONS.fetch_sub(1, Ordering::AcqRel);
            None
        } else {
            Some(Self)
        }
    }
}

impl Drop for ConnectionSlot {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        ACTIVE_CONNECTIONS.fetch_sub(1, Ordering::AcqRel);
    }
}

/// Same handler, but `cancel` becoming `true` closes the socket immediately.
pub async fn handle_cluster_connection_until(
    mut stream: TcpStream,
    pool: SqlitePool,
    cancel: watch::Receiver<bool>,
) {
    tokio::select! {
        biased;
        _ = connection_cancelled(cancel) => {
            let _ = stream.shutdown().await;
        }
        result = tokio::time::timeout(
            CLUSTER_CONN_TIMEOUT,
            handle_cluster_connection_inner(&mut stream, &pool),
        ) => {
            match result {
                Ok(Ok(())) => {}
                Ok(Err(e)) => tracing::warn!(
                    target: "medoc::cluster",
                    event = "CLUSTER_CONN_HANDLER_ERR",
                    error = %e
                ),
                Err(_) => tracing::warn!(
                    target: "medoc::cluster",
                    event = "CLUSTER_CONN_TIMEOUT"
                ),
            }
            let _ = stream.shutdown().await;
        }
    }
}

async fn connection_cancelled(mut cancel: watch::Receiver<bool>) {
    if *cancel.borrow() {
        return;
    }
    loop {
        if cancel.changed().await.is_err() {
            return;
        }
        if *cancel.borrow() {
            return;
        }
    }
}

/// Backward-compatible alias used by the listener loop.
pub async fn handle_join_connection(stream: TcpStream, pool: SqlitePool) {
    handle_cluster_connection(stream, pool).await;
}

const CLUSTER_CONN_STACK: usize = 8 * 1024 * 1024;

/// Run inbound cluster TCP on a dedicated thread with a larger stack (Noise/snow).
///
/// The app runtime is multi-thread. Drive the handler on that runtime so the
/// pairing insert and the inbox query share one SQLite pool. A private runtime
/// can leave the write invisible and stall later inbox refreshes.
pub fn spawn_cluster_connection_handler(stream: TcpStream, pool: SqlitePool) {
    let (tx, cancel) = watch::channel(false);
    // The sender must outlive the handler. Dropping it closes the watch
    // channel, and `connection_cancelled` treats that as "stop now", which
    // resets the socket before the handshake.
    spawn_cluster_connection(stream, pool, cancel, Some(tx));
}

/// Spawn a handler that closes its socket when `cancel` becomes true.
pub fn spawn_cluster_connection_handler_until(
    stream: TcpStream,
    pool: SqlitePool,
    cancel: watch::Receiver<bool>,
) {
    spawn_cluster_connection(stream, pool, cancel, None);
}

fn spawn_cluster_connection(
    stream: TcpStream,
    pool: SqlitePool,
    cancel: watch::Receiver<bool>,
    keepalive: Option<watch::Sender<bool>>,
) {
    let Some(slot) = ConnectionSlot::acquire() else {
        tracing::warn!(
            target: "medoc::cluster",
            event = "CLUSTER_CONN_LIMIT",
            limit = MAX_CONCURRENT_CONNECTIONS
        );
        drop(stream);
        return;
    };
    let parent = tokio::runtime::Handle::try_current().ok();
    if std::thread::Builder::new()
        .name("cluster-tcp".into())
        .stack_size(CLUSTER_CONN_STACK)
        .spawn(move || {
            let _slot = slot;
            let _keepalive = keepalive;
            drive_cluster_connection(parent, stream, pool, cancel)
        })
        .is_err()
    {
        tracing::warn!(
            target: "medoc::cluster",
            event = "CLUSTER_CONN_THREAD_SPAWN_FAILED"
        );
    }
}

fn drive_cluster_connection(
    parent: Option<tokio::runtime::Handle>,
    stream: TcpStream,
    pool: SqlitePool,
    cancel: watch::Receiver<bool>,
) {
    if let Some(handle) = parent.as_ref() {
        if handle.runtime_flavor() == tokio::runtime::RuntimeFlavor::MultiThread {
            handle.block_on(handle_cluster_connection_until(stream, pool, cancel));
            return;
        }
    }
    let rt = match tokio::runtime::Builder::new_current_thread()
        .enable_all()
        .build()
    {
        Ok(rt) => rt,
        Err(e) => {
            tracing::warn!(
                target: "medoc::cluster",
                event = "CLUSTER_CONN_RUNTIME_ERR",
                error = %e
            );
            return;
        }
    };
    rt.block_on(handle_cluster_connection_until(stream, pool, cancel));
}

async fn handle_cluster_connection_inner(
    stream: &mut TcpStream,
    pool: &SqlitePool,
) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    let (mut transport, transcript) = run_xx_responder(stream).await?;
    let msg = recv_wire_message(stream, &mut transport).await?;

    match msg {
        WireMessage::JoinRequest {
            fingerprint,
            pubkey,
            hostname,
            ip,
            requested_role,
            signature,
            ..
        } => {
            if !verify_channel_signature(&pubkey, ChannelPurpose::Join, &transcript, &signature) {
                return send_error(
                    stream,
                    &mut transport,
                    "JOIN_FAILED",
                    "Device signature missing or invalid. Update MeDoc on this device and try again.",
                )
                .await;
            }
            handle_join_request(
                stream,
                &mut transport,
                pool,
                &transcript,
                fingerprint,
                hostname,
                ip,
                requested_role,
                pubkey,
            )
            .await
        }
        WireMessage::ClusterStatusRequest { cluster_id, .. } => {
            handle_cluster_status_request(stream, &mut transport, pool, &cluster_id).await
        }
        WireMessage::ClusterReset {
            token_json,
            signature_b64,
        } => {
            handle_inbound_cluster_reset(stream, &mut transport, pool, &token_json, &signature_b64)
                .await
        }
        WireMessage::StaffDirectoryRequest {
            fingerprint,
            session_id,
            pubkey,
            signature,
        } => {
            if !proves_device_key(
                &fingerprint,
                &pubkey,
                ChannelPurpose::StaffDirectory,
                &transcript,
                &signature,
            ) {
                return send_error(
                    stream,
                    &mut transport,
                    "STAFF_DENIED",
                    "Device not authorised for staff directory.",
                )
                .await;
            }
            handle_staff_directory_request(stream, &mut transport, pool, &fingerprint, &session_id)
                .await
        }
        WireMessage::ReplicaAuthRequest {
            fingerprint,
            session_id,
            pubkey,
            signature,
        } => {
            if !proves_device_key(
                &fingerprint,
                &pubkey,
                ChannelPurpose::ReplicaAuth,
                &transcript,
                &signature,
            ) {
                return send_error(
                    stream,
                    &mut transport,
                    "STAFF_DENIED",
                    "Device not authorised for replica auth.",
                )
                .await;
            }
            handle_replica_auth_request(stream, &mut transport, pool, &fingerprint, &session_id)
                .await
        }
        WireMessage::SasConfirm {
            session_id,
            digits,
            pubkey,
            signature,
        } => {
            // The SAS may only be redeemed by the device that sent the join
            // request: its key must match the session fingerprint.
            let session_fp = session_fingerprint(pool, &session_id).await?;
            let fp_match = session_fp
                .as_deref()
                .is_some_and(|fp| fingerprint_matches_pubkey(&pubkey, fp));
            let sig_ok = fp_match
                && verify_channel_signature(
                    &pubkey,
                    ChannelPurpose::SasConfirm,
                    &transcript,
                    &signature,
                );
            if !sig_ok {
                tracing::warn!(
                    target: "medoc::cluster",
                    event = "CLUSTER_SAS_KEY_REJECTED",
                    session_id = %session_id.trim(),
                    session_fp = session_fp.as_deref().unwrap_or(""),
                    pubkey_len = pubkey.len(),
                    signature_len = signature.len(),
                    fingerprint_match = fp_match
                );
                let message = if fp_match {
                    "Device signature does not match this connection."
                } else {
                    "This confirmation was signed by a different device key than the join."
                };
                return send_error(stream, &mut transport, "SAS_MISMATCH", message).await;
            }
            handle_sas_confirm(stream, &mut transport, pool, &session_id, &digits).await
        }
        _ => send_error(stream, &mut transport, "INVALID_MSG", "unexpected message").await,
    }
}

async fn send_error(
    stream: &mut TcpStream,
    transport: &mut NoiseTransport,
    code: &str,
    message: &str,
) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    send_wire_message(
        stream,
        transport,
        &WireMessage::Error {
            code: code.into(),
            message: message.into(),
        },
    )
    .await
}

/// The peer owns the key behind `fingerprint` and signed this Noise session.
fn proves_device_key(
    fingerprint: &str,
    pubkey: &[u8],
    purpose: ChannelPurpose,
    transcript: &[u8],
    signature: &[u8],
) -> bool {
    let fp = fingerprint.trim();
    !fp.is_empty()
        && fingerprint_matches_pubkey(pubkey, fp)
        && verify_channel_signature(pubkey, purpose, transcript, signature)
}

async fn session_fingerprint(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    session_id: &str,
) -> Result<Option<String>, AppError> {
    let sid = session_id.trim();
    if sid.is_empty() {
        return Ok(None);
    }
    let fp: Option<Option<String>> =
        sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT fingerprint FROM pairing_request WHERE id = ?1")
            .bind(sid)
            .fetch_optional(pool)
            .await
            .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    Ok(fp
        .flatten()
        .map(|s| s.trim().to_string())
        .filter(|s| !s.is_empty()))
}

async fn handle_join_request(
    stream: &mut TcpStream,
    transport: &mut NoiseTransport,
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    transcript: &[u8],
    fingerprint: String,
    hostname: String,
    advertised_ip: String,
    requested_role: SeatRoleWire,
    pubkey: Vec<u8>,
) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    let role = match requested_role {
        SeatRoleWire::Admin => SeatRole::Admin,
        SeatRoleWire::Member => SeatRole::Member,
    };

    if !crate::pairing::is_pairing_enabled(pool)
        .await
        .unwrap_or(true)
    {
        send_wire_message(
            stream,
            transport,
            &WireMessage::Error {
                code: "NEW_REPLICAS_DISABLED".into(),
                message: "New replica requests are closed. Already paired devices stay connected."
                    .into(),
            },
        )
        .await?;
        return Ok(());
    }
    if !fingerprint_matches_pubkey(&pubkey, fingerprint.trim()) {
        send_wire_message(
            stream,
            transport,
            &WireMessage::Error {
                code: "JOIN_FAILED".into(),
                message: "Fingerprint does not match the device key.".into(),
            },
        )
        .await?;
        return Ok(());
    }
    let peer_ip = stream
        .peer_addr()
        .ok()
        .map(|addr| crate::pairing::canonical_peer_ip(addr.ip()));
    let requester_ip = crate::pairing::choose_requester_ip(peer_ip.as_deref(), &advertised_ip);
    let requester_ip = if requester_ip.is_empty() {
        None
    } else {
        Some(requester_ip)
    };
    let hostname = {
        let trimmed = hostname.trim();
        if trimmed.is_empty() {
            None
        } else {
            Some(trimmed)
        }
    };
    let handle = match create_join_request(
        pool,
        &fingerprint,
        role,
        transcript,
        None,
        hostname,
        requester_ip.as_deref(),
    )
    .await
    {
        Ok(h) => h,
        Err(e) => {
            let _ =
                record_join_identity(pool, &fingerprint, hostname, requester_ip.as_deref()).await;
            tracing::warn!(
                target: "medoc::cluster",
                event = "CLUSTER_JOIN_REJECTED",
                fingerprint = %fingerprint,
                error = %e
            );
            // Generic text for the peer; details stay in the admin log.
            let message = match e {
                AppError::Forbidden => "This device is blocked on the main device.".to_string(),
                AppError::Conflict(m) => m,
                _ => "Join request could not be stored.".to_string(),
            };
            return send_error(stream, transport, "JOIN_FAILED", &message).await;
        }
    };
    if !pubkey.is_empty() {
        if let Err(e) = store_join_device_pubkey(pool, &handle.session_id, &pubkey).await {
            tracing::warn!(
                target: "medoc::cluster",
                event = "CLUSTER_JOIN_PUBKEY_STORE_FAILED",
                error = %e
            );
        }
    }

    send_wire_message(
        stream,
        transport,
        &WireMessage::JoinAccept {
            session_id: handle.session_id,
            activation_token: None,
            master_pubkey: None,
            master_device_id: None,
            master_cert_sha256: None,
            master_http_port: None,
        },
    )
    .await?;

    tracing::info!(
        target: "medoc::cluster",
        event = "CLUSTER_JOIN_ACCEPTED",
        fingerprint = %fingerprint,
    );
    Ok(())
}

async fn handle_sas_confirm(
    stream: &mut TcpStream,
    transport: &mut NoiseTransport,
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    session_id: &str,
    digits: &str,
) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    match verify_inbound_sas(pool, session_id, digits).await {
        Ok(()) => {
            let (activation_token, master_pubkey, master_device_id, master_cert_sha256) =
                load_join_lan_credentials(pool, session_id).await;
            let master_http_port = Some(crate::cluster::services::local_lan_http_port(pool).await);
            send_wire_message(
                stream,
                transport,
                &WireMessage::JoinAccept {
                    session_id: session_id.to_string(),
                    activation_token,
                    master_pubkey,
                    master_device_id,
                    master_cert_sha256,
                    master_http_port,
                },
            )
            .await?;
        }
        Err(e) => {
            tracing::warn!(
                target: "medoc::cluster",
                event = "CLUSTER_SAS_REJECTED",
                session_id = %session_id,
                error = %e
            );
            send_error(stream, transport, "SAS_MISMATCH", &e.to_string()).await?;
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

async fn handle_cluster_status_request(
    stream: &mut TcpStream,
    transport: &mut NoiseTransport,
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    cluster_id: &str,
) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    let pending = load_pending_reset(pool).await?;
    let response = if let Some(p) = pending {
        WireMessage::ClusterStatusResponse {
            reset_pending: true,
            token_json: Some(p.token_json),
            signature_b64: Some(p.signature_b64),
        }
    } else {
        WireMessage::ClusterStatusResponse {
            reset_pending: false,
            token_json: None,
            signature_b64: None,
        }
    };
    let _ = cluster_id;
    send_wire_message(stream, transport, &response).await?;
    Ok(())
}

async fn handle_staff_directory_request(
    stream: &mut TcpStream,
    transport: &mut NoiseTransport,
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    fingerprint: &str,
    session_id: &str,
) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    let allowed = staff_directory_request_allowed(pool, fingerprint, session_id).await?;
    if !allowed {
        tracing::warn!(
            target: "medoc::cluster",
            event = "STAFF_DENIED",
            fingerprint = %fingerprint.trim(),
            session_id = %session_id.trim()
        );
        send_wire_message(
            stream,
            transport,
            &WireMessage::Error {
                code: "STAFF_DENIED".into(),
                message: "Device not authorised for staff directory.".into(),
            },
        )
        .await?;
        return Ok(());
    }

    let directory_json = export_staff_directory_json(pool).await?;
    send_wire_message(
        stream,
        transport,
        &WireMessage::StaffDirectoryResponse { directory_json },
    )
    .await?;
    Ok(())
}

async fn handle_replica_auth_request(
    stream: &mut TcpStream,
    transport: &mut NoiseTransport,
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    fingerprint: &str,
    session_id: &str,
) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    let allowed = staff_directory_request_allowed(pool, fingerprint, session_id).await?;
    if !allowed {
        tracing::warn!(
            target: "medoc::cluster",
            event = "REPLICA_AUTH_DENIED",
            fingerprint = %fingerprint.trim(),
            session_id = %session_id.trim()
        );
        send_wire_message(
            stream,
            transport,
            &WireMessage::Error {
                code: "STAFF_DENIED".into(),
                message: "Device not authorised for replica auth.".into(),
            },
        )
        .await?;
        return Ok(());
    }
    match issue_replica_lan_auth(pool, fingerprint, session_id).await {
        Ok(lan) => {
            send_wire_message(
                stream,
                transport,
                &WireMessage::JoinAccept {
                    session_id: session_id.trim().to_string(),
                    activation_token: lan.activation_token,
                    master_pubkey: lan.master_pubkey,
                    master_device_id: lan.master_device_id,
                    master_cert_sha256: lan.master_cert_sha256,
                    master_http_port: lan.master_http_port,
                },
            )
            .await?;
        }
        Err(e) => {
            tracing::warn!(
                target: "medoc::cluster",
                event = "REPLICA_AUTH_FAILED",
                error = %e
            );
            send_wire_message(
                stream,
                transport,
                &WireMessage::Error {
                    code: "AUTH_FAILED".into(),
                    message: e.to_string(),
                },
            )
            .await?;
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

async fn staff_directory_request_allowed(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    fingerprint: &str,
    session_id: &str,
) -> Result<bool, AppError> {
    // Callers have already verified that the peer owns the key behind `fp`.
    // A session id alone is not a credential: it used to unlock the staff
    // directory for any session row, including requests the admin never
    // accepted or explicitly rejected.
    let fp = fingerprint.trim();
    let sid = session_id.trim();
    if fp.is_empty() {
        return Ok(false);
    }
    if crate::cluster::services::is_fingerprint_blocklisted(pool, fp).await? {
        return Ok(false);
    }
    if !sid.is_empty() {
        let by_session: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar(
            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM pairing_request
             WHERE id = ?1 AND status = 'ACCEPTED'
               AND (TRIM(fingerprint) = ?2 OR TRIM(device_id) = ?2)",
        )
        .bind(sid)
        .bind(fp)
        .fetch_one(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
        if by_session > 0 {
            return Ok(true);
        }
    }
    let device: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar(
        "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM sync_device
         WHERE TRIM(fingerprint) = ?1
           AND device_status = 'ACTIVE'",
    )
    .bind(fp)
    .fetch_one(pool)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    if device > 0 {
        return Ok(true);
    }
    let session: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar(
        "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM pairing_request
         WHERE status = 'ACCEPTED'
           AND (TRIM(fingerprint) = ?1 OR TRIM(device_id) = ?1)",
    )
    .bind(fp)
    .fetch_one(pool)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    Ok(session > 0)
}

async fn load_join_lan_credentials(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    session_id: &str,
) -> (
    Option<String>,
    Option<String>,
    Option<String>,
    Option<String>,
) {
    let token: Option<String> =
        sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT activation_token FROM pairing_request WHERE id = ?1")
            .bind(session_id)
            .fetch_optional(pool)
            .await
            .ok()
            .flatten()
            .filter(|s: &String| !s.trim().is_empty());
    let master_pubkey = crate::master_keys::align_to_license(pool)
        .await
        .ok()
        .map(|sk| crate::master_keys::pubkey_b64(&sk));
    let master_device_id = crate::repo::ensure_local_device(pool, "Main")
        .await
        .ok()
        .filter(|s| !s.trim().is_empty());
    let master_cert_sha256 = crate::tls_pin::load_local_pin(pool).await;
    (token, master_pubkey, master_device_id, master_cert_sha256)
}

async fn handle_inbound_cluster_reset(
    stream: &mut TcpStream,
    transport: &mut NoiseTransport,
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    token_json: &str,
    signature_b64: &str,
) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    if let Err(e) = queue_verified_remote_reset(pool, token_json, signature_b64).await {
        tracing::warn!(
            target: "medoc::cluster",
            event = "CLUSTER_RESET_REJECTED_INBOUND",
            error = %e
        );
        return send_error(
            stream,
            transport,
            "RESET_REJECTED",
            "Cluster reset rejected",
        )
        .await;
    }
    send_wire_message(
        stream,
        transport,
        &WireMessage::Error {
            code: "RESET_QUEUED".into(),
            message: "Cluster reset queued".into(),
        },
    )
    .await?;
    tracing::warn!(
        target: "medoc::cluster",
        event = "CLUSTER_RESET_QUEUED_INBOUND",
    );
    Ok(())
}

================================================================================
FILE: src/net/listener.rs
================================================================================
//! L4 TCP receive port with private-address bind guard.
//!
//! When DATA_* protocol handlers are added to the accept loop, each owner-side
//! connection must call `require_owner_admin` and `verify_peer_connection` before
//! serving cluster data (single chokepoint for license + peer authz).

use std::net::{IpAddr, SocketAddr};

use medoc_core::error::AppError;
use tokio::net::TcpListener;

use super::bind_guard::assert_private_bind;

pub const DEFAULT_CLUSTER_PORT: u16 = 49300;

pub async fn bind_cluster_listener(addr: IpAddr, port: u16) -> Result<TcpListener, AppError> {
    assert_private_bind(addr)?;
    let sock = SocketAddr::new(addr, port);
    TcpListener::bind(sock).await.map_err(|e| {
        if e.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::AddrInUse {
            AppError::Conflict(format!(
                "cluster port {sock} already in use (another MeDoc instance)"
            ))
        } else {
            AppError::Internal(format!("cluster bind {sock}: {e}"))
        }
    })
}

FILE: src/net/member_cluster_watch.rs
================================================================================
//! Member device: poll admin for cluster reset and apply queued resets.

use std::path::Path;
use std::time::Duration;

use medoc_core::error::AppError;
use sqlx::SqlitePool;
use tokio::net::TcpStream;

use crate::cluster::crypto::DeviceIdentity;
use crate::cluster::services::cluster_reset_service::{
    apply_queued_remote_reset_if_any, load_pending_reset, queue_verified_remote_reset,
};
use crate::cluster::services::license_service::cluster_status;

use super::channel::{recv_wire_message, send_wire_message};
use super::discovery::{scan_admins, AdminEndpoint};
use super::handshake::run_xx_initiator;
use super::listener::DEFAULT_CLUSTER_PORT;
use super::wire::WireMessage;

const POLL_INTERVAL: Duration = Duration::from_secs(30);

/// Spawn a background task that polls the admin for cluster reset and applies queued resets.
pub fn spawn_member_cluster_watch(
    pool: SqlitePool,
    app_data_dir: std::path::PathBuf,
    emit_reset: impl Fn() + Send + Sync + 'static,
) {
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        loop {
            tokio::time::sleep(POLL_INTERVAL).await;
            if let Err(e) = member_watch_tick(&pool, &app_data_dir, &emit_reset).await {
                tracing::debug!(
                    target: "medoc::cluster",
                    event = "MEMBER_CLUSTER_WATCH_TICK_ERR",
                    error = %e
                );
            }
        }
    });
}

async fn member_watch_tick(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    app_data_dir: &Path,
    emit_reset: &impl Fn(),
) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    let status = cluster_status(pool).await?;
    if !status.provisioned || status.is_owner {
        return Ok(());
    }

    if apply_queued_remote_reset_if_any(pool, app_data_dir)
        .await?
        .is_some()
    {
        emit_reset();
        return Ok(());
    }

    if load_pending_reset(pool).await?.is_some() {
        return Ok(());
    }

    let cluster_id = status.cluster_id.unwrap_or_default();
    if cluster_id.is_empty() {
        return Ok(());
    }

    let admins = scan_admins(Duration::from_secs(2))?;
    let admin = admins
        .into_iter()
        .find(|a| a.cluster_id.as_deref() == Some(cluster_id.as_str()));
    let Some(admin) = admin else {
        return Ok(());
    };

    if let Ok((token_json, signature_b64)) = poll_admin_for_reset(&admin, &cluster_id).await {
        queue_verified_remote_reset(pool, &token_json, &signature_b64).await?;
        if apply_queued_remote_reset_if_any(pool, app_data_dir)
            .await?
            .is_some()
        {
            emit_reset();
        }
    }

    Ok(())
}

async fn poll_admin_for_reset(
    admin: &AdminEndpoint,
    cluster_id: &str,
) -> Result<(String, String), AppError> {
    let identity = DeviceIdentity::load_or_create()?;
    let addr = format!("{}:{DEFAULT_CLUSTER_PORT}", admin.host);
    let mut stream = tokio::time::timeout(Duration::from_secs(5), TcpStream::connect(&addr))
        .await
        .map_err(|_| AppError::Internal(format!("cluster status connect timeout {addr}")))?
        .map_err(|e| AppError::Internal(format!("cluster status connect {addr}: {e}")))?;

    let (mut transport, _transcript) = run_xx_initiator(&mut stream).await?;
    send_wire_message(
        &mut stream,
        &mut transport,
        &WireMessage::ClusterStatusRequest {
            fingerprint: identity.fingerprint.clone(),
            cluster_id: cluster_id.to_string(),
        },
    )
    .await?;

    match recv_wire_message(&mut stream, &mut transport).await? {
        WireMessage::ClusterStatusResponse {
            reset_pending: true,
            token_json: Some(token_json),
            signature_b64: Some(signature_b64),
        } => Ok((token_json, signature_b64)),
        _ => Err(AppError::Validation("Kein Reset outstanding".into())),
    }
}

//! Owner-side push of signed cluster reset to member devices (best effort).

use std::time::Duration;

use medoc_core::error::AppError;
use tokio::net::TcpStream;

use super::channel::{recv_wire_message, send_wire_message};
use super::handshake::run_xx_initiator;
use super::listener::DEFAULT_CLUSTER_PORT;
use super::wire::WireMessage;

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct MemberResetTarget {
    pub fingerprint: String,
    pub ip: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]
pub struct ResetBroadcastResult {
    pub notified: u32,
    pub unreachable: Vec<String>,
}

/// Push `CLUSTER_RESET` to each member IP over Noise XX (3s timeout per device).
pub async fn broadcast_cluster_reset(
    targets: &[MemberResetTarget],
    token_json: &str,
    signature_b64: &str,
) -> ResetBroadcastResult {
    let mut result = ResetBroadcastResult::default();
    for target in targets {
        match push_reset_to_member(&target.ip, token_json, signature_b64).await {
            Ok(()) => {
                result.notified += 1;
                tracing::info!(
                    target: "medoc::cluster",
                    event = "CLUSTER_RESET_PUSH_OK",
                    fingerprint = %target.fingerprint,
                    ip = %target.ip,
                );
            }
            Err(e) => {
                tracing::warn!(
                    target: "medoc::cluster",
                    event = "CLUSTER_RESET_PUSH_FAIL",
                    fingerprint = %target.fingerprint,
                    ip = %target.ip,
                    error = %e,
                );
                result.unreachable.push(target.fingerprint.clone());
            }
        }
    }
    result
}

async fn push_reset_to_member(
    ip: &str,
    token_json: &str,
    signature_b64: &str,
) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    let addr = format!("{ip}:{DEFAULT_CLUSTER_PORT}");
    let connect = tokio::time::timeout(Duration::from_secs(3), TcpStream::connect(&addr)).await;
    let mut stream = connect
        .map_err(|_| AppError::Internal(format!("cluster reset connect timeout {addr}")))?
        .map_err(|e| AppError::Internal(format!("cluster reset connect {addr}: {e}")))?;

    let (mut transport, _transcript) = run_xx_initiator(&mut stream).await?;
    send_wire_message(
        &mut stream,
        &mut transport,
        &WireMessage::ClusterReset {
            token_json: token_json.to_string(),
            signature_b64: signature_b64.to_string(),
        },
    )
    .await?;

    // Optional ack — ignore unexpected responses.
    let _ = tokio::time::timeout(
        Duration::from_secs(2),
        recv_wire_message(&mut stream, &mut transport),
    )
    .await;

    Ok(())
}

================================================================================
FILE: src/net/transport.rs
================================================================================
//! L5/L6 Noise XX (pairing) and KK (reconnect) transport with AEAD framing.

use medoc_core::error::AppError;
use snow::{Builder, HandshakeState, Keypair, TransportState};

pub const NOISE_XX: &str = "Noise_XX_25519_ChaChaPoly_SHA256";
pub const NOISE_KK: &str = "Noise_KK_25519_ChaChaPoly_SHA256";
const HS_BUF: usize = 2048;

pub struct NoiseHandshake {
    state: HandshakeState,
    transcript: Vec<u8>,
}

impl NoiseHandshake {
    pub fn new_xx_initiator(local: &Keypair) -> Result<Self, AppError> {
        let builder = Builder::new(NOISE_XX.parse().map_err(map_noise)?);
        let state = builder
            .local_private_key(&local.private)
            .build_initiator()
            .map_err(map_noise)?;
        Ok(Self {
            state,
            transcript: Vec::new(),
        })
    }

    pub fn new_xx_responder(local: &Keypair) -> Result<Self, AppError> {
        let builder = Builder::new(NOISE_XX.parse().map_err(map_noise)?);
        let state = builder
            .local_private_key(&local.private)
            .build_responder()
            .map_err(map_noise)?;
        Ok(Self {
            state,
            transcript: Vec::new(),
        })
    }

    pub fn new_kk_initiator(local: &Keypair, remote_static: &[u8]) -> Result<Self, AppError> {
        let builder = Builder::new(NOISE_KK.parse().map_err(map_noise)?);
        let state = builder
            .local_private_key(&local.private)
            .remote_public_key(remote_static)
            .build_initiator()
            .map_err(map_noise)?;
        Ok(Self {
            state,
            transcript: Vec::new(),
        })
    }

    pub fn new_kk_responder(local: &Keypair, remote_static: &[u8]) -> Result<Self, AppError> {
        let builder = Builder::new(NOISE_KK.parse().map_err(map_noise)?);
        let state = builder
            .local_private_key(&local.private)
            .remote_public_key(remote_static)
            .build_responder()
            .map_err(map_noise)?;
        Ok(Self {
            state,
            transcript: Vec::new(),
        })
    }

    pub fn write_message(&mut self, payload: &[u8], out: &mut [u8]) -> Result<usize, AppError> {
        let n = self.state.write_message(payload, out).map_err(map_noise)?;
        self.transcript.extend_from_slice(&out[..n]);
        Ok(n)
    }

    pub fn read_message(&mut self, msg: &[u8], payload: &mut [u8]) -> Result<usize, AppError> {
        self.transcript.extend_from_slice(msg);
        self.state.read_message(msg, payload).map_err(map_noise)
    }

    pub fn is_complete(&self) -> bool {
        self.state.is_handshake_finished()
    }

    pub fn into_transport(self) -> Result<NoiseTransport, AppError> {
        let state = self.state.into_transport_mode().map_err(map_noise)?;
        Ok(NoiseTransport { state })
    }

    pub fn transcript(&self) -> &[u8] {
        &self.transcript
    }
}

pub struct NoiseTransport {
    state: TransportState,
}

impl NoiseTransport {
    pub fn encrypt(&mut self, plaintext: &[u8], out: &mut [u8]) -> Result<usize, AppError> {
        self.state.write_message(plaintext, out).map_err(map_noise)
    }

    pub fn decrypt(&mut self, ciphertext: &[u8], out: &mut [u8]) -> Result<usize, AppError> {
        self.state.read_message(ciphertext, out).map_err(map_noise)
    }
}

pub fn generate_keypair() -> Result<Keypair, AppError> {
    Builder::new(NOISE_XX.parse().map_err(map_noise)?)
        .generate_keypair()
        .map_err(map_noise)
}

fn map_noise(e: snow::Error) -> AppError {
    AppError::Internal(format!("noise: {e}"))
}

/// Run a full XX handshake between initiator and responder (in-process).
pub fn complete_xx_handshake(
    initiator_kp: &Keypair,
    responder_kp: &Keypair,
) -> Result<(NoiseTransport, NoiseTransport, Vec<u8>), AppError> {
    let mut buf1 = vec![0u8; HS_BUF];
    let mut buf2 = vec![0u8; HS_BUF];
    let mut payload = vec![0u8; HS_BUF];

    let mut ini = NoiseHandshake::new_xx_initiator(initiator_kp)?;
    let mut res = NoiseHandshake::new_xx_responder(responder_kp)?;

    // -> e
    let n1 = ini.write_message(&[], &mut buf1)?;
    let _ = res.read_message(&buf1[..n1], &mut payload)?;

    // <- e, ee, s, es
    let n2 = res.write_message(&[], &mut buf2)?;
    let _ = ini.read_message(&buf2[..n2], &mut payload)?;

    // -> s, se
    let n3 = ini.write_message(&[], &mut buf1)?;
    let _ = res.read_message(&buf1[..n3], &mut payload)?;

    if !ini.is_complete() || !res.is_complete() {
        return Err(AppError::Internal("Noise XX handshake incomplete".into()));
    }

    let transcript = ini.transcript().to_vec();
    let t1 = ini.into_transport()?;
    let t2 = res.into_transport()?;
    Ok((t1, t2, transcript))
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::cluster::crypto::derive_sas_from_transcript;
    use crate::net::wire::{encode_frame, WireMessage};

    #[test]
    fn xx_handshake_and_encrypted_frame() {
        let kp1 = generate_keypair().expect("kp1");
        let kp2 = generate_keypair().expect("kp2");
        let (mut t1, mut t2, transcript) = complete_xx_handshake(&kp1, &kp2).expect("hs");

        let sas = derive_sas_from_transcript(&transcript);
        assert_eq!(sas.len(), 4);

        let frame = encode_frame(&WireMessage::Discover).expect("encode");
        let mut enc = [0u8; 4096];
        let enc_len = t1.encrypt(&frame, &mut enc).expect("enc");

        let mut dec = [0u8; 4096];
        let dec_len = t2.decrypt(&enc[..enc_len], &mut dec).expect("dec");
        assert_eq!(&frame, &dec[..dec_len]);
    }
}

//! L6/L7 length-prefixed CBOR message codec.

use std::io::{Read, Write};

use ciborium::{from_reader, into_writer};
use medoc_core::error::AppError;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

use crate::cluster::SeatRole;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[serde(tag = "type", rename_all = "SCREAMING_SNAKE_CASE")]
pub enum WireMessage {
    Discover,
    JoinRequest {
        fingerprint: String,
        pubkey: Vec<u8>,
        hostname: String,
        os: String,
        app_version: String,
        ip: String,
        requested_role: SeatRoleWire,
        /// Ed25519 signature over the Noise transcript ([`ChannelPurpose::Join`]).
        #[serde(default)]
        signature: Vec<u8>,
    },
    JoinAccept {
        session_id: String,
        #[serde(default)]
        activation_token: Option<String>,
        #[serde(default)]
        master_pubkey: Option<String>,
        #[serde(default)]
        master_device_id: Option<String>,
        /// SHA-256 of the main device's LAN certificate. Sent after SAS so the
        /// replica can pin HTTPS sync.
        #[serde(default)]
        master_cert_sha256: Option<String>,
        /// HTTPS port of the main device's LAN server (`lan.server.config.v1`
        /// `httpPort`). Absent from older main devices; replicas then use 8787.
        #[serde(default)]
        master_http_port: Option<u16>,
    },
    SasConfirm {
        session_id: String,
        digits: String,
        /// Device public key of the joiner (must match the session fingerprint).
        #[serde(default)]
        pubkey: Vec<u8>,
        /// Signature over the Noise transcript ([`ChannelPurpose::SasConfirm`]).
        #[serde(default)]
        signature: Vec<u8>,
    },
    Provision {
        seat_certificate: String,
        settings_json: String,
        wrapped_secrets: Vec<u8>,
    },
    MemberHello {
        fingerprint: String,
    },
    DataRequest {
        path: String,
    },
    DataPush {
        path: String,
        payload: Vec<u8>,
    },
    Revoke {
        fingerprint: String,
    },
    ClusterReset {
        token_json: String,
        signature_b64: String,
    },
    StaffDirectoryRequest {
        fingerprint: String,
        #[serde(default)]
        session_id: String,
        #[serde(default)]
        pubkey: Vec<u8>,
        #[serde(default)]
        signature: Vec<u8>,
    },
    StaffDirectoryResponse {
        directory_json: String,
    },
    ReplicaAuthRequest {
        fingerprint: String,
        #[serde(default)]
        session_id: String,
        #[serde(default)]
        pubkey: Vec<u8>,
        #[serde(default)]
        signature: Vec<u8>,
    },
    ClusterStatusRequest {
        fingerprint: String,
        cluster_id: String,
    },
    ClusterStatusResponse {
        reset_pending: bool,
        #[serde(default)]
        token_json: Option<String>,
        #[serde(default)]
        signature_b64: Option<String>,
    },
    Error {
        code: String,
        message: String,
    },
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq, Eq)]
#[serde(rename_all = "SCREAMING_SNAKE_CASE")]
pub enum SeatRoleWire {
    Admin,
    Member,
}

impl From<SeatRole> for SeatRoleWire {
    fn from(r: SeatRole) -> Self {
        match r {
            SeatRole::Admin => Self::Admin,
            SeatRole::Member => Self::Member,
        }
    }
}

const MAX_FRAME: usize = 4 * 1024 * 1024;

pub fn encode_frame(msg: &WireMessage) -> Result<Vec<u8>, AppError> {
    let mut body = Vec::new();
    into_writer(msg, &mut body).map_err(|e| AppError::Internal(format!("cbor encode: {e}")))?;
    if body.len() > MAX_FRAME {
        return Err(AppError::Validation("Message too large".into()));
    }
    let len = u32::try_from(body.len())
        .map_err(|_| AppError::Internal("frame length overflow".into()))?;
    let mut out = len.to_be_bytes().to_vec();
    out.extend_from_slice(&body);
    Ok(out)
}

pub fn decode_frame(mut data: &[u8]) -> Result<WireMessage, AppError> {
    if data.len() < 4 {
        return Err(AppError::Validation("Frame too short".into()));
    }
    let len = u32::from_be_bytes([data[0], data[1], data[2], data[3]]) as usize;
    data = &data[4..];
    if len > MAX_FRAME || data.len() < len {
        return Err(AppError::Validation("Invalid frame length".into()));
    }
    from_reader(&data[..len]).map_err(|e| AppError::Validation(format!("cbor decode: {e}")))
}

pub fn read_frame<R: Read>(reader: &mut R) -> Result<WireMessage, AppError> {
    let mut len_buf = [0u8; 4];
    reader
        .read_exact(&mut len_buf)
        .map_err(|e| AppError::Internal(format!("read len: {e}")))?;
    let len = u32::from_be_bytes(len_buf) as usize;
    if len > MAX_FRAME {
        return Err(AppError::Validation("Frame too large".into()));
    }
    let mut body = vec![0u8; len];
    reader
        .read_exact(&mut body)
        .map_err(|e| AppError::Internal(format!("read body: {e}")))?;
    from_reader(body.as_slice()).map_err(|e| AppError::Validation(format!("cbor decode: {e}")))
}

pub fn write_frame<W: Write>(writer: &mut W, msg: &WireMessage) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    let frame = encode_frame(msg)?;
    writer
        .write_all(&frame)
        .map_err(|e| AppError::Internal(format!("write frame: {e}")))?;
    Ok(())
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn roundtrip_join_request() {
        let msg = WireMessage::JoinRequest {
            fingerprint: "FP".into(),
            pubkey: vec![1, 2, 3],
            hostname: "host".into(),
            os: "macos".into(),
            app_version: "0.1".into(),
            ip: "192.168.0.2".into(),
            requested_role: SeatRoleWire::Member,
            signature: vec![7; 64],
        };
        let frame = encode_frame(&msg).expect("encode");
        let decoded = decode_frame(&frame).expect("decode");
        assert_eq!(decoded, msg);
    }

    #[test]
    fn roundtrip_sas_confirm() {
        let msg = WireMessage::SasConfirm {
            session_id: "sess".into(),
            digits: "4829".into(),
            pubkey: vec![1; 32],
            signature: vec![2; 64],
        };
        let frame = encode_frame(&msg).expect("encode");
        let decoded = decode_frame(&frame).expect("decode");
        assert_eq!(decoded, msg);
    }

    #[test]
    fn roundtrip_join_accept_with_lan_credentials() {
        let msg = WireMessage::JoinAccept {
            session_id: "sess".into(),
            activation_token: Some("mt2.token".into()),
            master_pubkey: Some("pk".into()),
            master_device_id: Some("master".into()),
            master_cert_sha256: Some("ab".repeat(32)),
            master_http_port: Some(8787),
        };
        let frame = encode_frame(&msg).expect("encode");
        let decoded = decode_frame(&frame).expect("decode");
        assert_eq!(decoded, msg);
    }

    #[test]
    fn roundtrip_replica_auth_request() {
        let msg = WireMessage::ReplicaAuthRequest {
            fingerprint: "fp".into(),
            session_id: "sess".into(),
            pubkey: vec![1; 32],
            signature: vec![2; 64],
        };
        let frame = encode_frame(&msg).expect("encode");
        let decoded = decode_frame(&frame).expect("decode");
        assert_eq!(decoded, msg);
    }
}

================================================================================
FILE: src/numbering.rs

//! F76/F85: cluster-wide document numbers.
//!
//! On the master (or a standalone install) numbers come from the local
//! `number_sequence` table. A replica asks the master over the pinned LAN
//! channel, so two devices never hand out the same invoice or order number.
//! There is no offline fallback on purpose: a replica-local counter is what
//! produced duplicate numbers before.

use medoc_core::error::AppError;
use medoc_core::infrastructure::database::number_sequence::{self, NumberStyle};
use serde::Deserialize;
use sqlx::SqlitePool;

use crate::deployment::{DeviceRole, SyncDeploymentConfig};

#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct NumberReply {
    number: String,
}

fn is_replica(d: &SyncDeploymentConfig) -> bool {
    d.role == DeviceRole::Replica && !d.master_base_url.trim().is_empty()
}

fn bearer(d: &SyncDeploymentConfig) -> Result<String, AppError> {
    if !d.activation_token.trim().is_empty() {
        return Ok(d.activation_token.trim().to_string());
    }
    if !d.master_access_token.trim().is_empty() {
        return Ok(d.master_access_token.trim().to_string());
    }
    Err(AppError::Validation(
        "Replica not paired — complete the pairing flow".into(),
    ))
}

pub async fn allocate_document_number(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    prefix: &str,
    ymd: &str,
    style: NumberStyle,
) -> Result<String, AppError> {
    let deployment = crate::repo::load_deployment(pool).await?;
    if !is_replica(&deployment) {
        return number_sequence::allocate_styled(pool, prefix, ymd, style).await;
    }
    let client = crate::engine::master_http_client_for_reconcile(pool, &deployment).await?;
    let url = crate::paths::api_url(&deployment.master_base_url, crate::paths::SYNC_NUMBER);
    let resp = client
        .post(&url)
        .bearer_auth(bearer(&deployment)?)
        .json(&serde_json::json!({ "prefix": prefix, "date": ymd, "style": style.as_str() }))
        .send()
        .await
        .map_err(|e| {
            AppError::Validation(format!(
                "Main device not reachable — document numbers are issued by the main device ({e})"
            ))
        })?;
    let status = resp.status();
    if !status.is_success() {
        let body = resp.text().await.unwrap_or_default();
        return Err(AppError::Validation(format!(
            "Main device refused number allocation ({status}): {body}"
        )));
    }
    let reply: NumberReply = resp
        .json()
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::Internal(format!("number reply: {e}")))?;
    if reply.number.trim().is_empty() {
        return Err(AppError::Internal("empty number from main device".into()));
    }
    Ok(reply.number)
}

FILE: src/pairing/pin.rs

//! Four-digit out-of-band confirmation for pairing (device cluster + legacy HTTP).

use hmac::{Hmac, Mac};
use rand::Rng;
use sha2::Sha256;
use subtle::ConstantTimeEq;

use crate::cluster::crypto::sas::SAS_LEN;

type HmacSha256 = Hmac<Sha256>;

pub const CONFIRM_PIN_LEN: usize = SAS_LEN;
pub const CONFIRM_PIN_TTL_SECS: i64 = 600;
pub const CONFIRM_PIN_MAX_ATTEMPTS: i32 = 5;

/// Legacy random PIN for HTTP pairing until Noise cutover.
pub fn generate_confirm_pin_random() -> String {
    let n: u16 = rand::thread_rng().gen_range(0..10_000);
    format!("{n:04}")
}

pub fn normalise_pin_input(raw: &str) -> Option<String> {
    let digits: String = raw.chars().filter(|c| c.is_ascii_digit()).collect();
    if digits.len() != CONFIRM_PIN_LEN {
        return None;
    }
    Some(digits)
}

fn hex_encode(bytes: &[u8]) -> String {
    bytes.iter().map(|b| format!("{b:02x}")).collect()
}

/// HMAC-SHA256 hex digest of `(request_id, pin)` keyed by master Ed25519 seed bytes.
pub fn hash_confirm_pin(request_id: &str, pin: &str, master_signing_seed: &[u8]) -> String {
    let mut mac = HmacSha256::new_from_slice(master_signing_seed).expect("HMAC key length");
    mac.update(request_id.as_bytes());
    mac.update(b"|");
    mac.update(pin.as_bytes());
    hex_encode(&mac.finalize().into_bytes())
}

pub fn verify_confirm_pin(
    request_id: &str,
    pin: &str,
    expected_hex: &str,
    master_signing_seed: &[u8],
) -> bool {
    let computed = hash_confirm_pin(request_id, pin, master_signing_seed);
    computed.as_bytes().ct_eq(expected_hex.as_bytes()).into()
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn pin_normalisation_and_verify() {
        let seed = b"test-seed-32-bytes-long-enough!!";
        let pin = "4829";
        let hash = hash_confirm_pin("req-1", pin, seed);
        assert!(verify_confirm_pin("req-1", pin, &hash, seed));
        assert!(!verify_confirm_pin("req-1", "0000", &hash, seed));
    }
}

FILE: src/pairing/policy.rs

//! Pairing policy helpers (URLs, feature toggle, action sanitisation).

use std::net::IpAddr;

use medoc_core::error::AppError;
use sqlx::SqlitePool;

use super::types::{APP_KV_NETWORK_HIDDEN, APP_KV_PAIRING_ENABLED, DEFAULT_ALLOWED_ACTIONS};

/// IPv4 form of a socket address. Mapped IPv6 (`::ffff:192.168.1.5`) becomes `192.168.1.5`.
/// Unspecified addresses (`0.0.0.0`, `::`) are treated as unknown.
pub fn canonical_peer_ip(ip: IpAddr) -> String {
    let ip = match ip {
        IpAddr::V6(v6) => v6
            .to_ipv4_mapped()
            .map(IpAddr::V4)
            .unwrap_or(IpAddr::V6(v6)),
        other => other,
    };
    if ip.is_unspecified() {
        String::new()
    } else {
        ip.to_string()
    }
}

/// Normalize an IP string already stored or received from a client.
pub fn canonical_ip_str(raw: &str) -> String {
    let trimmed = raw.trim();
    if trimmed.is_empty() {
        return String::new();
    }
    match trimmed.parse::<IpAddr>() {
        Ok(ip) => canonical_peer_ip(ip),
        Err(_) => trimmed.to_string(),
    }
}

/// Addresses that are not the replica's LAN address.
fn unusable_requester_ip(ip: &str) -> bool {
    ip == "::1" || ip.starts_with("127.") || ip.starts_with("fe80:") || ip.starts_with("169.254.")
}

/// Prefer the address the master saw on the socket. If that is loopback or link-local,
/// use the LAN address the replica advertised in the join.
pub fn choose_requester_ip(peer: Option<&str>, advertised: &str) -> String {
    let peer = peer.map(canonical_ip_str).filter(|ip| !ip.is_empty());
    let advertised = {
        let ip = canonical_ip_str(advertised);
        if ip.is_empty() {
            None
        } else {
            Some(ip)
        }
    };
    match (peer, advertised) {
        (Some(peer), Some(advertised)) if unusable_requester_ip(&peer) => advertised,
        (Some(peer), _) => peer,
        (None, Some(advertised)) => advertised,
        (None, None) => String::new(),
    }
}

/// Build HTTPS base URL for a replica from its LAN IP (mesh + peer discovery).
pub fn peer_base_url_from_ip(ip: &str, http_port: u16) -> Option<String> {
    let ip = ip.trim();
    if ip.is_empty() {
        return None;
    }
    Some(format!("https://{ip}:{http_port}"))
}

/// Prefer `sync_device.peer_base_url` (updated on sync push); fall back to request IP.
pub async fn peer_advertised_url(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    device_id: &str,
    requester_ip: &str,
    http_port: u16,
) -> Result<Option<String>, AppError> {
    let stored: Option<String> =
        sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT peer_base_url FROM sync_device WHERE device_id = ?1")
            .bind(device_id)
            .fetch_optional(pool)
            .await
            .map_err(AppError::Database)?
            .flatten()
            .filter(|s: &String| !s.trim().is_empty());
    Ok(stored.or_else(|| peer_base_url_from_ip(requester_ip, http_port)))
}

/// Hide network — defaults to visible when unset.
pub async fn is_network_hidden(pool: &SqlitePool) -> Result<bool, AppError> {
    let raw = medoc_core::infrastructure::database::repos::admin::app_kv::get(
        pool,
        APP_KV_NETWORK_HIDDEN,
    )
    .await?;
    Ok(matches!(raw.as_deref(), Some("1") | Some("true")))
}

/// Whether a newly accepted cluster TCP socket may proceed.
///
/// Hidden network refuses every connection. Disallowing new replica requests
/// refuses unknown peers and still admits addresses that already belong to a
/// paired device.
pub fn admit_inbound_cluster(
    network_hidden: bool,
    pairing_enabled: bool,
    known_peer: bool,
) -> bool {
    if network_hidden {
        return false;
    }
    pairing_enabled || known_peer
}

/// Master-side toggle — defaults to enabled when unset.
pub async fn is_pairing_enabled(pool: &SqlitePool) -> Result<bool, AppError> {
    let raw = medoc_core::infrastructure::database::repos::admin::app_kv::get(
        pool,
        APP_KV_PAIRING_ENABLED,
    )
    .await?;
    Ok(!matches!(raw.as_deref(), Some("0") | Some("false")))
}

/// Authorised actions for a slave device (master-side check).
pub async fn slave_actions(pool: &SqlitePool, device_id: &str) -> Result<Vec<String>, AppError> {
    crate::schema::ensure_sync_tables(pool).await?;
    let rows: Vec<(String,)> = sqlx::query_as(
        "SELECT action FROM slave_permission WHERE device_id = ?1 ORDER BY action ASC",
    )
    .bind(device_id)
    .fetch_all(pool)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    Ok(rows.into_iter().map(|(a,)| a).collect())
}

pub(crate) fn sanitise_actions(input: &[String]) -> Vec<String> {
    if input.is_empty() {
        return DEFAULT_ALLOWED_ACTIONS
            .iter()
            .map(|s| (*s).to_string())
            .collect();
    }
    let mut out: Vec<String> = input
        .iter()
        .map(|s| s.trim().to_string())
        .filter(|s| !s.is_empty())
        .collect();
    out.sort();
    out.dedup();
    out
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use std::net::IpAddr;

    use super::{admit_inbound_cluster, canonical_ip_str, canonical_peer_ip};

    #[test]
    fn hidden_network_refuses_every_connection() {
        assert!(!admit_inbound_cluster(true, true, true));
        assert!(!admit_inbound_cluster(true, false, false));
    }

    #[test]
    fn new_replicas_disabled_keeps_known_peers() {
        assert!(!admit_inbound_cluster(false, false, false));
        assert!(admit_inbound_cluster(false, false, true));
        assert!(admit_inbound_cluster(false, true, false));
    }

    #[test]
    fn mapped_ipv6_displays_as_ipv4() {
        let ip: IpAddr = "::ffff:192.168.1.20".parse().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(canonical_peer_ip(ip), "192.168.1.20");
        assert_eq!(canonical_ip_str("  ::ffff:10.0.0.8 "), "10.0.0.8");
        assert_eq!(canonical_ip_str("0.0.0.0"), "");
        assert_eq!(canonical_ip_str("::"), "");
        assert_eq!(canonical_ip_str("192.168.0.4"), "192.168.0.4");
    }

    #[test]
    fn requester_ip_prefers_advertised_lan_over_loopback() {
        assert_eq!(
            super::choose_requester_ip(Some("127.0.0.1"), "192.168.1.42"),
            "192.168.1.42"
        );
        assert_eq!(
            super::choose_requester_ip(Some("10.0.0.8"), "192.168.1.42"),
            "10.0.0.8"
        );
        assert_eq!(
            super::choose_requester_ip(None, "::ffff:10.1.1.5"),
            "10.1.1.5"
        );
    }
}

FILE: src/pairing/store/finalize.rs
