//! Post-PIN acceptance — mint token, permissions, sync registry.

use chrono::Utc;
use ed25519_dalek::SigningKey;
use medoc_core::error::AppError;
use sqlx::SqlitePool;
use uuid::Uuid;

use super::super::policy::{peer_base_url_from_ip, sanitise_actions};
use super::super::token::mint_activation_token;
use super::super::types::{ActivationTokenPayload, PairingRequest};
use super::load_by_id;

pub(super) async fn finalize_accept(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    master_device_id: &str,
    request_id: &str,
    request: &PairingRequest,
    replica_http_port: u16,
    signing_key: &SigningKey,
) -> Result<PairingRequest, AppError> {
    let now = Utc::now().to_rfc3339();
    let actions = sanitise_actions(&request.allowed_actions);
    let payload = ActivationTokenPayload {
        version: 2,
        device_id: request.device_id.clone(),
        slave_label: request.slave_label.clone(),
        master_device_id: master_device_id.to_string(),
        allowed_actions: actions.clone(),
        issued_at: now.clone(),
        nonce: Uuid::new_v4().to_string(),
    };
    let token = mint_activation_token(signing_key, &payload)?;

    sqlx::query(
        "UPDATE pairing_request SET status = 'ACCEPTED',
            activation_token = ?1,
            confirm_pin_hash = NULL,
            confirm_pin_expires_at = NULL,
            confirm_pin_attempts = 0
         WHERE id = ?2",
    )
    .bind(&token)
    .bind(request_id)
    .execute(pool)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;

    sqlx::query("DELETE FROM slave_permission WHERE device_id = ?1")
        .bind(&request.device_id)
        .execute(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    for action in &actions {
        sqlx::query(
            "INSERT INTO slave_permission (device_id, action, granted_at, granted_by)
             VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4)",
        )
        .bind(&request.device_id)
        .bind(action)
        .bind(&now)
        .bind(request.decided_by.as_deref().unwrap_or("master"))
        .execute(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    }

    let peer_base_url = peer_base_url_from_ip(&request.requester_ip, replica_http_port);
    sqlx::query(
        "INSERT INTO sync_device (device_id, display_name, role, is_local, peer_base_url, last_seen_at,
            fingerprint, device_status, seat_role)
         VALUES (?1, ?2, 'REPLICA', 0, ?3, ?4, ?5, 'ACTIVE', 'MEMBER')
         ON CONFLICT(device_id) DO UPDATE SET
             display_name = excluded.display_name,
             role = 'REPLICA',
             is_local = 0,
             peer_base_url = COALESCE(excluded.peer_base_url, sync_device.peer_base_url),
             last_seen_at = excluded.last_seen_at,
             fingerprint = COALESCE(excluded.fingerprint, sync_device.fingerprint),
             device_status = 'ACTIVE',
             seat_role = COALESCE(sync_device.seat_role, 'MEMBER')",
    )
    .bind(&request.device_id)
    .bind(&request.slave_label)
    .bind(&peer_base_url)
    .bind(&now)
    .bind(&request.device_id)
    .execute(pool)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    sqlx::query("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO sync_vector (device_id, seq) VALUES (?1, 0)")
        .bind(&request.device_id)
        .execute(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(AppError::Database)?;

    load_by_id(pool, request_id)
        .await?
        .ok_or_else(|| AppError::Internal("pairing_request disappeared after confirm".into()))
}

FILE: src/pairing/store/mod.rs

//! SQLite persistence for pairing requests and slave permissions.

mod finalize;
mod row;

use chrono::Utc;
use medoc_core::error::AppError;
use sqlx::SqlitePool;
use uuid::Uuid;

use crate::master_keys;
use crate::schema::ensure_sync_tables;

use super::pin::{
    generate_confirm_pin_random, hash_confirm_pin, normalise_pin_input, verify_confirm_pin,
    CONFIRM_PIN_MAX_ATTEMPTS, CONFIRM_PIN_TTL_SECS,
};
use super::policy::{is_pairing_enabled, sanitise_actions};
use super::types::{PairingDecideResult, PairingDecision, PairingRequest, PairingRequestSubmit};

use row::{RawRow, PAIRING_SELECT};

pub async fn submit_request(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    submit: PairingRequestSubmit,
) -> Result<PairingRequest, AppError> {
    ensure_sync_tables(pool).await?;
    if !is_pairing_enabled(pool).await? {
        return Err(AppError::Forbidden);
    }
    if submit.device_id.trim().is_empty() {
        return Err(AppError::validation_code("error.pairing.device_id_empty"));
    }
    if submit.slave_pubkey.trim().is_empty() {
        return Err(AppError::validation_code(
            "error.pairing.slave_pubkey_empty",
        ));
    }

    let existing: Option<(String, String, Option<String>)> =
        sqlx::query_as("SELECT id, status, slave_pubkey FROM pairing_request WHERE device_id = ?1")
            .bind(&submit.device_id)
            .fetch_optional(pool)
            .await
            .map_err(AppError::Database)?;

    let now = Utc::now().to_rfc3339();
    let slave_label = submit.slave_label.trim().to_string();
    let requester_ip = super::policy::canonical_ip_str(&submit.requester_ip);

    if let Some((existing_id, status, existing_key)) = existing {
        if status == "ACCEPTED" {
            return load_by_id(pool, &existing_id).await?.ok_or_else(|| {
                AppError::Internal("pairing_request disappeared during read".into())
            });
        }
        // `device_id` is not secret. Overwriting the key of an open request
        // let any LAN host swap in its own key just before the admin accepts
        // (and inherit the token). A pending request can only be refreshed
        // with the same key; other keys must wait until it is decided.
        let same_key = existing_key.as_deref().map(str::trim) == Some(submit.slave_pubkey.trim());
        if status == "PENDING" && !same_key {
            return Err(AppError::Conflict(
                "A pairing request for this device is already pending".into(),
            ));
        }
        sqlx::query(
            "UPDATE pairing_request SET
                slave_pubkey = ?1,
                slave_label = ?2,
                requester_ip = ?3,
                transport = ?4,
                status = 'PENDING',
                allowed_actions_json = '[]',
                activation_token = NULL,
                confirm_pin_hash = NULL,
                confirm_pin_expires_at = NULL,
                confirm_pin_attempts = 0,
                requested_at = ?5,
                decided_at = NULL,
                decided_by = NULL
             WHERE id = ?6",
        )
        .bind(&submit.slave_pubkey)
        .bind(&slave_label)
        .bind(&requester_ip)
        .bind(&submit.transport)
        .bind(&now)
        .bind(&existing_id)
        .execute(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
        return load_by_id(pool, &existing_id)
            .await?
            .ok_or_else(|| AppError::Internal("pairing_request disappeared after update".into()));
    }

    let id = Uuid::new_v4().to_string();
    sqlx::query(
        "INSERT INTO pairing_request (
            id, device_id, slave_pubkey, slave_label, requester_ip, transport,
            status, allowed_actions_json, requested_at
        ) VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4, ?5, ?6, 'PENDING', '[]', ?7)",
    )
    .bind(&id)
    .bind(&submit.device_id)
    .bind(&submit.slave_pubkey)
    .bind(&slave_label)
    .bind(&requester_ip)
    .bind(&submit.transport)
    .bind(&now)
    .execute(pool)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;

    load_by_id(pool, &id).await?.ok_or_else(|| {
        AppError::Internal("pairing_request lookup after insert returned None".into())
    })
}

pub async fn list_pending(pool: &SqlitePool) -> Result<Vec<PairingRequest>, AppError> {
    ensure_sync_tables(pool).await?;
    list_by_status(pool, "PENDING").await
}

pub async fn list_all(pool: &SqlitePool) -> Result<Vec<PairingRequest>, AppError> {
    ensure_sync_tables(pool).await?;
    let sql = format!("{PAIRING_SELECT} FROM pairing_request ORDER BY requested_at DESC");
    let rows: Vec<RawRow> = sqlx::query_as(&sql)
        .fetch_all(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    Ok(rows.into_iter().map(Into::into).collect())
}

async fn list_by_status(pool: &SqlitePool, status: &str) -> Result<Vec<PairingRequest>, AppError> {
    let sql = format!(
        "{PAIRING_SELECT} FROM pairing_request WHERE status = ?1 ORDER BY requested_at ASC"
    );
    let rows: Vec<RawRow> = sqlx::query_as(&sql)
        .bind(status)
        .fetch_all(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    Ok(rows.into_iter().map(Into::into).collect())
}

pub async fn load_by_id(pool: &SqlitePool, id: &str) -> Result<Option<PairingRequest>, AppError> {
    ensure_sync_tables(pool).await?;
    let sql = format!("{PAIRING_SELECT} FROM pairing_request WHERE id = ?1");
    let row: Option<RawRow> = sqlx::query_as(&sql)
        .bind(id)
        .fetch_optional(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    Ok(row.map(Into::into))
}

pub async fn load_by_device(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    device_id: &str,
) -> Result<Option<PairingRequest>, AppError> {
    ensure_sync_tables(pool).await?;
    let sql = format!("{PAIRING_SELECT} FROM pairing_request WHERE device_id = ?1");
    let row: Option<RawRow> = sqlx::query_as(&sql)
        .bind(device_id)
        .fetch_optional(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    Ok(row.map(Into::into))
}

/// Master decides on a pending request. On accept, generates a 4-digit PIN shown
/// on the master; the replica must confirm it before the activation token is minted.
pub async fn decide(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    master_device_id: &str,
    request_id: &str,
    decision: PairingDecision,
    replica_http_port: u16,
) -> Result<PairingDecideResult, AppError> {
    let _ = (master_device_id, replica_http_port);
    let request = load_by_id(pool, request_id)
        .await?
        .ok_or_else(|| AppError::NotFound("pairing_request".into()))?;
    if request.status != "PENDING" {
        return Err(AppError::Conflict(format!(
            "pairing_request already decided (status={})",
            request.status
        )));
    }
    if request.awaiting_pin {
        return Err(AppError::Conflict(
            "pairing_request already awaiting PIN confirmation".into(),
        ));
    }

    let now = Utc::now().to_rfc3339();
    if !decision.accept {
        sqlx::query(
            "UPDATE pairing_request SET status = 'REJECTED', decided_at = ?1, decided_by = ?2,
                confirm_pin_hash = NULL, confirm_pin_expires_at = NULL, confirm_pin_attempts = 0
             WHERE id = ?3",
        )
        .bind(&now)
        .bind(&decision.decided_by)
        .bind(request_id)
        .execute(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
        let row = load_by_id(pool, request_id)
            .await?
            .ok_or_else(|| AppError::Internal("pairing_request disappeared after reject".into()))?;
        return Ok(PairingDecideResult {
            request: row,
            confirm_pin: None,
        });
    }

    let actions = sanitise_actions(&decision.allowed_actions);
    let actions_json = serde_json::to_string(&actions)
        .map_err(|e| AppError::Internal(format!("actions json: {e}")))?;

    let signing_key = master_keys::load_or_create()?;
    // Same 4 digits as cluster `expected_sas` when a Noise transcript is stored.
    // A random PIN here is a different code and the replica cannot activate with it.
    let pin = confirm_pin_matching_handshake(pool, request_id).await?;
    let pin_hash = hash_confirm_pin(request_id, &pin, &signing_key.to_bytes());
    let expires_at = (Utc::now() + chrono::Duration::seconds(CONFIRM_PIN_TTL_SECS)).to_rfc3339();

    sqlx::query(
        "UPDATE pairing_request SET
            allowed_actions_json = ?1,
            confirm_pin_hash = ?2,
            confirm_pin_expires_at = ?3,
            confirm_pin_attempts = 0,
            decided_at = ?4,
            decided_by = ?5
         WHERE id = ?6",
    )
    .bind(&actions_json)
    .bind(&pin_hash)
    .bind(&expires_at)
    .bind(&now)
    .bind(&decision.decided_by)
    .bind(request_id)
    .execute(pool)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;

    let row = load_by_id(pool, request_id)
        .await?
        .ok_or_else(|| AppError::Internal("pairing_request disappeared after accept".into()))?;
    Ok(PairingDecideResult {
        request: row,
        confirm_pin: Some(pin),
    })
}

async fn confirm_pin_matching_handshake(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    request_id: &str,
) -> Result<String, AppError> {
    let row: Option<(Option<String>, Option<String>)> = sqlx::query_as(
        "SELECT handshake_transcript_b64, pairing_state FROM pairing_request WHERE id = ?1",
    )
    .bind(request_id)
    .fetch_optional(pool)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    let Some((b64, pairing_state)) = row else {
        return Ok(generate_confirm_pin_random());
    };
    if let Some(raw) = b64 {
        if let Some(bytes) = crate::cluster::crypto::decode_handshake_transcript(&raw) {
            if !bytes.is_empty() {
                return Ok(crate::cluster::crypto::derive_sas_from_transcript(&bytes));
            }
        }
    }
    if row::is_cluster_sas_state(pairing_state.as_deref()) {
        return Err(AppError::Validation(
            "Cluster handshake missing; send a new join request from the replica.".into(),
        ));
    }
    Ok(generate_confirm_pin_random())
}

/// Replica confirms the 4-digit PIN; master mints activation token on success.
pub async fn confirm_pin(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    master_device_id: &str,
    request_id: &str,
    pin_raw: &str,
    replica_http_port: u16,
) -> Result<PairingRequest, AppError> {
    let pin = normalise_pin_input(pin_raw)
        .ok_or_else(|| AppError::validation_code("error.pairing.pin_format"))?;

    let request = load_by_id(pool, request_id)
        .await?
        .ok_or_else(|| AppError::NotFound("pairing_request".into()))?;
    if request.status != "PENDING" || !request.awaiting_pin {
        return Err(AppError::Conflict(
            "pairing_request is not awaiting PIN confirmation".into(),
        ));
    }

    let pin_hash: Option<String> =
        sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT confirm_pin_hash FROM pairing_request WHERE id = ?1")
            .bind(request_id)
            .fetch_optional(pool)
            .await
            .map_err(AppError::Database)?
            .flatten();

    let pin_hash = pin_hash.ok_or_else(|| {
        AppError::Internal("pairing_request awaiting_pin without confirm_pin_hash".into())
    })?;

    let expires_at: Option<String> =
        sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT confirm_pin_expires_at FROM pairing_request WHERE id = ?1")
            .bind(request_id)
            .fetch_optional(pool)
            .await
            .map_err(AppError::Database)?
            .flatten();

    if let Some(exp) = expires_at {
        if let Ok(exp_dt) = chrono::DateTime::parse_from_rfc3339(&exp) {
            if exp_dt < Utc::now() {
                sqlx::query(
                    "UPDATE pairing_request SET confirm_pin_hash = NULL, confirm_pin_expires_at = NULL
                     WHERE id = ?1",
                )
                .bind(request_id)
                .execute(pool)
                .await
                .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
                return Err(AppError::validation_code("error.pairing.code_expired"));
            }
        }
    }

    // Consume one attempt atomically *before* checking the PIN. A separate
    // read-then-increment let parallel requests all see `attempts = 0` and
    // try every 4-digit PIN within the limit window.
    let consumed = sqlx::query(
        "UPDATE pairing_request SET confirm_pin_attempts = confirm_pin_attempts + 1
         WHERE id = ?1 AND confirm_pin_attempts < ?2",
    )
    .bind(request_id)
    .bind(CONFIRM_PIN_MAX_ATTEMPTS)
    .execute(pool)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?
    .rows_affected();
    if consumed == 0 {
        return Err(AppError::Validation(
            "Too many PIN attempts — submit a new pairing request".into(),
        ));
    }

    let signing_key = master_keys::load_or_create()?;
    if !verify_confirm_pin(request_id, &pin, &pin_hash, &signing_key.to_bytes()) {
        return Err(AppError::validation_code("error.pairing.code_wrong"));
    }

    finalize::finalize_accept(
        pool,
        master_device_id,
        request_id,
        &request,
        replica_http_port,
        &signing_key,
    )
    .await
}

/// Revoke a previously-accepted pairing.
///
/// `device_id` may be the replica device id, its fingerprint, or the
/// pairing-request id. The accepted row, its permissions, and the live
/// `sync_device` seat are all cleared. A miss is an error — a silent
/// no-op left the device paired.
pub async fn revoke(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    master_device_id: &str,
    device_id: &str,
    decided_by: &str,
) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    let _ = master_device_id;
    ensure_sync_tables(pool).await?;
    let key = device_id.trim();
    if key.is_empty() {
        return Err(AppError::validation_code("error.pairing.device_id_empty"));
    }
    let now = Utc::now().to_rfc3339();
    let updated = sqlx::query(
        "UPDATE pairing_request SET status = 'REVOKED', activation_token = NULL,
            decided_at = ?1, decided_by = ?2
         WHERE status = 'ACCEPTED'
           AND (
                id = ?3
                OR TRIM(device_id) = ?3
                OR TRIM(IFNULL(fingerprint, '')) = ?3
           )",
    )
    .bind(&now)
    .bind(decided_by)
    .bind(key)
    .execute(pool)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?
    .rows_affected();
    if updated == 0 {
        return Err(AppError::validation_code("error.pairing.not_accepted"));
    }
    sqlx::query(
        "DELETE FROM slave_permission
         WHERE device_id = ?1
            OR device_id IN (
                SELECT device_id FROM pairing_request
                 WHERE id = ?1 OR TRIM(device_id) = ?1 OR TRIM(IFNULL(fingerprint, '')) = ?1
            )",
    )
    .bind(key)
    .execute(pool)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    // The seat stays usable while device_status is ACTIVE, even after the
    // pairing row is revoked (staff directory and join both trust it).
    sqlx::query(
        "UPDATE sync_device SET device_status = 'REVOKED'
         WHERE COALESCE(is_local, 0) = 0
           AND (
                device_id = ?1
                OR TRIM(IFNULL(fingerprint, '')) = ?1
                OR device_id IN (
                    SELECT device_id FROM pairing_request
                     WHERE id = ?1 OR TRIM(IFNULL(fingerprint, '')) = ?1
                )
                OR TRIM(IFNULL(fingerprint, '')) IN (
                    SELECT device_id FROM pairing_request
                     WHERE id = ?1 OR TRIM(IFNULL(fingerprint, '')) = ?1
                )
           )",
    )
    .bind(key)
    .execute(pool)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    Ok(())
}

/// Revoke, block, and reclaim drop the live token and do not leave an ACCEPTED row to remint.
pub async fn clear_device_activation(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    device_or_fingerprint: &str,
) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    ensure_sync_tables(pool).await?;
    let now = Utc::now().to_rfc3339();
    sqlx::query(
        "UPDATE pairing_request
         SET status = 'REVOKED', activation_token = NULL, decided_at = ?2
         WHERE status = 'ACCEPTED'
           AND (device_id = ?1 OR IFNULL(fingerprint, '') = ?1)",
    )
    .bind(device_or_fingerprint)
    .bind(&now)
    .execute(pool)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    sqlx::query(
        "DELETE FROM slave_permission
         WHERE device_id = ?1
            OR device_id IN (
                SELECT device_id FROM pairing_request WHERE IFNULL(fingerprint, '') = ?1
            )",
    )
    .bind(device_or_fingerprint)
    .execute(pool)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    Ok(())
}

================================================================================
FILE: src/pairing/store/row.rs
================================================================================
//! SQL row mapping for `pairing_request`.

use super::super::types::PairingRequest;

pub(crate) const PAIRING_SELECT: &str = "SELECT id, device_id,
            COALESCE(slave_pubkey, '') AS slave_pubkey,
            COALESCE(slave_label, '') AS slave_label,
            COALESCE(
                NULLIF(TRIM(requester_ip), ''),
                (SELECT NULLIF(TRIM(last_ip), '') FROM sync_device
                  WHERE sync_device.fingerprint = pairing_request.device_id
                     OR sync_device.device_id = pairing_request.device_id
                     OR sync_device.fingerprint = pairing_request.fingerprint
                  LIMIT 1),
                ''
            ) AS requester_ip,
            COALESCE(status, 'PENDING') AS status,
            COALESCE(allowed_actions_json, '[]') AS allowed_actions_json,
            activation_token,
            COALESCE(requested_at, '') AS requested_at,
            decided_at, decided_by,
            confirm_pin_hash, confirm_pin_expires_at,
            COALESCE(confirm_pin_attempts, 0) AS confirm_pin_attempts,
            COALESCE(transport, 'lan') AS transport,
            pairing_state, handshake_transcript_b64,
            COALESCE(
                NULLIF(TRIM(hostname), ''),
                (SELECT NULLIF(TRIM(hostname), '') FROM sync_device
                  WHERE sync_device.fingerprint = pairing_request.device_id
                     OR sync_device.device_id = pairing_request.device_id
                     OR sync_device.fingerprint = pairing_request.fingerprint
                  LIMIT 1)
            ) AS hostname";

#[derive(sqlx::FromRow)]
#[allow(dead_code)] // pin expiry/attempts loaded for schema parity; confirm path reads via scalar queries
pub(crate) struct RawRow {
    pub id: String,
    pub device_id: String,
    pub slave_pubkey: String,
    pub slave_label: String,
    pub requester_ip: String,
    pub status: String,
    pub allowed_actions_json: String,
    pub activation_token: Option<String>,
    pub requested_at: String,
    pub decided_at: Option<String>,
    pub decided_by: Option<String>,
    pub confirm_pin_hash: Option<String>,
    pub confirm_pin_expires_at: Option<String>,
    pub confirm_pin_attempts: i32,
    pub transport: String,
    pub pairing_state: Option<String>,
    pub handshake_transcript_b64: Option<String>,
    pub hostname: Option<String>,
}

impl From<RawRow> for PairingRequest {
    fn from(r: RawRow) -> Self {
        let actions: Vec<String> =
            serde_json::from_str(&r.allowed_actions_json).unwrap_or_default();
        let cluster_sas = is_cluster_sas_state(r.pairing_state.as_deref());
        let awaiting_pin =
            r.status == "PENDING" && r.confirm_pin_hash.is_some() && r.activation_token.is_none();
        let coupling_code = if r.status == "PENDING" {
            coupling_code_from_transcript_b64(r.handshake_transcript_b64.as_deref())
        } else {
            None
        };
        let slave_label = display_device_label(&r.slave_label, r.hostname.as_deref(), &r.device_id);
        let requester_ip = super::super::policy::canonical_ip_str(&r.requester_ip);
        Self {
            id: r.id,
            device_id: r.device_id,
            slave_pubkey: r.slave_pubkey,
            slave_label,
            requester_ip,
            status: r.status,
            allowed_actions: actions,
            activation_token: r.activation_token,
            requested_at: r.requested_at,
            decided_at: r.decided_at,
            decided_by: r.decided_by,
            awaiting_pin,
            transport: r.transport,
            cluster_sas,
            coupling_code,
        }
    }
}

fn coupling_code_from_transcript_b64(b64: Option<&str>) -> Option<String> {
    use crate::cluster::crypto::{decode_handshake_transcript, derive_sas_from_transcript};
    let bytes = decode_handshake_transcript(b64?)?;
    if bytes.is_empty() {
        return None;
    }
    Some(derive_sas_from_transcript(&bytes))
}

pub(crate) fn is_cluster_sas_state(pairing_state: Option<&str>) -> bool {
    matches!(
        pairing_state.map(str::trim),
        Some("JOIN_REQUESTED" | "AWAITING_SAS" | "SAS_CONFIRMED" | "PROVISIONED" | "ABORTED")
    )
}

/// Prefer the operator label, then the computer name sent with the join.
/// A label that is only the device fingerprint is not a name.
pub(crate) fn display_device_label(
    slave_label: &str,
    hostname: Option<&str>,
    device_id: &str,
) -> String {
    let device_id = device_id.trim();
    let label = slave_label.trim();
    if !label.is_empty() && label != device_id {
        return label.to_string();
    }
    let host = hostname.unwrap_or("").trim();
    if !host.is_empty() && host != device_id {
        return host.to_string();
    }
    String::new()
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::display_device_label;

    #[test]
    fn label_uses_hostname_when_slave_label_is_blank() {
        assert_eq!(
            display_device_label("", Some("Reception-iMac"), "FINGERPRINT"),
            "Reception-iMac"
        );
        assert_eq!(
            display_device_label("   ", Some(" Reception-iMac "), "FINGERPRINT"),
            "Reception-iMac"
        );
        assert_eq!(
            display_device_label("Front desk", Some("other"), "FINGERPRINT"),
            "Front desk"
        );
        assert_eq!(display_device_label("", None, "FINGERPRINT"), "");
        assert_eq!(
            display_device_label("FINGERPRINT", Some("Clinic-Mac"), "FINGERPRINT"),
            "Clinic-Mac"
        );
        assert_eq!(display_device_label("FINGERPRINT", None, "FINGERPRINT"), "");
    }
}

use base64::{
    engine::general_purpose::{STANDARD, STANDARD_NO_PAD},
    Engine,
};
use ed25519_dalek::SigningKey;
use medoc_core::error::AppError;
use medoc_core::infrastructure::database::connection::{run_migrations, test_memory_pool};
use rand::rngs::OsRng;
use sqlx::SqlitePool;

use super::policy::slave_actions;
use super::store::{confirm_pin, decide, load_by_device, revoke, submit_request};
use super::token::verify_activation_token;
use super::types::{
    PairingDecision, PairingRequest, PairingRequestSubmit, ACTIVATION_TOKEN_PREFIX,
    DEFAULT_REPLICA_HTTP_PORT,
};
use crate::master_keys;

async fn fresh_pool() -> SqlitePool {
    std::env::set_var(
        "MEDOC_PAIRING_MASTER_SECRET",
        "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef",
    );
    let pool = test_memory_pool().await.expect("pool");
    run_migrations(&pool).await.expect("migrate");
    crate::schema::ensure_sync_tables(&pool)
        .await
        .expect("schema");
    pool
}

async fn accept_with_pin(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    master_id: &str,
    request_id: &str,
    allowed_actions: Vec<String>,
    decided_by: &str,
) -> PairingRequest {
    let decided = decide(
        pool,
        master_id,
        request_id,
        PairingDecision {
            accept: true,
            allowed_actions,
            decided_by: decided_by.into(),
        },
        DEFAULT_REPLICA_HTTP_PORT,
    )
    .await
    .expect("decide ok");
    assert!(decided.confirm_pin.is_some());
    assert!(decided.request.awaiting_pin);
    let pin = decided.confirm_pin.expect("pin");
    confirm_pin(pool, master_id, request_id, &pin, DEFAULT_REPLICA_HTTP_PORT)
        .await
        .expect("confirm ok")
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn submit_then_accept_round_trip_issues_token() {
    let pool = fresh_pool().await;
    let slave_sk = SigningKey::generate(&mut OsRng);
    let slave_pubkey_b64 = STANDARD_NO_PAD.encode(slave_sk.verifying_key().to_bytes());

    let req = submit_request(
        &pool,
        PairingRequestSubmit {
            device_id: "slave-1".into(),
            slave_pubkey: slave_pubkey_b64,
            slave_label: "Empfang iPad".into(),
            requester_ip: "192.168.1.20".into(),
            transport: "lan".into(),
        },
    )
    .await
    .expect("submit ok");
    assert_eq!(req.status, "PENDING");
    assert!(req.activation_token.is_none());

    let decided = accept_with_pin(
        &pool,
        "master-1",
        &req.id,
        vec!["sync.push".into(), "sync.pull".into()],
        "admin@practice",
    )
    .await;
    assert_eq!(decided.status, "ACCEPTED");
    let token = decided.activation_token.expect("token attached");
    assert!(token.starts_with(ACTIVATION_TOKEN_PREFIX));

    let master_sk = master_keys::load_or_create().expect("master key");
    let payload = verify_activation_token(&token, &master_sk.verifying_key()).expect("verify");
    assert_eq!(payload.device_id, "slave-1");
    assert_eq!(payload.master_device_id, "master-1");
    assert_eq!(
        payload.allowed_actions,
        vec!["sync.pull".to_string(), "sync.push".to_string()]
    );

    let actions = slave_actions(&pool, "slave-1").await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        actions,
        vec!["sync.pull".to_string(), "sync.push".to_string()]
    );

    let peer_url: Option<String> =
        sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT peer_base_url FROM sync_device WHERE device_id = ?1")
            .bind("slave-1")
            .fetch_one(&pool)
            .await
            .expect("sync_device row");
    assert_eq!(peer_url.as_deref(), Some("https://192.168.1.20:8787"));
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn wrong_pin_rejected_before_accept() {
    let pool = fresh_pool().await;
    let req = submit_request(
        &pool,
        PairingRequestSubmit {
            device_id: "slave-pin".into(),
            slave_pubkey: "AAAA".into(),
            slave_label: "Test".into(),
            requester_ip: "10.0.0.1".into(),
            transport: "lan".into(),
        },
    )
    .await
    .unwrap();
    let decided = decide(
        &pool,
        "master-1",
        &req.id,
        PairingDecision {
            accept: true,
            allowed_actions: vec!["sync.push".into()],
            decided_by: "admin".into(),
        },
        DEFAULT_REPLICA_HTTP_PORT,
    )
    .await
    .unwrap();
    assert!(decided.confirm_pin.is_some());

    let err = confirm_pin(
        &pool,
        "master-1",
        &req.id,
        "0000",
        DEFAULT_REPLICA_HTTP_PORT,
    )
    .await
    .expect_err("wrong pin");
    assert!(
        matches!(err, AppError::ValidationCode(_)),
        "expected ValidationCode for wrong PIN, got {err:?}"
    );

    let still = load_by_device(&pool, "slave-pin").await.unwrap().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(still.status, "PENDING");
    assert!(still.awaiting_pin);
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn second_submit_replaces_pending_row() {
    let pool = fresh_pool().await;
    let req1 = submit_request(
        &pool,
        PairingRequestSubmit {
            device_id: "slave-2".into(),
            slave_pubkey: "AAAA".into(),
            slave_label: "Lab".into(),
            requester_ip: "10.0.0.5".into(),
            transport: "lan".into(),
        },
    )
    .await
    .unwrap();
    let req2 = submit_request(
        &pool,
        PairingRequestSubmit {
            device_id: "slave-2".into(),
            slave_pubkey: "AAAA".into(),
            slave_label: "Lab (renamed)".into(),
            requester_ip: "10.0.0.5".into(),
            transport: "lan".into(),
        },
    )
    .await
    .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(req1.id, req2.id, "same row reused for same device_id");
    assert_eq!(req2.slave_label, "Lab (renamed)");
    assert_eq!(req2.status, "PENDING");

    // Another key for the same (public) device_id must not take over the
    // open request.
    let hijack = submit_request(
        &pool,
        PairingRequestSubmit {
            device_id: "slave-2".into(),
            slave_pubkey: "BBBB".into(),
            slave_label: "Attacker".into(),
            requester_ip: "10.0.0.66".into(),
            transport: "lan".into(),
        },
    )
    .await;
    assert!(matches!(hijack, Err(AppError::Conflict(_))));
    let still = load_by_device(&pool, "slave-2").await.unwrap().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(still.slave_pubkey, "AAAA");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn reject_keeps_no_token_and_no_permissions() {
    let pool = fresh_pool().await;
    let req = submit_request(
        &pool,
        PairingRequestSubmit {
            device_id: "slave-3".into(),
            slave_pubkey: "CCCC".into(),
            slave_label: "Test".into(),
            requester_ip: "1.2.3.4".into(),
            transport: "lan".into(),
        },
    )
    .await
    .unwrap();
    let decided = decide(
        &pool,
        "master-1",
        &req.id,
        PairingDecision {
            accept: false,
            allowed_actions: vec![],
            decided_by: "admin".into(),
        },
        DEFAULT_REPLICA_HTTP_PORT,
    )
    .await
    .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(decided.request.status, "REJECTED");
    assert!(decided.request.activation_token.is_none());
    assert!(slave_actions(&pool, "slave-3").await.unwrap().is_empty());
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn revoke_clears_permissions_and_marks_revoked() {
    let pool = fresh_pool().await;
    let req = submit_request(
        &pool,
        PairingRequestSubmit {
            device_id: "slave-4".into(),
            slave_pubkey: "DDDD".into(),
            slave_label: "Tablet".into(),
            requester_ip: "1.1.1.1".into(),
            transport: "lan".into(),
        },
    )
    .await
    .unwrap();
    accept_with_pin(&pool, "m", &req.id, vec!["sync.push".into()], "admin").await;
    revoke(&pool, "m", "slave-4", "admin").await.unwrap();
    let after = load_by_device(&pool, "slave-4").await.unwrap().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(after.status, "REVOKED");
    assert!(after.activation_token.is_none());
    assert!(slave_actions(&pool, "slave-4").await.unwrap().is_empty());
    let seat: String =
        sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT device_status FROM sync_device WHERE device_id = 'slave-4'")
            .fetch_one(&pool)
            .await
            .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(seat, "REVOKED");
    assert!(revoke(&pool, "m", "slave-4", "admin").await.is_err());
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn verify_token_rejects_wrong_master_pubkey() {
    let pool = fresh_pool().await;
    let req = submit_request(
        &pool,
        PairingRequestSubmit {
            device_id: "slave-5".into(),
            slave_pubkey: "EEEE".into(),
            slave_label: "X".into(),
            requester_ip: "".into(),
            transport: "lan".into(),
        },
    )
    .await
    .unwrap();
    let decided = accept_with_pin(&pool, "m", &req.id, vec!["sync.push".into()], "a").await;
    let other = SigningKey::generate(&mut OsRng).verifying_key();
    let err = verify_activation_token(decided.activation_token.as_ref().unwrap(), &other)
        .expect_err("must fail for wrong pubkey");
    assert!(matches!(err, AppError::Validation(_)));
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn accept_pin_matches_handshake_transcript_sas() {
    use crate::cluster::crypto::derive_sas_from_transcript;
    use base64::{engine::general_purpose::STANDARD, Engine};

    let pool = fresh_pool().await;
    let transcript = b"noise-xx-handshake-transcript-bytes";
    let expected = derive_sas_from_transcript(transcript);
    let req = submit_request(
        &pool,
        PairingRequestSubmit {
            device_id: "slave-sas".into(),
            slave_pubkey: "AAAA".into(),
            slave_label: "Replica".into(),
            requester_ip: "192.168.1.30".into(),
            transport: "lan".into(),
        },
    )
    .await
    .expect("submit");
    sqlx::query("UPDATE pairing_request SET handshake_transcript_b64 = ?1 WHERE id = ?2")
        .bind(STANDARD.encode(transcript))
        .bind(&req.id)
        .execute(&pool)
        .await
        .expect("store transcript");

    let decided = decide(
        &pool,
        "master-1",
        &req.id,
        PairingDecision {
            accept: true,
            allowed_actions: vec!["sync.push".into()],
            decided_by: "admin".into(),
        },
        DEFAULT_REPLICA_HTTP_PORT,
    )
    .await
    .expect("decide");
    assert_eq!(decided.confirm_pin.as_deref(), Some(expected.as_str()));
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn revoked_device_is_not_reminted() {
    let pool = fresh_pool().await;
    let req = submit_request(
        &pool,
        PairingRequestSubmit {
            device_id: "fp-revoked".into(),
            slave_pubkey: "cHVi".into(),
            slave_label: "Front".into(),
            requester_ip: "10.0.0.9".into(),
            transport: "lan".into(),
        },
    )
    .await
    .expect("submit");
    sqlx::query(
        "UPDATE pairing_request SET status = 'ACCEPTED', activation_token = 'mt2.live' WHERE id = ?1",
    )
    .bind(&req.id)
    .execute(&pool)
    .await
    .unwrap();
    super::clear_device_activation(&pool, "fp-revoked")
        .await
        .expect("clear");
    let token: Option<String> =
        sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT activation_token FROM pairing_request WHERE id = ?1")
            .bind(&req.id)
            .fetch_one(&pool)
            .await
            .unwrap();
    assert!(token.is_none());
    let err = crate::cluster::services::issue_replica_lan_auth(&pool, "fp-revoked", &req.id)
        .await
        .expect_err("revoked");
    assert!(matches!(err, AppError::Forbidden));
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn foreign_fingerprint_does_not_match_the_noise_key() {
    let previous = std::env::var("MEDOC_CLUSTER_DEVICE_SECRET").ok();
    std::env::set_var(
        "MEDOC_CLUSTER_DEVICE_SECRET",
        "1111111111111111111111111111111111111111111111111111111111111111",
    );
    let identity = crate::cluster::crypto::DeviceIdentity::load_or_create().expect("identity");
    match previous {
        Some(value) => std::env::set_var("MEDOC_CLUSTER_DEVICE_SECRET", value),
        None => std::env::remove_var("MEDOC_CLUSTER_DEVICE_SECRET"),
    }
    let pool = fresh_pool().await;
    let req = submit_request(
        &pool,
        PairingRequestSubmit {
            device_id: "desk-1".into(),
            slave_pubkey: STANDARD.encode(identity.pubkey_bytes),
            slave_label: "Front".into(),
            requester_ip: "10.0.0.11".into(),
            transport: "lan".into(),
        },
    )
    .await
    .expect("submit");
    sqlx::query("UPDATE pairing_request SET status = 'ACCEPTED', fingerprint = ?1 WHERE id = ?2")
        .bind(&identity.fingerprint)
        .bind(&req.id)
        .execute(&pool)
        .await
        .unwrap();

    let foreign = crate::cluster::services::issue_replica_lan_auth(&pool, "desk-1", &req.id)
        .await
        .expect_err("foreign fingerprint");
    assert!(matches!(foreign, AppError::Forbidden));
    let token: Option<String> =
        sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT activation_token FROM pairing_request WHERE id = ?1")
            .bind(&req.id)
            .fetch_one(&pool)
            .await
            .unwrap();
    assert!(token.is_none());
}

================================================================================

//! Activation token minting and verification (Ed25519 over canonical JSON).

use base64::{engine::general_purpose::STANDARD_NO_PAD, Engine};
use medoc_core::error::AppError;

use crate::master_keys;

use super::types::{ActivationTokenPayload, ACTIVATION_TOKEN_PREFIX};

/// Build & sign an activation token from a payload. Public so vendor/test
/// utilities can mint tokens without going through the database.
pub fn mint_activation_token(
    signing_key: &ed25519_dalek::SigningKey,
    payload: &ActivationTokenPayload,
) -> Result<String, AppError> {
    let body = serde_json::to_vec(payload)
        .map_err(|e| AppError::Internal(format!("token serialise: {e}")))?;
    let body_b64 = STANDARD_NO_PAD.encode(&body);
    let sig = master_keys::sign(signing_key, body_b64.as_bytes());
    Ok(format!("{ACTIVATION_TOKEN_PREFIX}{body_b64}.{sig}"))
}

/// Parse + verify an activation token presented by a replica.
pub fn verify_activation_token(
    token: &str,
    master_pubkey: &ed25519_dalek::VerifyingKey,
) -> Result<ActivationTokenPayload, AppError> {
    let rest = token.strip_prefix(ACTIVATION_TOKEN_PREFIX).ok_or_else(|| {
        AppError::Validation(format!(
            "Activation token: expected prefix `{ACTIVATION_TOKEN_PREFIX}`"
        ))
    })?;
    let (body_b64, sig_b64) = rest
        .split_once('.')
        .ok_or_else(|| AppError::Validation("Activation token: missing separator".into()))?;
    master_keys::verify(master_pubkey, body_b64.as_bytes(), sig_b64)?;
    let body_bytes = STANDARD_NO_PAD
        .decode(body_b64)
        .map_err(|e| AppError::Validation(format!("Activation token decode: {e}")))?;
    let payload: ActivationTokenPayload = serde_json::from_slice(&body_bytes)
        .map_err(|e| AppError::Validation(format!("Activation token JSON: {e}")))?;
    if payload.version != 2 {
        return Err(AppError::Validation(format!(
            "Activation token version {} (expected 2)",
            payload.version
        )));
    }
    Ok(payload)
}

//! Pairing DTOs and shared constants.

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

pub const ACTIVATION_TOKEN_PREFIX: &str = "mt2.";
pub const APP_KV_PAIRING_ENABLED: &str = "pairing.enabled.v1";
/// When set, this computer does not listen on the practice LAN address.
pub const APP_KV_NETWORK_HIDDEN: &str = "network.hidden.v1";
pub const DEFAULT_REPLICA_HTTP_PORT: u16 = 8787;

/// Canonical default actions granted to a freshly accepted replica.
pub const DEFAULT_ALLOWED_ACTIONS: &[&str] = &[
    "sync.push",
    "sync.pull",
    "sync.status",
    "sync.blob.get",
    "sync.blob.put",
    "sync.reconcile",
    "sync.number",
    "pairing.peers",
];

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct PairingRequest {
    pub id: String,
    pub device_id: String,
    pub slave_pubkey: String,
    pub slave_label: String,
    pub requester_ip: String,
    pub status: String,
    pub allowed_actions: Vec<String>,
    pub activation_token: Option<String>,
    pub requested_at: String,
    pub decided_at: Option<String>,
    pub decided_by: Option<String>,
    /// True when the master approved the request and waits for the 4-digit PIN on the replica.
    #[serde(default)]
    pub awaiting_pin: bool,
    /// How the request was initiated (`lan` | `bluetooth`).
    #[serde(default = "default_transport")]
    pub transport: String,
    /// Cluster Noise-XX join (same 4-digit code as Device cluster SAS).
    #[serde(default)]
    pub cluster_sas: bool,
    /// 4-digit coupling code while the request is pending (transcript SAS). Not stored after accept token mint.
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub coupling_code: Option<String>,
}

fn default_transport() -> String {
    "lan".into()
}

impl PairingRequest {
    /// Drop the live activation token before a response that is not the PIN confirm.
    pub fn redact_public_token(mut self) -> Self {
        self.activation_token = None;
        self
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct PairingDecideResult {
    pub request: PairingRequest,
    /// Shown once on the accepting (master) device; replica must enter it to finish coupling.
    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub confirm_pin: Option<String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct PairingRequestSubmit {
    pub device_id: String,
    pub slave_pubkey: String,
    pub slave_label: String,
    pub requester_ip: String,
    #[serde(default = "default_transport")]
    pub transport: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct PairingDecision {
    pub accept: bool,
    pub allowed_actions: Vec<String>,
    pub decided_by: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct ActivationTokenPayload {
    pub version: u32,
    pub device_id: String,
    pub slave_label: String,
    pub master_device_id: String,
    pub allowed_actions: Vec<String>,
    pub issued_at: String,
    pub nonce: String,
}

//! LAN sync routes. The client and `medoc-lan` both use these.
//! Axum mounts `route` under [`API_PREFIX`].

pub const API_PREFIX: &str = "/api/v1";

pub const SYNC_PUSH: &str = "/sync/push";
pub const SYNC_PULL: &str = "/sync/pull";
/// One round trip: the caller’s pending packets go in, the callee’s pending
/// packets come back. Both the main device and a replica serve and call it.
pub const SYNC_EXCHANGE: &str = "/sync/exchange";
pub const SYNC_STATUS: &str = "/sync/status";
pub const SYNC_DIGEST: &str = "/sync/digest";
pub const SYNC_ROW_HASHES: &str = "/sync/row-hashes";
pub const SYNC_ROWS: &str = "/sync/rows";
pub const SYNC_NUMBER: &str = "/sync/number";
pub const SYNC_BLOB: &str = "/sync/blob";
pub const SYNC_BLOB_PREFIX: &str = "/sync/blob/";
pub const SYNC_BLOB_ROUTE: &str = "/sync/blob/{sha256}";

pub fn api_url(base: &str, route: &str) -> String {
    format!("{}{API_PREFIX}{route}", base.trim_end_matches('/'))
}

pub fn blob_url(base: &str, sha: &str) -> String {
    format!(
        "{}{API_PREFIX}{SYNC_BLOB}/{sha}",
        base.trim_end_matches('/')
    )
}

//! Hourly replica digest reconcile against the main device.

use std::collections::{BTreeMap, HashMap, HashSet};
use std::sync::{Mutex, OnceLock};

use medoc_core::error::AppError;
use medoc_core::infrastructure::database::recorded_write::json_object_sql;
use medoc_core::infrastructure::database::sync_registry::{deny_app_kv_key, synced_tables};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
use sqlx::SqlitePool;

use crate::deployment::DeviceRole;
use crate::merge::force_apply_payload;
use crate::repo::{ensure_local_device, list_pending_outbox, load_deployment};

pub const LAST_RUN_KV: &str = "sync.reconcile.last_run.v1";
pub const LAST_RESULT_KV: &str = "sync.reconcile.last_result.v1";
const REPAIR_KV: &str = "sync.repair.required.v1";
const MAX_ROWS: usize = 500;
const HOUR_SECS: i64 = 3600;

static IN_FLIGHT: OnceLock<Mutex<HashSet<String>>> = OnceLock::new();

fn in_flight() -> &'static Mutex<HashSet<String>> {
    IN_FLIGHT.get_or_init(|| Mutex::new(HashSet::new()))
}

pub fn try_begin_reconcile(device_id: &str) -> bool {
    in_flight()
        .lock()
        .map(|mut set| set.insert(device_id.to_string()))
        .unwrap_or(false)
}

pub fn end_reconcile(device_id: &str) {
    if let Ok(mut set) = in_flight().lock() {
        set.remove(device_id);
    }
}

pub struct ReconcileGuard(String);

impl ReconcileGuard {
    pub fn acquire(device_id: &str) -> Option<Self> {
        if try_begin_reconcile(device_id) {
            Some(Self(device_id.to_string()))
        } else {
            None
        }
    }
}

impl Drop for ReconcileGuard {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        end_reconcile(&self.0);
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct ReconcileReport {
    pub buckets_checked: u32,
    pub rows_fetched: u32,
    pub applied: u32,
    pub queued: u32,
    pub skipped: bool,
    pub error: Option<String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct DigestResponse {
    pub buckets: Vec<String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct RowHash {
    pub table: String,
    pub id: String,
    pub hash: String,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub tombstone: bool,
}

#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct RowHashesRequest {
    pub buckets: Vec<u8>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct RowsRequest {
    pub ids: Vec<RowId>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct RowId {
    pub table: String,
    pub id: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct RowBody {
    pub table: String,
    pub id: String,
    pub op: String,
    pub payload_json: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct RowHashesBody {
    pub rows: Vec<RowHash>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct RowsBody {
    pub rows: Vec<RowBody>,
}

pub fn hash_row_json(json: &str) -> String {
    let parsed: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(json).unwrap_or(serde_json::Value::Null);
    let canonical = serde_json::to_string(&sort_value(parsed)).unwrap_or_else(|_| json.to_string());
    sha256_hex(canonical.as_bytes())
}

fn sort_value(value: serde_json::Value) -> serde_json::Value {
    match value {
        serde_json::Value::Object(map) => {
            let ordered: serde_json::Map<String, serde_json::Value> = map
                .into_iter()
                .map(|(k, v)| (k, sort_value(v)))
                .collect::<BTreeMap<_, _>>()
                .into_iter()
                .collect();
            serde_json::Value::Object(ordered)
        }
        serde_json::Value::Array(items) => {
            serde_json::Value::Array(items.into_iter().map(sort_value).collect())
        }
        other => other,
    }
}

pub fn id_bucket(id: &str) -> u8 {
    Sha256::digest(id.as_bytes())[0]
}

fn sha256_hex(bytes: &[u8]) -> String {
    hex_encode(&Sha256::digest(bytes))
}

fn hex_encode(bytes: &[u8]) -> String {
    bytes.iter().map(|b| format!("{b:02x}")).collect()
}

async fn table_exists(pool: &SqlitePool, table: &str) -> bool {
    sqlx::query_scalar::<_, i64>(
        "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM sqlite_master WHERE type = 'table' AND name = ?1",
    )
    .bind(table)
    .fetch_one(pool)
    .await
    .unwrap_or(0)
        > 0
}

async fn snapshot_row(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    table: &str,
    id: &str,
) -> Result<Option<String>, AppError> {
    if table == "app_kv" && deny_app_kv_key(id) {
        return Ok(None);
    }
    let cols: Vec<String> =
        sqlx::query_scalar(&format!("SELECT name FROM pragma_table_info('{table}')"))
            .fetch_all(pool)
            .await
            .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    if cols.is_empty() {
        return Ok(None);
    }
    let key = medoc_core::infrastructure::database::sync_registry::key_expr(table);
    let expr = json_object_sql(table, &cols);
    let sql = format!("SELECT {expr} FROM \"{table}\" WHERE {key} = ?1");
    sqlx::query_scalar(&sql)
        .bind(id)
        .fetch_optional(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(AppError::Database)
}

/// One-transaction read of live rows + tombstones for digest buckets.
pub async fn compute_digest(pool: &SqlitePool) -> Result<DigestResponse, AppError> {
    let mut tx = pool.begin().await.map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    let hashes = collect_row_hashes(&mut tx, None).await?;
    tx.commit().await.map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    let mut buckets = vec![Vec::<u8>::new(); 256];
    for row in &hashes {
        let b = id_bucket(&row.id) as usize;
        buckets[b].extend_from_slice(row.hash.as_bytes());
        buckets[b].extend_from_slice(row.id.as_bytes());
        buckets[b].extend_from_slice(row.table.as_bytes());
    }
    Ok(DigestResponse {
        buckets: buckets.into_iter().map(|b| sha256_hex(&b)).collect(),
    })
}

pub async fn compute_row_hashes(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    buckets: &[u8],
) -> Result<Vec<RowHash>, AppError> {
    let filter: HashSet<u8> = buckets.iter().copied().collect();
    let mut tx = pool.begin().await.map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    let hashes = collect_row_hashes(&mut tx, Some(&filter)).await?;
    tx.commit().await.map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    Ok(hashes)
}

async fn collect_row_hashes(
    tx: &mut sqlx::Transaction<'_, sqlx::Sqlite>,
    buckets: Option<&HashSet<u8>>,
) -> Result<Vec<RowHash>, AppError> {
    let mut out = Vec::new();
    for table in synced_tables() {
        let exists: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar(
            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM sqlite_master WHERE type = 'table' AND name = ?1",
        )
        .bind(table)
        .fetch_one(&mut **tx)
        .await
        .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
        if exists == 0 {
            continue;
        }
        let key = medoc_core::infrastructure::database::sync_registry::key_expr(table);
        let cols: Vec<String> =
            sqlx::query_scalar(&format!("SELECT name FROM pragma_table_info('{table}')"))
                .fetch_all(&mut **tx)
                .await
                .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
        if cols.is_empty() {
            continue;
        }
        let expr = json_object_sql(table, &cols);
        let sql = format!("SELECT CAST({key} AS TEXT), {expr} FROM \"{table}\"");
        let rows: Vec<(String, Option<String>)> = sqlx::query_as(&sql)
            .fetch_all(&mut **tx)
            .await
            .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
        for (id, json) in rows {
            if buckets.is_some_and(|set| !set.contains(&id_bucket(&id))) {
                continue;
            }
            if table == "app_kv" && deny_app_kv_key(&id) {
                continue;
            }
            let Some(json) = json else { continue };
            out.push(RowHash {
                table: table.to_string(),
                id,
                hash: hash_row_json(&json),
                tombstone: false,
            });
        }
    }
    let tombs: Vec<(String, String, String)> =
        sqlx::query_as("SELECT entity_table, entity_id, deleted_at FROM sync_tombstone")
            .fetch_all(&mut **tx)
            .await
            .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    for (table, id, deleted_at) in tombs {
        if buckets.is_some_and(|set| !set.contains(&id_bucket(&id))) {
            continue;
        }
        let payload =
            serde_json::json!({ "tombstone": true, "deleted_at": deleted_at }).to_string();
        out.push(RowHash {
            table,
            id,
            hash: hash_row_json(&payload),
            tombstone: true,
        });
    }
    Ok(out)
}

pub async fn fetch_rows(pool: &SqlitePool, ids: &[RowId]) -> Result<Vec<RowBody>, AppError> {
    if ids.len() > MAX_ROWS {
        return Err(AppError::Validation(format!(
            "reconcile rows request exceeds {MAX_ROWS}"
        )));
    }
    let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(ids.len());
    for row in ids {
        if !table_exists(pool, &row.table).await {
            continue;
        }
        if let Some(json) = snapshot_row(pool, &row.table, &row.id).await? {
            out.push(RowBody {
                table: row.table.clone(),
                id: row.id.clone(),
                op: "UPDATE".into(),
                payload_json: json,
            });
            continue;
        }
        let deleted: Option<String> = sqlx::query_scalar(
            "SELECT deleted_at FROM sync_tombstone WHERE entity_table = ?1 AND entity_id = ?2",
        )
        .bind(&row.table)
        .bind(&row.id)
        .fetch_optional(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
        if deleted.is_some() {
            out.push(RowBody {
                table: row.table.clone(),
                id: row.id.clone(),
                op: "DELETE".into(),
                payload_json: serde_json::json!({ "id": row.id }).to_string(),
            });
        }
    }
    Ok(out)
}

async fn pending_empty(pool: &SqlitePool) -> Result<bool, AppError> {
    let deployment = load_deployment(pool).await?;
    let local = ensure_local_device(pool, &deployment.device_label).await?;
    Ok(list_pending_outbox(pool, &local, 1).await?.is_empty())
}

async fn repair_required(pool: &SqlitePool) -> bool {
    medoc_core::infrastructure::database::repos::admin::app_kv::get(pool, REPAIR_KV)
        .await
        .ok()
        .flatten()
        .is_some_and(|v| v == "1" || v.eq_ignore_ascii_case("true"))
}

async fn last_run_too_recent(pool: &SqlitePool) -> bool {
    let Some(raw) =
        medoc_core::infrastructure::database::repos::admin::app_kv::get(pool, LAST_RUN_KV)
            .await
            .ok()
            .flatten()
    else {
        return false;
    };
    let Ok(ts) = chrono::DateTime::parse_from_rfc3339(&raw) else {
        return false;
    };
    let age = chrono::Utc::now().signed_duration_since(ts.with_timezone(&chrono::Utc));
    age.num_seconds() < HOUR_SECS
}

/// Automatic hourly pass after a caught-up replica cycle.
pub async fn maybe_hourly(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    pulled: u32,
    sync_error: Option<&str>,
) -> Result<Option<ReconcileReport>, AppError> {
    let deployment = load_deployment(pool).await?;
    if deployment.role != DeviceRole::Replica {
        return Ok(None);
    }
    if sync_error.is_some()
        || pulled >= 500
        || !pending_empty(pool).await?
        || repair_required(pool).await
    {
        return Ok(None);
    }
    if last_run_too_recent(pool).await {
        return Ok(None);
    }
    Ok(Some(run_against_master(pool).await?))
}

/// Operator-triggered reconcile (`ops.system`).
pub async fn run_now(pool: &SqlitePool) -> Result<ReconcileReport, AppError> {
    let deployment = load_deployment(pool).await?;
    if deployment.role != DeviceRole::Replica {
        return Err(AppError::Validation(
            "reconcile runs on a replica only".into(),
        ));
    }
    if repair_required(pool).await {
        return Err(AppError::Validation("re-pair required".into()));
    }
    if !pending_empty(pool).await? {
        return Err(AppError::Validation(
            "reconcile waits until the outbox is empty".into(),
        ));
    }
    run_against_master(pool).await
}

async fn run_against_master(pool: &SqlitePool) -> Result<ReconcileReport, AppError> {
    let deployment = load_deployment(pool).await?;
    let local = ensure_local_device(pool, &deployment.device_label).await?;
    let Some(_guard) = ReconcileGuard::acquire(&local) else {
        return Ok(ReconcileReport {
            skipped: true,
            error: Some("reconcile already running".into()),
            ..Default::default()
        });
    };
    let mut report = ReconcileReport::default();
    if deployment.master_base_url.is_empty() || deployment.activation_token.is_empty() {
        report.error = Some("master URL or activation token missing".into());
        persist_result(pool, &report).await?;
        return Ok(report);
    }
    let client = crate::engine::master_http_client_for_reconcile(pool, &deployment).await?;
    let base = deployment.master_base_url.trim_end_matches('/');
    let token = &deployment.activation_token;

    let local_digest = compute_digest(pool).await?;
    let remote_digest: DigestResponse = post_json(
        &client,
        &crate::paths::api_url(base, crate::paths::SYNC_DIGEST),
        token,
        &serde_json::json!({}),
    )
    .await?;
    let mut mismatch = Vec::new();
    for (i, (local, remote)) in local_digest
        .buckets
        .iter()
        .zip(remote_digest.buckets.iter())
        .enumerate()
    {
        if local != remote {
            mismatch.push(i as u8);
        }
    }
    report.buckets_checked = mismatch.len() as u32;
    if mismatch.is_empty() {
        persist_result(pool, &report).await?;
        return Ok(report);
    }

    let remote_wrap: RowHashesBody = post_json(
        &client,
        &crate::paths::api_url(base, crate::paths::SYNC_ROW_HASHES),
        token,
        &RowHashesRequest {
            buckets: mismatch.clone(),
        },
    )
    .await?;
    let remote_hashes = remote_wrap.rows;
    let local_hashes = compute_row_hashes(pool, &mismatch).await?;
    let remote_map: HashMap<(String, String), &RowHash> = remote_hashes
        .iter()
        .map(|r| ((r.table.clone(), r.id.clone()), r))
        .collect();
    let local_map: HashMap<(String, String), &RowHash> = local_hashes
        .iter()
        .map(|r| ((r.table.clone(), r.id.clone()), r))
        .collect();

    let mut fetch_ids = Vec::new();
    let mut keys = HashSet::new();
    for key in remote_map.keys().chain(local_map.keys()) {
        keys.insert(key.clone());
    }
    for key in keys {
        let remote = remote_map.get(&key);
        let local = local_map.get(&key);
        match (local, remote) {
            (Some(l), Some(r)) if l.hash == r.hash => {}
            _ => fetch_ids.push(RowId {
                table: key.0,
                id: key.1,
            }),
        }
        if fetch_ids.len() >= MAX_ROWS {
            break;
        }
    }

    if !fetch_ids.is_empty() {
        let rows_wrap: RowsBody = post_json(
            &client,
            &crate::paths::api_url(base, crate::paths::SYNC_ROWS),
            token,
            &RowsRequest {
                ids: fetch_ids.clone(),
            },
        )
        .await?;
        let rows = rows_wrap.rows;
        report.rows_fetched = rows.len() as u32;
        for row in rows {
            force_apply_payload(pool, &row.table, &row.id, &row.op, &row.payload_json).await?;
            report.applied += 1;
        }
        for id in fetch_ids {
            if remote_map.get(&(id.table.clone(), id.id.clone())).is_none() {
                if snapshot_row(pool, &id.table, &id.id).await?.is_some() {
                    force_apply_payload(
                        pool,
                        &id.table,
                        &id.id,
                        "DELETE",
                        &serde_json::json!({ "id": id.id }).to_string(),
                    )
                    .await?;
                    report.applied += 1;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    persist_result(pool, &report).await?;
    let _ = medoc_core::infrastructure::database::repos::admin::audit::create(
        pool,
        "system",
        "RECONCILE",
        "sync",
        Some(&local),
        Some(&serde_json::to_string(&report).unwrap_or_default()),
    )
    .await;
    Ok(report)
}

async fn persist_result(pool: &SqlitePool, report: &ReconcileReport) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    let now = chrono::Utc::now().to_rfc3339();
    medoc_core::infrastructure::database::repos::admin::app_kv::set(pool, LAST_RUN_KV, &now)
        .await?;
    medoc_core::infrastructure::database::repos::admin::app_kv::set(
        pool,
        LAST_RESULT_KV,
        &serde_json::to_string(report).unwrap_or_default(),
    )
    .await?;
    Ok(())
}

async fn post_json<T: serde::de::DeserializeOwned, B: Serialize>(
    client: &reqwest::Client,
    url: &str,
    token: &str,
    body: &B,
) -> Result<T, AppError> {
    let resp = client
        .post(url)
        .bearer_auth(token)
        .json(body)
        .send()
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::Internal(format!("reconcile http: {e}")))?;
    if !resp.status().is_success() {
        let status = resp.status();
        let body = resp.text().await.unwrap_or_default();
        return Err(AppError::Internal(format!(
            "reconcile http status {status}: {body}"
        )));
    }
    resp.json()
        .await
        .map_err(|e| AppError::Internal(format!("reconcile json: {e}")))
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn hash_is_stable_across_key_order() {
        let a = r#"{"b":1,"a":2}"#;
        let b = r#"{"a":2,"b":1}"#;
        assert_eq!(hash_row_json(a), hash_row_json(b));
    }

    #[test]
    fn buckets_span_full_byte() {
        let mut seen = HashSet::new();
        for i in 0..1024u32 {
            seen.insert(id_bucket(&format!("id-{i}")));
        }
        assert!(seen.len() > 8);
    }
}
