use std::collections::HashMap;

use chrono::Utc;
use medoc_core::error::AppError;
use sqlx::SqlitePool;
use uuid::Uuid;

use crate::deployment::{
    DeviceRole, SyncDeploymentConfig, APP_KV_DEPLOYMENT_KEY, APP_KV_DEVICE_ID_KEY,
};
use crate::schema::ensure_sync_tables;

use super::types::{is_synced_table, synced_tables, OutboxEntry, SyncPeer, SyncStatusSnapshot};

pub async fn ensure_local_device(pool: &SqlitePool, label: &str) -> Result<String, AppError> {
    ensure_sync_tables(pool).await?;
    let display_name = if label.is_empty() {
        "This device"
    } else {
        label
    };
    let existing =
        medoc_core::infrastructure::database::repos::admin::app_kv::get(pool, APP_KV_DEVICE_ID_KEY)
            .await?;
    if let Some(id) = existing.filter(|s| !s.trim().is_empty()) {
        let stored_role: Option<String> =
            sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT role FROM sync_device WHERE device_id = ?1")
                .bind(&id)
                .fetch_optional(pool)
                .await
                .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
        let role = stored_role
            .as_deref()
            .and_then(DeviceRole::parse)
            .unwrap_or(DeviceRole::Master);
        register_device_row(pool, &id, display_name, role, true, None, None).await?;
        return Ok(id);
    }
    let legacy = medoc_core::infrastructure::database::repos::admin::app_kv::get(
        pool,
        medoc_core::infrastructure::database::repos::admin::license::KEY_DEVICE_ID,
    )
    .await?;
    let id = legacy
        .filter(|s| !s.trim().is_empty())
        .unwrap_or_else(|| Uuid::new_v4().to_string());
    medoc_core::infrastructure::database::repos::admin::app_kv::set(
        pool,
        APP_KV_DEVICE_ID_KEY,
        &id,
    )
    .await?;
    register_device_row(
        pool,
        &id,
        display_name,
        DeviceRole::Master,
        true,
        None,
        None,
    )
    .await?;
    Ok(id)
}

/// Point this install's sync identity at `to_id` and move the local outbox
/// with it. The activation token's `device_id` is what the master compares
/// to `fromDeviceId`; a newer local fingerprint must not keep its own id.
pub async fn adopt_local_device_id(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    from_id: &str,
    to_id: &str,
    label: &str,
    role: DeviceRole,
) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    if to_id.is_empty() || from_id == to_id {
        return Ok(());
    }
    ensure_sync_tables(pool).await?;
    let display = if label.is_empty() {
        "This device"
    } else {
        label
    };
    let mut tx = pool.begin().await.map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    sqlx::query(
        "INSERT INTO sync_device (device_id, display_name, role, is_local, last_seen_at)
         VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, 1, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP)
         ON CONFLICT(device_id) DO UPDATE SET
           display_name = excluded.display_name,
           role = excluded.role,
           is_local = 1,
           last_seen_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP",
    )
    .bind(to_id)
    .bind(display)
    .bind(role.as_str())
    .execute(&mut *tx)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;

    let max_to: i64 =
        sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT COALESCE(MAX(seq), 0) FROM sync_outbox WHERE device_id = ?1")
            .bind(to_id)
            .fetch_one(&mut *tx)
            .await
            .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    if max_to > 0 {
        sqlx::query("UPDATE sync_outbox SET seq = seq + ?1 WHERE device_id = ?2")
            .bind(max_to)
            .bind(from_id)
            .execute(&mut *tx)
            .await
            .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    }
    sqlx::query("UPDATE sync_outbox SET device_id = ?1 WHERE device_id = ?2")
        .bind(to_id)
        .bind(from_id)
        .execute(&mut *tx)
        .await
        .map_err(AppError::Database)?;

    let from_seq: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar(
        "SELECT COALESCE((SELECT seq FROM sync_vector WHERE device_id = ?1), 0)",
    )
    .bind(from_id)
    .fetch_one(&mut *tx)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    let to_seq: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar(
        "SELECT COALESCE((SELECT seq FROM sync_vector WHERE device_id = ?1), 0)",
    )
    .bind(to_id)
    .fetch_one(&mut *tx)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    let max_out: i64 =
        sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT COALESCE(MAX(seq), 0) FROM sync_outbox WHERE device_id = ?1")
            .bind(to_id)
            .fetch_one(&mut *tx)
            .await
            .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    let merged = from_seq.max(to_seq).max(max_out);
    sqlx::query("DELETE FROM sync_vector WHERE device_id = ?1")
        .bind(from_id)
        .execute(&mut *tx)
        .await
        .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    sqlx::query(
        "INSERT INTO sync_vector (device_id, seq) VALUES (?1, ?2)
         ON CONFLICT(device_id) DO UPDATE SET seq = excluded.seq",
    )
    .bind(to_id)
    .bind(merged)
    .execute(&mut *tx)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;

    let occupied: Vec<String> = sqlx::query_scalar(
        "SELECT target_device_id FROM sync_peer_vector WHERE source_device_id = ?1",
    )
    .bind(to_id)
    .fetch_all(&mut *tx)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    for target in occupied {
        sqlx::query(
            "DELETE FROM sync_peer_vector WHERE source_device_id = ?1 AND target_device_id = ?2",
        )
        .bind(from_id)
        .bind(&target)
        .execute(&mut *tx)
        .await
        .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    }
    sqlx::query("UPDATE sync_peer_vector SET source_device_id = ?1 WHERE source_device_id = ?2")
        .bind(to_id)
        .bind(from_id)
        .execute(&mut *tx)
        .await
        .map_err(AppError::Database)?;

    sqlx::query("UPDATE sync_device SET is_local = 0 WHERE is_local = 1 AND device_id != ?1")
        .bind(to_id)
        .execute(&mut *tx)
        .await
        .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    sqlx::query(
        "INSERT INTO app_kv (key, value, updated_at) VALUES (?1, ?2, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP)
         ON CONFLICT(key) DO UPDATE SET value = excluded.value, updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP",
    )
    .bind(APP_KV_DEVICE_ID_KEY)
    .bind(to_id)
    .execute(&mut *tx)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    tx.commit().await.map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    Ok(())
}

pub async fn load_deployment(pool: &SqlitePool) -> Result<SyncDeploymentConfig, AppError> {
    let raw = medoc_core::infrastructure::database::repos::admin::app_kv::get(
        pool,
        APP_KV_DEPLOYMENT_KEY,
    )
    .await?;
    match raw {
        Some(s) => serde_json::from_str(&s)
            .map_err(|e| AppError::Validation(format!("sync.deployment.v1: {e}"))),
        None => Ok(SyncDeploymentConfig::default()),
    }
}

pub async fn save_deployment(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    cfg: &SyncDeploymentConfig,
) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    let s = serde_json::to_string(cfg)
        .map_err(|e| AppError::Internal(format!("sync deployment serialise: {e}")))?;
    medoc_core::infrastructure::database::repos::admin::app_kv::set(pool, APP_KV_DEPLOYMENT_KEY, &s)
        .await
}

/// Recorder helper for repository write paths.
///
/// Semantics:
///
/// 1. Looks up the current deployment. If it is not `serverless_peer`,
///    the call is a no-op (master-only practices stay on the simpler path).
/// 2. Drops any write whose table is not in [`SYNCED_TABLES`].
/// 3. Otherwise appends one row to `sync_outbox` and bumps the local vector.
///
/// Returns `Ok(())` in all cases so callers can `?` it from their write
/// paths without changing return types.
pub async fn sync_record_or_noop(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    entity_table: &str,
    entity_id: &str,
    op: &str,
    payload_json: &str,
) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    if !is_synced_table(entity_table) {
        return Ok(());
    }
    let deployment = load_deployment(pool).await.unwrap_or_default();
    let peer_mode = deployment.mode == crate::deployment::DeploymentMode::ServerlessPeer;
    let replica_joined: i64 =
        medoc_core::infrastructure::database::recorded_write::count_remote_replicas(pool)
            .await
            .unwrap_or(0);
    if !peer_mode && replica_joined == 0 {
        return Ok(());
    }
    let local_id = ensure_local_device(pool, &deployment.device_label).await?;
    append_outbox(pool, &local_id, entity_table, entity_id, op, payload_json).await?;
    Ok(())
}

pub async fn append_outbox(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    device_id: &str,
    entity_table: &str,
    entity_id: &str,
    op: &str,
    payload_json: &str,
) -> Result<OutboxEntry, AppError> {
    ensure_sync_tables(pool).await?;
    let seq: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar(
        "INSERT INTO sync_vector (device_id, seq) VALUES (?1, 1)
         ON CONFLICT(device_id) DO UPDATE SET seq = seq + 1
         RETURNING seq",
    )
    .bind(device_id)
    .fetch_one(pool)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;

    let id = Uuid::new_v4().to_string();
    let created_at = Utc::now().to_rfc3339();
    let payload_json =
        crate::hlc::stamp_payload(pool, entity_table, entity_id, payload_json).await?;
    sqlx::query(
        "INSERT INTO sync_outbox (id, device_id, seq, entity_table, entity_id, op, payload_json, created_at)
         VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4, ?5, ?6, ?7, ?8)",
    )
    .bind(&id)
    .bind(device_id)
    .bind(seq)
    .bind(entity_table)
    .bind(entity_id)
    .bind(op)
    .bind(&payload_json)
    .bind(&created_at)
    .execute(pool)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;

    enqueue_packet_row(pool, device_id, entity_table, seq, &id, &created_at).await?;

    Ok(OutboxEntry {
        id,
        device_id: device_id.to_string(),
        seq,
        entity_table: entity_table.to_string(),
        entity_id: entity_id.to_string(),
        op: op.to_string(),
        payload_json: payload_json.to_string(),
        created_at,
    })
}

const EXCHANGE_QUEUES: &[&str] = &[
    "patients",
    "appointments",
    "charts",
    "finance",
    "parameters",
    "orders",
    "tasks",
    "work_time",
    "practice",
];

fn exchange_queue_filter(queues: &[String]) -> Result<String, AppError> {
    if queues.is_empty() {
        return Ok(String::new());
    }
    let list = queues
        .iter()
        .map(|queue| queue.trim())
        .filter(|queue| EXCHANGE_QUEUES.contains(queue))
        .map(|queue| format!("'{queue}'"))
        .collect::<Vec<_>>()
        .join(", ");
    if list.is_empty() {
        return Err(AppError::Validation(
            "no known sync queue in the exchange request".into(),
        ));
    }
    Ok(format!(" AND q.queue_name IN ({list})"))
}

async fn enqueue_packet_row(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    device_id: &str,
    entity_table: &str,
    seq: i64,
    outbox_id: &str,
    created_at: &str,
) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    medoc_core::infrastructure::database::migrations::ensure_packet_queue(pool).await?;
    let queue = medoc_core::infrastructure::database::sync_registry::queue_of(entity_table);
    sqlx::query(
        "INSERT INTO sync_packet_queue (id, device_id, queue_name, seq, outbox_id, status, created_at)
         VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4, ?5, 'pending', ?6)
         ON CONFLICT(device_id, seq) DO NOTHING",
    )
    .bind(outbox_id)
    .bind(device_id)
    .bind(queue)
    .bind(seq)
    .bind(outbox_id)
    .bind(created_at)
    .execute(pool)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    Ok(())
}

/// Append one local change onto this device's packet queue.
///
/// The main device and a replica both use this. The next exchange sends the
/// packet to the other device, which applies it.
pub async fn enqueue_update_packet(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    entity_table: &str,
    entity_id: &str,
    op: &str,
    payload_json: &str,
) -> Result<OutboxEntry, AppError> {
    if !is_synced_table(entity_table) {
        return Err(AppError::Validation(format!(
            "table {entity_table} is not on a sync queue"
        )));
    }
    let deployment = load_deployment(pool).await.unwrap_or_default();
    let local_id = ensure_local_device(pool, &deployment.device_label).await?;
    append_outbox(
        pool,
        &local_id,
        entity_table,
        entity_id,
        op,
        payload_json,
    )
    .await
}

/// Packets this device still owes `peer_device_id`, newest page first by seq.
pub async fn list_exchange_packets(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    device_id: &str,
    peer_device_id: &str,
    queues: &[String],
    limit: i64,
) -> Result<Vec<OutboxEntry>, AppError> {
    let filter = match exchange_queue_filter(queues) {
        Ok(filter) => filter,
        Err(_) => return Ok(Vec::new()),
    };
    medoc_core::infrastructure::database::migrations::ensure_packet_queue(pool).await?;
    let since: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar(
        "SELECT COALESCE(through_seq, 0) FROM sync_peer_vector
         WHERE source_device_id = ?1 AND target_device_id = ?2",
    )
    .bind(device_id)
    .bind(peer_device_id)
    .fetch_optional(pool)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?
    .unwrap_or(0);
    let sql = format!(
        "SELECT o.id, o.device_id, o.seq, o.entity_table, o.entity_id, o.op, o.payload_json, o.created_at
         FROM sync_packet_queue q
         JOIN sync_outbox o ON o.id = q.outbox_id
         WHERE q.device_id = ?1 AND q.seq > ?2
           AND o.delivered_at IS NULL
           AND (o.fail_reason IS NULL OR o.fail_reason = '')
           {filter}
         ORDER BY q.seq ASC
         LIMIT ?3"
    );
    let rows =
        sqlx::query_as::<_, (String, String, i64, String, String, String, String, String)>(&sql)
            .bind(device_id)
            .bind(since)
            .bind(limit)
            .fetch_all(pool)
            .await
            .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    Ok(rows
        .into_iter()
        .map(
            |(id, device_id, seq, entity_table, entity_id, op, payload_json, created_at)| {
                OutboxEntry {
                    id,
                    device_id,
                    seq,
                    entity_table,
                    entity_id,
                    op,
                    payload_json,
                    created_at,
                }
            },
        )
        .collect())
}

pub async fn list_pending_outbox(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    device_id: &str,
    limit: i64,
) -> Result<Vec<OutboxEntry>, AppError> {
    let rows = sqlx::query_as::<_, (String, String, i64, String, String, String, String, String)>(
        "SELECT id, device_id, seq, entity_table, entity_id, op, payload_json, created_at
         FROM sync_outbox
         WHERE device_id = ?1 AND delivered_at IS NULL
           AND (fail_reason IS NULL OR fail_reason = '')
         ORDER BY seq ASC
         LIMIT ?2",
    )
    .bind(device_id)
    .bind(limit)
    .fetch_all(pool)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;

    Ok(rows
        .into_iter()
        .map(
            |(id, device_id, seq, entity_table, entity_id, op, payload_json, created_at)| {
                OutboxEntry {
                    id,
                    device_id,
                    seq,
                    entity_table,
                    entity_id,
                    op,
                    payload_json,
                    created_at,
                }
            },
        )
        .collect())
}

/// Pending rows a joined device may send while the first snapshot is still
/// copying. Practice settings (`app_kv`, absences) stay queued so this
/// device's seed copy does not overwrite the main device.
/// Pending rows for one page queue. A blocked queue does not hide the others.
pub async fn list_pending_outbox_tables(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    device_id: &str,
    tables: &[&str],
    limit: i64,
) -> Result<Vec<OutboxEntry>, AppError> {
    let list = tables
        .iter()
        .filter(|table| is_synced_table(table))
        .map(|table| format!("'{table}'"))
        .collect::<Vec<_>>()
        .join(", ");
    if list.is_empty() {
        return Ok(Vec::new());
    }
    let sql = format!(
        "SELECT id, device_id, seq, entity_table, entity_id, op, payload_json, created_at
         FROM sync_outbox
         WHERE device_id = ?1 AND delivered_at IS NULL
           AND (fail_reason IS NULL OR fail_reason = '')
           AND entity_table IN ({list})
         ORDER BY seq ASC
         LIMIT ?2"
    );
    let rows =
        sqlx::query_as::<_, (String, String, i64, String, String, String, String, String)>(&sql)
            .bind(device_id)
            .bind(limit)
            .fetch_all(pool)
            .await
            .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    Ok(rows
        .into_iter()
        .map(
            |(id, device_id, seq, entity_table, entity_id, op, payload_json, created_at)| {
                OutboxEntry {
                    id,
                    device_id,
                    seq,
                    entity_table,
                    entity_id,
                    op,
                    payload_json,
                    created_at,
                }
            },
        )
        .collect())
}

pub async fn list_pending_member_outbox(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    device_id: &str,
    limit: i64,
) -> Result<Vec<OutboxEntry>, AppError> {
    let tables = member_table_sql_list();
    let sql = format!(
        "SELECT id, device_id, seq, entity_table, entity_id, op, payload_json, created_at
         FROM sync_outbox
         WHERE device_id = ?1 AND delivered_at IS NULL
           AND (fail_reason IS NULL OR fail_reason = '')
           AND entity_table IN ({tables})
         ORDER BY seq ASC
         LIMIT ?2"
    );
    let rows =
        sqlx::query_as::<_, (String, String, i64, String, String, String, String, String)>(&sql)
            .bind(device_id)
            .bind(limit)
            .fetch_all(pool)
            .await
            .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    Ok(rows
        .into_iter()
        .map(
            |(id, device_id, seq, entity_table, entity_id, op, payload_json, created_at)| {
                OutboxEntry {
                    id,
                    device_id,
                    seq,
                    entity_table,
                    entity_id,
                    op,
                    payload_json,
                    created_at,
                }
            },
        )
        .collect())
}

fn member_table_sql_list() -> String {
    use medoc_core::infrastructure::database::sync_registry::{class_of, SyncClass};
    synced_tables()
        .filter(|table| {
            matches!(
                class_of(table),
                Some(SyncClass::Shared | SyncClass::Numbered | SyncClass::Files)
            )
        })
        .map(|table| format!("'{table}'"))
        .collect::<Vec<_>>()
        .join(", ")
}

pub async fn mark_outbox_ids_delivered(pool: &SqlitePool, ids: &[String]) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    if ids.is_empty() {
        return Ok(());
    }
    let now = Utc::now().to_rfc3339();
    for id in ids {
        sqlx::query(
            "UPDATE sync_outbox SET delivered_at = ?1 WHERE id = ?2 AND delivered_at IS NULL",
        )
        .bind(&now)
        .bind(id)
        .execute(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    }
    Ok(())
}

pub async fn mark_outbox_delivered(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    device_id: &str,
    through_seq: i64,
) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    let now = Utc::now().to_rfc3339();
    sqlx::query(
        "UPDATE sync_outbox SET delivered_at = ?1
         WHERE device_id = ?2 AND seq <= ?3 AND delivered_at IS NULL
           AND (fail_reason IS NULL OR fail_reason = '')",
    )
    .bind(&now)
    .bind(device_id)
    .bind(through_seq)
    .execute(pool)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    Ok(())
}

pub async fn mark_outbox_failed(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    entry_id: &str,
    reason: &str,
) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    sqlx::query("UPDATE sync_outbox SET fail_reason = ?1 WHERE id = ?2")
        .bind(reason)
        .bind(entry_id)
        .execute(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    Ok(())
}

/// Outbox rows from `source_device_id` not yet pushed to `target_device_id` (mesh).
pub async fn list_pending_outbox_for_peer(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    source_device_id: &str,
    target_device_id: &str,
) -> Result<Vec<OutboxEntry>, AppError> {
    let since: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar(
        "SELECT COALESCE(through_seq, 0) FROM sync_peer_vector
         WHERE source_device_id = ?1 AND target_device_id = ?2",
    )
    .bind(source_device_id)
    .bind(target_device_id)
    .fetch_optional(pool)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?
    .unwrap_or(0);

    let rows = sqlx::query_as::<_, (String, String, i64, String, String, String, String, String)>(
        "SELECT id, device_id, seq, entity_table, entity_id, op, payload_json, created_at
         FROM sync_outbox
         WHERE device_id = ?1 AND seq > ?2
           AND (fail_reason IS NULL OR fail_reason = '')
         ORDER BY seq ASC",
    )
    .bind(source_device_id)
    .bind(since)
    .fetch_all(pool)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;

    Ok(rows
        .into_iter()
        .map(
            |(id, device_id, seq, entity_table, entity_id, op, payload_json, created_at)| {
                OutboxEntry {
                    id,
                    device_id,
                    seq,
                    entity_table,
                    entity_id,
                    op,
                    payload_json,
                    created_at,
                }
            },
        )
        .collect())
}

pub async fn mark_peer_delivery(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    source_device_id: &str,
    target_device_id: &str,
    through_seq: i64,
) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    let now = Utc::now().to_rfc3339();
    sqlx::query(
        "INSERT INTO sync_peer_vector (source_device_id, target_device_id, through_seq, updated_at)
         VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4)
         ON CONFLICT(source_device_id, target_device_id) DO UPDATE SET
           through_seq = MAX(sync_peer_vector.through_seq, excluded.through_seq),
           updated_at = excluded.updated_at",
    )
    .bind(source_device_id)
    .bind(target_device_id)
    .bind(through_seq)
    .bind(&now)
    .execute(pool)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    Ok(())
}

pub async fn list_entries_since(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    device_id: &str,
    since_seq: i64,
) -> Result<Vec<OutboxEntry>, AppError> {
    let rows = sqlx::query_as::<_, (String, String, i64, String, String, String, String, String)>(
        "SELECT id, device_id, seq, entity_table, entity_id, op, payload_json, created_at
         FROM sync_outbox
         WHERE device_id = ?1 AND seq > ?2
         ORDER BY seq ASC
         LIMIT 500",
    )
    .bind(device_id)
    .bind(since_seq)
    .fetch_all(pool)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;

    Ok(rows
        .into_iter()
        .map(
            |(id, device_id, seq, entity_table, entity_id, op, payload_json, created_at)| {
                OutboxEntry {
                    id,
                    device_id,
                    seq,
                    entity_table,
                    entity_id,
                    op,
                    payload_json,
                    created_at,
                }
            },
        )
        .collect())
}

/// Keys that decide how the appointment calendar is drawn: hours, closures,
/// blocked times, and appointment preferences (buffer, colours, reminders).
const CALENDAR_CONTROL_KV_KEYS: &[&str] = &[
    "practice.work_hours.v1",
    "practice.blockedTimes.v1",
    "practice.preferences.v1",
    "practice.preferences-appointment.v1",
    "practice.calendar-workflows.v1",
];

/// Queue the current calendar controls so the other device can apply the
/// newer copy. Safe to call once per launch; later edits are recorded on write.
pub async fn enqueue_calendar_controls(pool: &SqlitePool) -> Result<u32, AppError> {
    let deployment = load_deployment(pool).await?;
    if deployment.mode != crate::deployment::DeploymentMode::ServerlessPeer {
        return Ok(0);
    }
    let local_id = ensure_local_device(pool, &deployment.device_label).await?;
    let mut queued = 0u32;
    for key in CALENDAR_CONTROL_KV_KEYS {
        let row: Option<(String, String)> =
            sqlx::query_as("SELECT value, updated_at FROM app_kv WHERE key = ?1")
                .bind(key)
                .fetch_optional(pool)
                .await
                .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
        let Some((value, updated_at)) = row else {
            continue;
        };
        let payload = serde_json::json!({
            "key": key,
            "value": value,
            "updated_at": updated_at,
        });
        append_outbox(
            pool,
            &local_id,
            "app_kv",
            key,
            "UPDATE",
            &payload.to_string(),
        )
        .await?;
        queued += 1;
    }
    queued += snapshot_table(pool, &local_id, "absence").await?;
    Ok(queued)
}

/// Dump current practice rows into the outbox so a replica can pull a full initial package.
pub async fn enqueue_full_snapshot(pool: &SqlitePool) -> Result<u32, AppError> {
    ensure_sync_tables(pool).await?;
    let deployment = load_deployment(pool).await?;
    let local_id = ensure_local_device(pool, &deployment.device_label).await?;
    let mut queued = 0u32;
    for table in synced_tables() {
        queued += snapshot_table(pool, &local_id, table).await?;
    }
    tracing::info!(
        target: "medoc::sync",
        event = "SYNC_SNAPSHOT_QUEUED",
        rows = queued
    );
    Ok(queued)
}

async fn snapshot_table(pool: &SqlitePool, device_id: &str, table: &str) -> Result<u32, AppError> {
    let cols: Vec<String> =
        sqlx::query_scalar(&format!("SELECT name FROM pragma_table_info('{table}')"))
            .fetch_all(pool)
            .await
            .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    if cols.is_empty() {
        return Ok(0);
    }
    // F146/F147: entity id is not always column `id` (hint, preference, chart_validation).
    let key = medoc_core::infrastructure::database::sync_registry::key_expr(table);
    if medoc_core::infrastructure::database::sync_registry::key_is_column(table) {
        let id_col = medoc_core::infrastructure::database::sync_registry::id_column(table);
        if !cols.iter().any(|c| c == id_col) {
            return Ok(0);
        }
    } else if !(cols.iter().any(|c| c == "patient_id")
        && cols.iter().any(|c| c == "section_or_item"))
    {
        return Ok(0);
    }
    let mut from_sql = table.to_string();
    if table == "app_kv" {
        let predicate =
            medoc_core::infrastructure::database::sync_registry::synced_app_kv_predicate("key");
        from_sql.push_str(" WHERE ");
        from_sql.push_str(&predicate);
    }
    let count: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar(&format!("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM {from_sql}"))
        .fetch_one(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    if count <= 0 {
        return Ok(0);
    }
    sqlx::query(
        "INSERT INTO sync_vector (device_id, seq) VALUES (?1, 0)
         ON CONFLICT(device_id) DO NOTHING",
    )
    .bind(device_id)
    .execute(pool)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    let new_max: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar(
        "UPDATE sync_vector SET seq = seq + ?2 WHERE device_id = ?1 RETURNING seq",
    )
    .bind(device_id)
    .bind(count)
    .fetch_one(pool)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    let seq_base = new_max - count;
    let json_expr = snapshot_pairs(table, &cols);
    let created_at = chrono::Utc::now().to_rfc3339();
    let insert_sql = format!(
        "INSERT INTO sync_outbox (id, device_id, seq, entity_table, entity_id, op, payload_json, created_at)
         SELECT lower(hex(randomblob(16))), ?1, ?2 + ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY {key}),
                ?3, CAST({key} AS TEXT), 'UPDATE', {json_expr}, ?4
         FROM {from_sql}"
    );
    sqlx::query(&insert_sql)
        .bind(device_id)
        .bind(seq_base)
        .bind(table)
        .bind(&created_at)
        .execute(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    Ok(count as u32)
}

fn snapshot_pairs(table: &str, cols: &[String]) -> String {
    medoc_core::infrastructure::database::recorded_write::json_object_sql(table, cols)
}

fn is_private_app_kv_key(key: &str) -> bool {
    medoc_core::infrastructure::database::sync_registry::deny_app_kv_key(key)
}

/// JSON object for one live row, using the same `json_object` shape as a snapshot.
/// `None` when the table is not synced or the row is gone.
pub async fn load_row_payload_json(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    table: &str,
    entity_id: &str,
) -> Result<Option<String>, AppError> {
    if !is_synced_table(table) {
        return Ok(None);
    }
    let key = medoc_core::infrastructure::database::sync_registry::key_expr(table);
    let cols: Vec<String> =
        sqlx::query_scalar(&format!("SELECT name FROM pragma_table_info('{table}')"))
            .fetch_all(pool)
            .await
            .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    if cols.is_empty() {
        return Ok(None);
    }
    if table == "app_kv" && is_private_app_kv_key(entity_id) {
        return Ok(None);
    }
    let json_expr = snapshot_pairs(table, &cols);
    let sql = format!("SELECT {json_expr} FROM \"{table}\" WHERE {key} = ?1");
    let json: Option<String> = sqlx::query_scalar(&sql)
        .bind(entity_id)
        .fetch_optional(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    Ok(json.filter(|s| !s.is_empty()))
}

/// Remember the highest seq observed from a remote device (replica pull cursor).
pub async fn set_observed_seq(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    device_id: &str,
    seq: i64,
) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    ensure_sync_tables(pool).await?;
    sqlx::query(
        "INSERT INTO sync_device (device_id, display_name, role, is_local)
         VALUES (?1, 'Main', 'MASTER', 0)
         ON CONFLICT(device_id) DO NOTHING",
    )
    .bind(device_id)
    .execute(pool)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    sqlx::query(
        "INSERT INTO sync_vector (device_id, seq) VALUES (?1, ?2)
         ON CONFLICT(device_id) DO UPDATE SET
           seq = CASE WHEN excluded.seq > sync_vector.seq THEN excluded.seq ELSE sync_vector.seq END",
    )
    .bind(device_id)
    .bind(seq)
    .execute(pool)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    Ok(())
}

/// Keep a foreign-key miss so a later page can apply it after the parent arrives.
/// Attempts stay put until the caller decides the snapshot is no longer moving.
pub async fn save_deferred_pull(pool: &SqlitePool, entry: &OutboxEntry) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    ensure_sync_tables(pool).await?;
    sqlx::query(
        "INSERT INTO sync_pull_failure (
            source_device_id, source_seq, attempts, payload_json, entity_table, entity_id, op,
            entry_created_at, entry_id
         ) VALUES (?1, ?2, 0, ?3, ?4, ?5, ?6, ?7, ?8)
         ON CONFLICT(source_device_id, source_seq) DO UPDATE SET
            payload_json = excluded.payload_json,
            entity_table = excluded.entity_table,
            entity_id = excluded.entity_id,
            op = excluded.op,
            entry_created_at = excluded.entry_created_at,
            entry_id = excluded.entry_id",
    )
    .bind(&entry.device_id)
    .bind(entry.seq)
    .bind(&entry.payload_json)
    .bind(&entry.entity_table)
    .bind(&entry.entity_id)
    .bind(&entry.op)
    .bind(&entry.created_at)
    .bind(&entry.id)
    .execute(pool)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    Ok(())
}

pub async fn list_deferred_pulls(pool: &SqlitePool) -> Result<Vec<OutboxEntry>, AppError> {
    ensure_sync_tables(pool).await?;
    let rows = sqlx::query_as::<_, (String, String, i64, String, String, String, String, String)>(
        "SELECT entry_id, source_device_id, source_seq, entity_table, entity_id, op,
                payload_json, entry_created_at
         FROM sync_pull_failure
         WHERE payload_json IS NOT NULL AND TRIM(payload_json) != ''
         ORDER BY source_seq ASC",
    )
    .fetch_all(pool)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    Ok(rows
        .into_iter()
        .map(
            |(id, device_id, seq, entity_table, entity_id, op, payload_json, created_at)| {
                OutboxEntry {
                    id,
                    device_id,
                    seq,
                    entity_table,
                    entity_id,
                    op,
                    payload_json,
                    created_at,
                }
            },
        )
        .collect())
}

pub async fn bump_deferred_pull(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    source_device_id: &str,
    source_seq: i64,
) -> Result<i64, AppError> {
    ensure_sync_tables(pool).await?;
    let attempts: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar(
        "UPDATE sync_pull_failure SET attempts = attempts + 1
         WHERE source_device_id = ?1 AND source_seq = ?2
         RETURNING attempts",
    )
    .bind(source_device_id)
    .bind(source_seq)
    .fetch_one(pool)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    Ok(attempts)
}

/// How many times this pulled row has failed to apply. Starts at 1.
pub async fn record_pull_failure(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    source_device_id: &str,
    source_seq: i64,
) -> Result<i64, AppError> {
    ensure_sync_tables(pool).await?;
    let attempts: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar(
        "INSERT INTO sync_pull_failure (source_device_id, source_seq, attempts)
         VALUES (?1, ?2, 1)
         ON CONFLICT(source_device_id, source_seq) DO UPDATE SET
           attempts = attempts + 1
         RETURNING attempts",
    )
    .bind(source_device_id)
    .bind(source_seq)
    .fetch_one(pool)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    Ok(attempts)
}

pub async fn clear_pull_failure(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    source_device_id: &str,
    source_seq: i64,
) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    ensure_sync_tables(pool).await?;
    sqlx::query("DELETE FROM sync_pull_failure WHERE source_device_id = ?1 AND source_seq = ?2")
        .bind(source_device_id)
        .bind(source_seq)
        .execute(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    Ok(())
}

pub async fn was_applied(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    source_device_id: &str,
    source_seq: i64,
) -> Result<bool, AppError> {
    let n: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar(
        "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM sync_applied WHERE source_device_id = ?1 AND source_seq = ?2",
    )
    .bind(source_device_id)
    .bind(source_seq)
    .fetch_one(pool)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    Ok(n > 0)
}

pub async fn mark_applied(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    source_device_id: &str,
    source_seq: i64,
) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    sqlx::query(
        "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO sync_applied (source_device_id, source_seq) VALUES (?1, ?2)",
    )
    .bind(source_device_id)
    .bind(source_seq)
    .execute(pool)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    Ok(())
}

pub async fn all_vectors(pool: &SqlitePool) -> Result<HashMap<String, i64>, AppError> {
    let rows = sqlx::query_as::<_, (String, i64)>(
        "SELECT device_id, seq FROM sync_vector ORDER BY device_id",
    )
    .fetch_all(pool)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    Ok(rows.into_iter().collect())
}

pub async fn status_snapshot(pool: &SqlitePool) -> Result<SyncStatusSnapshot, AppError> {
    ensure_sync_tables(pool).await?;
    let deployment = load_deployment(pool).await?;
    let local_device_id = ensure_local_device(pool, &deployment.device_label).await?;
    let local_seq: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar(
        "SELECT COALESCE((SELECT seq FROM sync_vector WHERE device_id = ?1), 0)",
    )
    .bind(&local_device_id)
    .fetch_one(pool)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    let pending_outbox: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar(
        "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM sync_outbox WHERE device_id = ?1 AND delivered_at IS NULL",
    )
    .bind(&local_device_id)
    .fetch_one(pool)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    let peers = list_peers(pool).await?;
    let vectors = all_vectors(pool).await?;
    Ok(SyncStatusSnapshot {
        local_device_id,
        deployment,
        local_seq,
        pending_outbox,
        peers,
        vectors,
        initial_copy: None,
    })
}

/// Refresh replica `last_seen_at` and LAN base URL after a successful push.
pub async fn touch_replica_seen(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    device_id: &str,
    peer_ip: &str,
    http_port: u16,
) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    ensure_sync_tables(pool).await?;
    let peer_base_url = crate::pairing::peer_base_url_from_ip(peer_ip, http_port);
    sqlx::query(
        "UPDATE sync_device SET last_seen_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
            peer_base_url = COALESCE(?1, peer_base_url)
         WHERE device_id = ?2",
    )
    .bind(peer_base_url)
    .bind(device_id)
    .execute(pool)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    Ok(())
}

pub async fn register_peer(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    device_id: &str,
    display_name: &str,
    role: DeviceRole,
    peer_base_url: Option<&str>,
    peer_cert_sha256: Option<&str>,
) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    register_device_row(
        pool,
        device_id,
        display_name,
        role,
        false,
        peer_base_url,
        peer_cert_sha256,
    )
    .await
}

pub async fn register_local_device(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    device_id: &str,
    display_name: &str,
    role: DeviceRole,
    peer_base_url: Option<&str>,
    peer_cert_sha256: Option<&str>,
) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    register_device_row(
        pool,
        device_id,
        display_name,
        role,
        true,
        peer_base_url,
        peer_cert_sha256,
    )
    .await
}

async fn register_device_row(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    device_id: &str,
    display_name: &str,
    role: DeviceRole,
    is_local: bool,
    peer_base_url: Option<&str>,
    peer_cert_sha256: Option<&str>,
) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    ensure_sync_tables(pool).await?;
    sqlx::query(
        "INSERT INTO sync_device (device_id, display_name, role, is_local, peer_base_url, peer_cert_sha256, last_seen_at)
         VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4, ?5, ?6, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP)
         ON CONFLICT(device_id) DO UPDATE SET
           display_name = excluded.display_name,
           role = excluded.role,
           is_local = excluded.is_local,
           peer_base_url = COALESCE(excluded.peer_base_url, sync_device.peer_base_url),
           peer_cert_sha256 = COALESCE(excluded.peer_cert_sha256, sync_device.peer_cert_sha256),
           last_seen_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP",
    )
    .bind(device_id)
    .bind(display_name)
    .bind(role.as_str())
    .bind(if is_local { 1 } else { 0 })
    .bind(peer_base_url)
    .bind(peer_cert_sha256)
    .execute(pool)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    sqlx::query("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO sync_vector (device_id, seq) VALUES (?1, 0)")
        .bind(device_id)
        .execute(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    Ok(())
}

async fn list_peers(pool: &SqlitePool) -> Result<Vec<SyncPeer>, AppError> {
    let rows = sqlx::query_as::<_, (String, String, String, Option<String>, Option<String>)>(
        "SELECT device_id, display_name, role, peer_base_url, last_seen_at
         FROM sync_device ORDER BY is_local DESC, display_name ASC",
    )
    .fetch_all(pool)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    Ok(rows
        .into_iter()
        .map(
            |(device_id, display_name, role, peer_base_url, last_seen_at)| SyncPeer {
                device_id,
                display_name,
                role,
                peer_base_url,
                last_seen_at,
            },
        )
        .collect())
}

//! Sync replication DTOs and allow-list constants.

use std::collections::HashMap;

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

use crate::deployment::SyncDeploymentConfig;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct OutboxEntry {
    pub id: String,
    pub device_id: String,
    pub seq: i64,
    pub entity_table: String,
    pub entity_id: String,
    pub op: String,
    pub payload_json: String,
    pub created_at: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct SyncPeer {
    pub device_id: String,
    pub display_name: String,
    pub role: String,
    pub peer_base_url: Option<String>,
    pub last_seen_at: Option<String>,
}

/// Live initial-copy banner for this device.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct InitialCopyStatus {
    /// `replica` while this device is copying, `master` while it is sending.
    pub side: String,
    pub device_label: String,
    pub done: i64,
    pub total: i64,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
pub struct SyncStatusSnapshot {
    pub local_device_id: String,
    pub deployment: SyncDeploymentConfig,
    pub local_seq: i64,
    pub pending_outbox: i64,
    pub peers: Vec<SyncPeer>,
    pub vectors: HashMap<String, i64>,
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub initial_copy: Option<InitialCopyStatus>,
}

    deny_column, is_synced_table, synced_tables,

FILE: src/schema.rs

use medoc_core::error::AppError;
use sqlx::SqlitePool;

/// Idempotent DDL for peer replication tables (practice `medoc.db` only).
pub async fn ensure_sync_tables(pool: &SqlitePool) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    if sync_schema_current(pool).await? {
        return Ok(());
    }
    ensure_sync_tables_cold(pool).await
}

async fn sync_schema_current(pool: &SqlitePool) -> Result<bool, AppError> {
    let ready: Option<(i64, i64, i64, i64, i64)> = sqlx::query_as(
        "SELECT
            (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM sqlite_master WHERE type = 'table' AND name = 'device_blocklist'),
            (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM pragma_table_info('sync_pull_failure') WHERE name = 'entry_id'),
            (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM pragma_table_info('pairing_request') WHERE name = 'transport'),
            (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM sqlite_master WHERE type = 'table' AND name = 'sync_row_hlc'),
            (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM sqlite_master WHERE type = 'table' AND name = 'sync_packet_queue')",
    )
    .fetch_optional(pool)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    Ok(matches!(
        ready,
        Some((tables, payload, transport, hlc, packets))
            if tables > 0 && payload > 0 && transport > 0 && hlc > 0 && packets > 0
    ))
}

async fn ensure_sync_tables_cold(pool: &SqlitePool) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    // Shared replication and pairing tables live in medoc-core. This function
    // only adds the tables the sync crate owns on top of that DDL.
    medoc_core::infrastructure::database::migrations::ensure_sync_replication_tables(pool).await?;

    sqlx::query(
        "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS sync_pull_failure (
            source_device_id TEXT NOT NULL,
            source_seq INTEGER NOT NULL,
            attempts INTEGER NOT NULL,
            PRIMARY KEY (source_device_id, source_seq)
        )",
    )
    .execute(pool)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    migrate_pull_failure_payload(pool).await?;

    medoc_core::infrastructure::database::hlc::ensure_sync_row_hlc(pool).await?;

    sqlx::query(
        "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS sync_watch_seen (
            entity_table TEXT NOT NULL,
            entity_id TEXT NOT NULL,
            stamp TEXT NOT NULL,
            PRIMARY KEY (entity_table, entity_id)
        )",
    )
    .execute(pool)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;

    migrate_pairing_pin_columns(pool).await?;

    medoc_core::infrastructure::database::migrations::ensure_cluster_tables(pool).await?;

    Ok(())
}

async fn migrate_pull_failure_payload(pool: &SqlitePool) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    for (name, ddl) in [
        (
            "payload_json",
            "ALTER TABLE sync_pull_failure ADD COLUMN payload_json TEXT",
        ),
        (
            "entity_table",
            "ALTER TABLE sync_pull_failure ADD COLUMN entity_table TEXT",
        ),
        (
            "entity_id",
            "ALTER TABLE sync_pull_failure ADD COLUMN entity_id TEXT",
        ),
        ("op", "ALTER TABLE sync_pull_failure ADD COLUMN op TEXT"),
        (
            "entry_created_at",
            "ALTER TABLE sync_pull_failure ADD COLUMN entry_created_at TEXT",
        ),
        (
            "entry_id",
            "ALTER TABLE sync_pull_failure ADD COLUMN entry_id TEXT",
        ),
    ] {
        let present: Option<i64> = sqlx::query_scalar(
            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM pragma_table_info('sync_pull_failure') WHERE name = ?1",
        )
        .bind(name)
        .fetch_one(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
        if present == Some(0) {
            sqlx::query(ddl)
                .execute(pool)
                .await
                .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::ensure_sync_tables;
    use medoc_core::infrastructure::database::connection::{run_migrations, test_memory_pool};

    async fn create_sql(pool: &sqlx::SqlitePool, name: &str) -> Option<String> {
        sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT sql FROM sqlite_master WHERE name = ?1")
            .bind(name)
            .fetch_optional(pool)
            .await
            .unwrap()
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn sync_extras_do_not_rewrite_the_core_replication_tables() {
        let pool = test_memory_pool().await.unwrap();
        run_migrations(&pool).await.unwrap();
        let before_outbox = create_sql(&pool, "sync_outbox").await;
        let before_pairing = create_sql(&pool, "pairing_request").await;
        let before_permission = create_sql(&pool, "slave_permission").await;
        assert!(
            before_outbox.is_some(),
            "core migration creates sync_outbox"
        );
        ensure_sync_tables(&pool).await.unwrap();
        assert_eq!(create_sql(&pool, "sync_outbox").await, before_outbox);
        assert_eq!(
            create_sql(&pool, "slave_permission").await,
            before_permission
        );
        let pairing = create_sql(&pool, "pairing_request").await.unwrap();
        assert!(
            pairing.starts_with(before_pairing.unwrap().trim_end_matches(')')),
            "pairing columns are added onto the core table"
        );
        assert!(pairing.contains("confirm_pin_hash"));
        assert!(pairing.contains("transport"));
        assert!(create_sql(&pool, "sync_pull_failure").await.is_some());
        assert!(create_sql(&pool, "sync_watch_seen").await.is_some());
        let transport: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar(
            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM pragma_table_info('pairing_request') WHERE name = 'transport'",
        )
        .fetch_one(&pool)
        .await
        .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(transport, 1);
    }
}

async fn migrate_pairing_pin_columns(pool: &SqlitePool) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    let has_hash: Option<i64> = sqlx::query_scalar(
        "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM pragma_table_info('pairing_request') WHERE name = 'confirm_pin_hash'",
    )
    .fetch_one(pool)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    if has_hash == Some(0) {
        sqlx::query("ALTER TABLE pairing_request ADD COLUMN confirm_pin_hash TEXT")
            .execute(pool)
            .await
            .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
        sqlx::query("ALTER TABLE pairing_request ADD COLUMN confirm_pin_expires_at TEXT")
            .execute(pool)
            .await
            .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
        sqlx::query(
            "ALTER TABLE pairing_request ADD COLUMN confirm_pin_attempts INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0",
        )
        .execute(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
        sqlx::query("ALTER TABLE pairing_request ADD COLUMN transport TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'lan'")
            .execute(pool)
            .await
            .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    }
    Ok(())
}

================================================================================
FILE: src/tls_pin.rs
================================================================================
//! HTTPS client that accepts a LAN certificate only when its SHA-256 matches
//! the fingerprint stored at pairing. An empty pin is a hard error.

use std::sync::Arc;
use std::time::Duration;

use rustls::client::danger::{HandshakeSignatureValid, ServerCertVerified, ServerCertVerifier};
use rustls::pki_types::{CertificateDer, ServerName, UnixTime};
use rustls::{DigitallySignedStruct, SignatureScheme};
use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};

use medoc_core::error::AppError;
use medoc_core::infrastructure::database::repos::admin::app_kv as app_kv_repo;
use sqlx::SqlitePool;

use crate::deployment::APP_KV_LAN_TLS_PIN;

pub fn certificate_pin_matches(der: &[u8], expected_hex: &str) -> bool {
    let digest = Sha256::digest(der);
    let actual: String = digest.iter().map(|b| format!("{b:02x}")).collect();
    actual == normalize_sha256_pin(expected_hex)
}

/// Lowercase hex only. Colons and spaces from a copied fingerprint are ignored.
pub fn normalize_sha256_pin(raw: &str) -> String {
    raw.chars()
        .filter(|c| c.is_ascii_hexdigit())
        .collect::<String>()
        .to_ascii_lowercase()
}

pub fn is_sha256_pin(raw: &str) -> bool {
    normalize_sha256_pin(raw).len() == 64
}

#[derive(Debug)]
struct PinVerifier {
    expected_hex: String,
}

impl ServerCertVerifier for PinVerifier {
    fn verify_server_cert(
        &self,
        end_entity: &CertificateDer<'_>,
        _intermediates: &[CertificateDer<'_>],
        _server_name: &ServerName<'_>,
        _ocsp_response: &[u8],
        _now: UnixTime,
    ) -> Result<ServerCertVerified, rustls::Error> {
        if certificate_pin_matches(end_entity.as_ref(), &self.expected_hex) {
            Ok(ServerCertVerified::assertion())
        } else {
            Err(rustls::Error::General("certificate pin mismatch".into()))
        }
    }

    fn verify_tls12_signature(
        &self,
        message: &[u8],
        cert: &CertificateDer<'_>,
        dss: &DigitallySignedStruct,
    ) -> Result<HandshakeSignatureValid, rustls::Error> {
        rustls::crypto::verify_tls12_signature(
            message,
            cert,
            dss,
            &rustls::crypto::aws_lc_rs::default_provider().signature_verification_algorithms,
        )
    }

    fn verify_tls13_signature(
        &self,
        message: &[u8],
        cert: &CertificateDer<'_>,
        dss: &DigitallySignedStruct,
    ) -> Result<HandshakeSignatureValid, rustls::Error> {
        rustls::crypto::verify_tls13_signature(
            message,
            cert,
            dss,
            &rustls::crypto::aws_lc_rs::default_provider().signature_verification_algorithms,
        )
    }

    fn supported_verify_schemes(&self) -> Vec<SignatureScheme> {
        rustls::crypto::aws_lc_rs::default_provider()
            .signature_verification_algorithms
            .supported_schemes()
    }
}

/// Remember this install's LAN certificate fingerprint so join can hand it to replicas.
pub async fn remember_local_pin(pool: &SqlitePool, fingerprint: &str) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    let pin = normalize_sha256_pin(fingerprint);
    if !is_sha256_pin(&pin) {
        return Err(AppError::Validation(
            "LAN TLS pin is not a SHA-256 fingerprint".into(),
        ));
    }
    app_kv_repo::set(pool, APP_KV_LAN_TLS_PIN, &pin).await
}

pub async fn load_local_pin(pool: &SqlitePool) -> Option<String> {
    let raw = app_kv_repo::get(pool, APP_KV_LAN_TLS_PIN).await.ok()??;
    let pin = normalize_sha256_pin(&raw);
    is_sha256_pin(&pin).then_some(pin)
}

/// Build a client that checks `cert_sha256` and rejects every other certificate.
pub fn pinned_http_client(
    cert_sha256: &str,
    timeout: Duration,
) -> Result<reqwest::Client, AppError> {
    let pin = normalize_sha256_pin(cert_sha256);
    if !is_sha256_pin(&pin) {
        return Err(AppError::Validation(
            "sync TLS pin missing — refusing to accept an unpinned certificate".into(),
        ));
    }
    let provider = Arc::new(rustls::crypto::aws_lc_rs::default_provider());
    let verifier = Arc::new(PinVerifier { expected_hex: pin });
    let config = rustls::ClientConfig::builder_with_provider(provider)
        .with_safe_default_protocol_versions()
        .map_err(|e| AppError::Internal(format!("tls versions: {e}")))?
        .dangerous()
        .with_custom_certificate_verifier(verifier)
        .with_no_client_auth();
    reqwest::Client::builder()
        .timeout(timeout)
        .use_preconfigured_tls(config)
        .build()
        .map_err(|e| AppError::Internal(format!("sync http client: {e}")))
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::{certificate_pin_matches, pinned_http_client};
    use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
    use std::time::Duration;

    #[test]
    fn pin_matches_the_certificate_bytes_only() {
        let der = b"practice-certificate";
        let digest = Sha256::digest(der);
        let hex: String = digest.iter().map(|b| format!("{b:02x}")).collect();
        assert!(certificate_pin_matches(der, &hex));
        assert!(certificate_pin_matches(der, &hex.to_ascii_uppercase()));
        assert!(!certificate_pin_matches(der, &"ab".repeat(32)));
    }

    #[test]
    fn colon_separated_fingerprint_is_a_pin() {
        let hex = "ab".repeat(32);
        let colon: String = hex
            .as_bytes()
            .chunks(2)
            .map(|pair| std::str::from_utf8(pair).unwrap())
            .collect::<Vec<_>>()
            .join(":");
        assert!(super::is_sha256_pin(&colon));
        assert!(pinned_http_client(&colon, Duration::from_secs(1)).is_ok());
    }

    #[test]
    fn empty_pin_is_refused() {
        let err = pinned_http_client("", Duration::from_secs(1)).unwrap_err();
        assert!(err.to_string().contains("unpinned"));
        assert!(pinned_http_client(&"ab".repeat(32), Duration::from_secs(1)).is_ok());
    }
}

================================================================================
FILE: src/watch.rs
================================================================================
//! Background scan of the tables the admin device and a joined user device share.
//!
//! Repository writes already append to `sync_outbox`. This scan catches a shared
//! row whose `updated_at` moved without that hook, queues it, and leaves the
//! caller to send the queue to the other device immediately.
//!
//! The first pass only records what is already on disk. Rows applied from the
//! other device are marked seen in [`note_remote_applied`] so they are not
//! queued back.

use std::collections::HashMap;

use medoc_core::error::AppError;
use sqlx::SqlitePool;

use crate::deployment::DeploymentMode;
use crate::repo::{self, is_synced_table, synced_tables};
use crate::schema::ensure_sync_tables;

const BASELINE_KEY: &str = "sync.watch.baseline.v1";
const HIGH_PREFIX: &str = "sync.watch.high.v1.";

/// How many shared rows changed since the last observation.
///
/// `0` when replication is off, or on the first pass (that pass only records
/// the current rows). A count above zero means the outbox now holds those
/// changes, or they were already waiting there from the write hook.
pub async fn queue_shared_changes(pool: &SqlitePool) -> Result<u32, AppError> {
    ensure_sync_tables(pool).await?;
    if !replication_active(pool).await? {
        return Ok(0);
    }
    if !baseline_done(pool).await? {
        remember_current(pool).await?;
        write_baseline(pool).await?;
        tracing::info!(target: "medoc::sync", event = "SHARED_SYNC_BASELINE");
        return Ok(0);
    }
    diff_and_queue(pool).await
}

/// Remember a row that just arrived from the other device so the next scan
/// does not queue it again.
pub async fn note_remote_applied(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    entity_table: &str,
    entity_id: &str,
) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    if !is_synced_table(entity_table) {
        return Ok(());
    }
    ensure_sync_tables(pool).await?;
    let Some(stamp) = row_stamp(pool, entity_table, entity_id).await? else {
        sqlx::query("DELETE FROM sync_watch_seen WHERE entity_table = ?1 AND entity_id = ?2")
            .bind(entity_table)
            .bind(entity_id)
            .execute(pool)
            .await
            .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
        return Ok(());
    };
    upsert_seen(pool, entity_table, entity_id, &stamp).await
}

async fn replication_active(pool: &SqlitePool) -> Result<bool, AppError> {
    let deployment = repo::load_deployment(pool).await.unwrap_or_default();
    if deployment.mode == DeploymentMode::ServerlessPeer {
        return Ok(true);
    }
    let joined: i64 =
        medoc_core::infrastructure::database::recorded_write::count_remote_replicas(pool)
            .await
            .unwrap_or(0);
    Ok(joined > 0)
}

async fn baseline_done(pool: &SqlitePool) -> Result<bool, AppError> {
    let value: Option<String> =
        medoc_core::infrastructure::database::repos::admin::app_kv::get(pool, BASELINE_KEY).await?;
    Ok(value.as_deref() == Some("1"))
}

async fn write_baseline(pool: &SqlitePool) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    sqlx::query(
        "INSERT INTO app_kv (key, value, updated_at) VALUES (?1, '1', CURRENT_TIMESTAMP)
         ON CONFLICT(key) DO UPDATE SET value = '1', updated_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP",
    )
    .bind(BASELINE_KEY)
    .execute(pool)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    Ok(())
}

async fn remember_current(pool: &SqlitePool) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    sqlx::query("DELETE FROM sync_watch_seen")
        .execute(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    for table in synced_tables() {
        let mut high = String::new();
        for (id, stamp) in list_stamps(pool, table).await? {
            if stamp > high {
                high.clone_from(&stamp);
            }
            upsert_seen(pool, table, &id, &stamp).await?;
        }
        store_watermark(pool, table, &high).await?;
    }
    Ok(())
}

async fn diff_and_queue(pool: &SqlitePool) -> Result<u32, AppError> {
    let mut changes = 0u32;
    for table in synced_tables() {
        let watermark = watermark(pool, table).await?;
        let mut high = watermark.clone();
        for (id, stamp) in list_changed_stamps(pool, table, &watermark).await? {
            if stamp > high {
                high.clone_from(&stamp);
            }
            let seen = seen_stamp(pool, table, &id).await?;
            if seen.as_deref() == Some(stamp.as_str()) {
                continue;
            }
            changes += 1;
            if let Some(payload) = repo::load_row_payload_json(pool, table, &id).await? {
                repo::sync_record_or_noop(pool, table, &id, "UPDATE", &payload).await?;
            }
            upsert_seen(pool, table, &id, &stamp).await?;
        }
        if high != watermark {
            store_watermark(pool, table, &high).await?;
        }
        for id in missing_seen_ids(pool, table).await? {
            changes += 1;
            repo::sync_record_or_noop(pool, table, &id, "DELETE", "{}").await?;
            sqlx::query("DELETE FROM sync_watch_seen WHERE entity_table = ?1 AND entity_id = ?2")
                .bind(table)
                .bind(&id)
                .execute(pool)
                .await
                .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
        }
    }
    Ok(changes)
}

fn watermark_key(table: &str) -> String {
    format!("{HIGH_PREFIX}{table}")
}

async fn watermark(pool: &SqlitePool, table: &str) -> Result<String, AppError> {
    let value = medoc_core::infrastructure::database::repos::admin::app_kv::get(
        pool,
        &watermark_key(table),
    )
    .await?;
    Ok(value.unwrap_or_default())
}

async fn store_watermark(pool: &SqlitePool, table: &str, stamp: &str) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    medoc_core::infrastructure::database::repos::admin::app_kv::set(
        pool,
        &watermark_key(table),
        stamp,
    )
    .await
}

async fn seen_stamp(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    table: &str,
    entity_id: &str,
) -> Result<Option<String>, AppError> {
    let stamp: Option<String> = sqlx::query_scalar(
        "SELECT stamp FROM sync_watch_seen WHERE entity_table = ?1 AND entity_id = ?2",
    )
    .bind(table)
    .bind(entity_id)
    .fetch_optional(pool)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    Ok(stamp)
}

async fn stamp_select(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    table: &str,
) -> Result<Option<(String, String)>, AppError> {
    let cols: Vec<String> =
        sqlx::query_scalar(&format!("SELECT name FROM pragma_table_info('{table}')"))
            .fetch_all(pool)
            .await
            .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    if cols.is_empty() {
        return Ok(None);
    }
    let key = medoc_core::infrastructure::database::sync_registry::key_expr(table);
    if medoc_core::infrastructure::database::sync_registry::key_is_column(table) {
        let id_col = medoc_core::infrastructure::database::sync_registry::id_column(table);
        if !cols.iter().any(|c| c == id_col) {
            return Ok(None);
        }
    }
    let stamp_sql = if cols.iter().any(|c| c == "updated_at") {
        "COALESCE(CAST(updated_at AS TEXT), '')".to_string()
    } else if cols.iter().any(|c| c == "created_at") {
        "COALESCE(CAST(created_at AS TEXT), '')".to_string()
    } else {
        "''".to_string()
    };
    Ok(Some((key, stamp_sql)))
}

async fn list_stamps(pool: &SqlitePool, table: &str) -> Result<HashMap<String, String>, AppError> {
    if !is_synced_table(table) {
        return Ok(HashMap::new());
    }
    let Some((key, stamp_sql)) = stamp_select(pool, table).await? else {
        return Ok(HashMap::new());
    };
    let mut from_sql = format!("\"{table}\"");
    if table == "app_kv" {
        from_sql.push_str(
            " WHERE key NOT LIKE 'sync.%'
               AND key NOT LIKE 'license.%'
               AND key NOT LIKE 'pairing.%'
               AND key NOT LIKE 'onboarding.%'
               AND key NOT LIKE 'cluster.%'",
        );
    }
    let sql = format!("SELECT CAST({key} AS TEXT), {stamp_sql} FROM {from_sql}");
    let rows: Vec<(String, String)> = sqlx::query_as(&sql)
        .fetch_all(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    Ok(rows.into_iter().collect())
}

/// Rows that are new, or whose `updated_at` moved past the last scan.
/// Tables without `updated_at` still return every stamp.
async fn list_changed_stamps(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    table: &str,
    watermark: &str,
) -> Result<HashMap<String, String>, AppError> {
    if !is_synced_table(table) {
        return Ok(HashMap::new());
    }
    let Some((key, stamp_sql)) = stamp_select(pool, table).await? else {
        return Ok(HashMap::new());
    };
    let cols: Vec<String> =
        sqlx::query_scalar(&format!("SELECT name FROM pragma_table_info('{table}')"))
            .fetch_all(pool)
            .await
            .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    if !cols.iter().any(|c| c == "updated_at") {
        return list_stamps(pool, table).await;
    }
    let from_sql = format!("\"{table}\"");
    let mut filter = String::new();
    if table == "app_kv" {
        filter.push_str(
            " key NOT LIKE 'sync.%'
               AND key NOT LIKE 'license.%'
               AND key NOT LIKE 'pairing.%'
               AND key NOT LIKE 'onboarding.%'
               AND key NOT LIKE 'cluster.%'
               AND ",
        );
    }
    let sql = format!(
        "SELECT CAST({key} AS TEXT), {stamp_sql} FROM {from_sql}
         WHERE {filter}(
            CAST(updated_at AS TEXT) > ?1
            OR CAST({key} AS TEXT) NOT IN (
                SELECT entity_id FROM sync_watch_seen WHERE entity_table = ?2
            )
         )"
    );
    let rows: Vec<(String, String)> = sqlx::query_as(&sql)
        .bind(watermark)
        .bind(table)
        .fetch_all(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    Ok(rows.into_iter().collect())
}

async fn missing_seen_ids(pool: &SqlitePool, table: &str) -> Result<Vec<String>, AppError> {
    if !is_synced_table(table) {
        return Ok(Vec::new());
    }
    let Some((key, _)) = stamp_select(pool, table).await? else {
        return Ok(Vec::new());
    };
    let sql = format!(
        "SELECT entity_id FROM sync_watch_seen
         WHERE entity_table = ?1
           AND entity_id NOT IN (SELECT CAST({key} AS TEXT) FROM \"{table}\")"
    );
    let ids: Vec<String> = sqlx::query_scalar(&sql)
        .bind(table)
        .fetch_all(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    Ok(ids)
}

async fn row_stamp(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    table: &str,
    entity_id: &str,
) -> Result<Option<String>, AppError> {
    if !is_synced_table(table) {
        return Ok(None);
    }
    let Some((key, stamp_sql)) = stamp_select(pool, table).await? else {
        return Ok(None);
    };
    let sql = format!("SELECT {stamp_sql} FROM \"{table}\" WHERE {key} = ?1");
    let stamp: Option<String> = sqlx::query_scalar(&sql)
        .bind(entity_id)
        .fetch_optional(pool)
        .await
        .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    Ok(stamp)
}

async fn upsert_seen(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    table: &str,
    entity_id: &str,
    stamp: &str,
) -> Result<(), AppError> {
    sqlx::query(
        "INSERT INTO sync_watch_seen (entity_table, entity_id, stamp) VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3)
         ON CONFLICT(entity_table, entity_id) DO UPDATE SET stamp = excluded.stamp",
    )
    .bind(table)
    .bind(entity_id)
    .bind(stamp)
    .execute(pool)
    .await
    .map_err(AppError::Database)?;
    Ok(())
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::deployment::{DeploymentMode, DeviceRole, SyncDeploymentConfig};
    use crate::schema::ensure_sync_tables;
    use medoc_core::infrastructure::database::connection::{run_migrations, test_memory_pool};

    async fn enable_peer(pool: &SqlitePool) {
        let mut cfg = SyncDeploymentConfig::default();
        cfg.mode = DeploymentMode::ServerlessPeer;
        cfg.role = DeviceRole::Replica;
        cfg.device_label = "watch-test".into();
        repo::save_deployment(pool, &cfg).await.unwrap();
    }

    async fn outbox_ops(pool: &SqlitePool, id: &str) -> Vec<String> {
        sqlx::query_scalar(
            "SELECT op FROM sync_outbox WHERE entity_table = 'app_kv' AND entity_id = ?1 ORDER BY seq",
        )
        .bind(id)
        .fetch_all(pool)
        .await
        .unwrap()
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn queues_a_shared_row_once_then_a_later_edit_and_a_delete() {
        let pool = test_memory_pool().await.unwrap();
        run_migrations(&pool).await.unwrap();
        ensure_sync_tables(&pool).await.unwrap();
        enable_peer(&pool).await;

        assert_eq!(queue_shared_changes(&pool).await.unwrap(), 0);

        sqlx::query(
            "INSERT INTO app_kv (key, value, updated_at) VALUES ('practice.watch.test', 'a', '2026-01-01T00:00:00Z')",
        )
        .execute(&pool)
        .await
        .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(queue_shared_changes(&pool).await.unwrap(), 1);
        assert_eq!(queue_shared_changes(&pool).await.unwrap(), 0);
        assert_eq!(
            outbox_ops(&pool, "practice.watch.test").await,
            vec!["UPDATE".to_string()]
        );

        sqlx::query(
            "UPDATE app_kv SET value = 'b', updated_at = '2026-01-02T00:00:00Z' WHERE key = 'practice.watch.test'",
        )
        .execute(&pool)
        .await
        .unwrap();
        note_remote_applied(&pool, "app_kv", "practice.watch.test")
            .await
            .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(queue_shared_changes(&pool).await.unwrap(), 0);

        sqlx::query("DELETE FROM app_kv WHERE key = 'practice.watch.test'")
            .execute(&pool)
            .await
            .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(queue_shared_changes(&pool).await.unwrap(), 1);
        assert_eq!(
            outbox_ops(&pool, "practice.watch.test").await,
            vec!["UPDATE".to_string(), "DELETE".to_string()]
        );
    }

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn stays_quiet_when_replication_is_off() {
        let pool = test_memory_pool().await.unwrap();
        run_migrations(&pool).await.unwrap();
        ensure_sync_tables(&pool).await.unwrap();
        sqlx::query(
            "INSERT INTO app_kv (key, value, updated_at) VALUES ('practice.watch.off', 'a', '2026-01-01T00:00:00Z')",
        )
        .execute(&pool)
        .await
        .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(queue_shared_changes(&pool).await.unwrap(), 0);
        assert!(outbox_ops(&pool, "practice.watch.off").await.is_empty());
    }
}

================================================================================
