//! Loopback integration: TCP listener bind + Noise XX handshake + JOIN_REQUEST.

use std::net::IpAddr;

use chrono::Utc;
use medoc_core::infrastructure::database::connection::{run_migrations, test_memory_pool};
use medoc_core::infrastructure::database::repos::admin::audit::init_audit_hmac_key;
use medoc_sync::cluster::crypto::{derive_sas_from_transcript, hash_sas, DeviceIdentity};
use medoc_sync::cluster::services::{
    list_pending_requests, mirror_join_session, store_join_admin_endpoint, submit_sas,
    PairingHandle,
};
use medoc_sync::cluster::SeatRole;
use medoc_sync::cluster::{License, PairingStatus, SqliteClusterRepos};
use medoc_sync::net::{
    bind_cluster_listener, complete_xx_handshake, confirm_sas_on_admin, fetch_staff_directory,
    generate_keypair, handle_join_connection, join_admin_endpoint, run_xx_initiator,
    run_xx_responder, spawn_cluster_connection_handler,
};
use medoc_sync::schema::ensure_sync_tables;
use tokio::net::TcpStream;

async fn fresh_pool() -> sqlx::SqlitePool {
    let audit_dir =
        std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("medoc-cluster-test-{}", uuid::Uuid::new_v4()));
    std::fs::create_dir_all(&audit_dir).expect("audit dir");
    init_audit_hmac_key(&audit_dir).expect("audit key");
    let pool = test_memory_pool().await.expect("pool");
    run_migrations(&pool).await.expect("migrate");
    ensure_sync_tables(&pool).await.expect("schema");
    pool
}

#[tokio::test]
#[serial_test::serial]
async fn cluster_listener_binds_private_loopback() {
    let addr: IpAddr = "127.0.0.1".parse().unwrap();
    let listener = bind_cluster_listener(addr, 0).await.expect("bind");
    assert!(listener.local_addr().unwrap().port() > 0);
}

#[test]
fn noise_xx_produces_shared_transcript() {
    let kp1 = generate_keypair().expect("kp1");
    let kp2 = generate_keypair().expect("kp2");
    let (_t1, _t2, transcript) = complete_xx_handshake(&kp1, &kp2).expect("hs");
    assert!(!transcript.is_empty());
}

#[tokio::test]
#[serial_test::serial]
async fn xx_handshake_over_tcp_matches_transcript() {
    let listener = tokio::net::TcpListener::bind("127.0.0.1:0")
        .await
        .expect("bind");
    let addr = listener.local_addr().expect("addr");
    let server = tokio::spawn(async move {
        let (mut stream, _) = listener.accept().await.expect("accept");
        run_xx_responder(&mut stream).await.expect("responder")
    });
    let mut client = TcpStream::connect(addr).await.expect("connect");
    let (_t1, tr1) = run_xx_initiator(&mut client).await.expect("initiator");
    let (_t2, tr2) = server.await.expect("server");
    assert_eq!(tr1, tr2);
}

#[tokio::test]
#[serial_test::serial]
async fn join_request_accept_over_loopback() {
    std::env::set_var(
        "MEDOC_CLUSTER_DEVICE_SECRET",
        "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef",
    );

    let pool = fresh_pool().await;
    let addr: IpAddr = "127.0.0.1".parse().unwrap();
    let listener = bind_cluster_listener(addr, 0).await.expect("bind");
    let port = listener.local_addr().unwrap().port();
    let pool_clone = pool.clone();

    let server = tokio::spawn(async move {
        let (stream, _) = listener.accept().await.expect("accept");
        handle_join_connection(stream, pool_clone).await;
    });

    let identity = DeviceIdentity::load_or_create().expect("identity");
    let outcome = join_admin_endpoint("127.0.0.1", port, &identity, SeatRole::Member)
        .await
        .expect("join");
    server.await.expect("server done");

    assert!(!outcome.session_id.is_empty());
    assert!(!outcome.handshake_transcript.is_empty());

    let pending = list_pending_requests(&pool).await.expect("pending");
    assert_eq!(pending.len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(pending[0].fingerprint, identity.fingerprint);

    let stored_b64: Option<String> =
        sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT handshake_transcript_b64 FROM pairing_request WHERE id = ?1")
            .bind(&pending[0].id)
            .fetch_optional(&pool)
            .await
            .expect("select transcript");
    let stored = stored_b64.filter(|s| !s.trim().is_empty()).map(|s| {
        base64::Engine::decode(&base64::engine::general_purpose::STANDARD, s.trim())
            .expect("decode stored transcript")
    });
    assert_eq!(
        stored.as_deref(),
        Some(outcome.handshake_transcript.as_slice()),
        "admin must persist the same Noise transcript the joiner uses for SAS"
    );
    assert_eq!(
        derive_sas_from_transcript(stored.as_ref().unwrap()),
        derive_sas_from_transcript(&outcome.handshake_transcript)
    );

    std::env::remove_var("MEDOC_CLUSTER_DEVICE_SECRET");
}

#[tokio::test]
#[serial_test::serial]
async fn replica_sas_uses_stored_transcript_not_ipc_garbage() {
    std::env::set_var(
        "MEDOC_CLUSTER_DEVICE_SECRET",
        "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef",
    );

    let admin_pool = fresh_pool().await;
    let replica_pool = fresh_pool().await;
    let addr: IpAddr = "127.0.0.1".parse().unwrap();
    let listener = bind_cluster_listener(addr, 0).await.expect("bind");
    let port = listener.local_addr().unwrap().port();
    let pool_clone = admin_pool.clone();

    let server = tokio::spawn(async move {
        let (stream, _) = listener.accept().await.expect("accept");
        handle_join_connection(stream, pool_clone).await;
    });

    let identity = DeviceIdentity::load_or_create().expect("identity");
    let outcome = join_admin_endpoint("127.0.0.1", port, &identity, SeatRole::Member)
        .await
        .expect("join");
    server.await.expect("server done");

    mirror_join_session(
        &replica_pool,
        &outcome.session_id,
        &outcome.fingerprint,
        SeatRole::Member,
        &outcome.handshake_transcript,
    )
    .await
    .expect("mirror");

    let digits = derive_sas_from_transcript(&outcome.handshake_transcript);
    let handle = PairingHandle {
        session_id: outcome.session_id,
        fingerprint: outcome.fingerprint,
    };
    let wrong_ipc = vec![0xAAu8; 64];
    submit_sas(&replica_pool, "joiner", &handle, &digits, &wrong_ipc)
        .await
        .expect(
            "SAS must follow the join transcript stored in SQLite, not a corrupted IPC payload",
        );

    std::env::remove_var("MEDOC_CLUSTER_DEVICE_SECRET");
}

async fn save_admin_license(pool: &sqlx::SqlitePool) -> Vec<u8> {
    let signing_key = vec![7u8; 32];
    let repos = SqliteClusterRepos { pool };
    repos
        .save(&License {
            cluster_id: "c-sas".into(),
            license_ref: "test".into(),
            signing_key_enc: signing_key.clone(),
            max_total: 10,
            max_admin: 3,
            max_member: 7,
            activated_at: Utc::now(),
        })
        .await
        .expect("license");
    signing_key
}

async fn arm_admin_sas(pool: &sqlx::SqlitePool, session_id: &str, digits: &str) {
    save_admin_license(pool).await;
    mark_admin_accepted(pool, session_id, digits).await;
}

/// What `accept_join_request` does on the admin: arm the SAS hash
/// (AWAITING_SAS). License must already be saved (`save_admin_license`).
async fn mark_admin_accepted(pool: &sqlx::SqlitePool, session_id: &str, digits: &str) {
    let signing_key = vec![7u8; 32];
    let sas_hash = hash_sas(session_id, digits, &signing_key);
    SqliteClusterRepos { pool }
        .update_state(session_id, PairingStatus::AwaitingSas, Some(&sas_hash))
        .await
        .expect("awaiting sas");
}

#[tokio::test]
#[serial_test::serial]
async fn sas_confirm_via_spawned_cluster_handler() {
    std::env::set_var(
        "MEDOC_CLUSTER_DEVICE_SECRET",
        "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef",
    );

    let admin_pool = fresh_pool().await;
    let replica_pool = fresh_pool().await;
    let addr: IpAddr = "127.0.0.1".parse().unwrap();
    let listener = bind_cluster_listener(addr, 0).await.expect("bind");
    let port = listener.local_addr().unwrap().port();
    let pool_clone = admin_pool.clone();
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        loop {
            match listener.accept().await {
                Ok((stream, _)) => spawn_cluster_connection_handler(stream, pool_clone.clone()),
                Err(_) => break,
            }
        }
    });

    let identity = DeviceIdentity::load_or_create().expect("identity");
    let outcome = join_admin_endpoint("127.0.0.1", port, &identity, SeatRole::Member)
        .await
        .expect("join");
    let digits = derive_sas_from_transcript(&outcome.handshake_transcript);
    arm_admin_sas(&admin_pool, &outcome.session_id, &digits).await;

    confirm_sas_on_admin("127.0.0.1", port, &identity, &outcome.session_id, &digits)
        .await
        .expect("admin hash must accept the displayed SAS");
    let bad = confirm_sas_on_admin("127.0.0.1", port, &identity, &outcome.session_id, "0000").await;
    assert!(bad.is_err(), "wrong digits must fail remote SAS");

    mirror_join_session(
        &replica_pool,
        &outcome.session_id,
        &outcome.fingerprint,
        SeatRole::Member,
        &vec![0xAAu8; 64],
    )
    .await
    .expect("mirror garbage transcript");
    store_join_admin_endpoint(&replica_pool, "127.0.0.1", port)
        .await
        .expect("endpoint");
    let handle = PairingHandle {
        session_id: outcome.session_id,
        fingerprint: outcome.fingerprint,
    };
    submit_sas(&replica_pool, "joiner", &handle, &digits, &[])
        .await
        .expect("replica must accept the admin-shown SAS even if local transcript differs");

    std::env::remove_var("MEDOC_CLUSTER_DEVICE_SECRET");
}

#[tokio::test]
#[serial_test::serial]
async fn sas_confirm_rejected_without_admin_accept() {
    std::env::set_var(
        "MEDOC_CLUSTER_DEVICE_SECRET",
        "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef",
    );

    let admin_pool = fresh_pool().await;
    save_admin_license(&admin_pool).await;
    let addr: IpAddr = "127.0.0.1".parse().unwrap();
    let listener = bind_cluster_listener(addr, 0).await.expect("bind");
    let port = listener.local_addr().unwrap().port();
    let pool_clone = admin_pool.clone();
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        loop {
            match listener.accept().await {
                Ok((stream, _)) => spawn_cluster_connection_handler(stream, pool_clone.clone()),
                Err(_) => break,
            }
        }
    });

    let identity = DeviceIdentity::load_or_create().expect("identity");
    let outcome = join_admin_endpoint("127.0.0.1", port, &identity, SeatRole::Member)
        .await
        .expect("join");
    let digits = derive_sas_from_transcript(&outcome.handshake_transcript);
    // The joiner can always derive the SAS from its own transcript. Before
    // the admin accepted, that must not enrol it or hand out a token.
    let res =
        confirm_sas_on_admin("127.0.0.1", port, &identity, &outcome.session_id, &digits).await;
    assert!(res.is_err(), "SAS confirm must require the admin's Accept");
    let status: Option<String> =
        sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT status FROM pairing_request WHERE id = ?1")
            .bind(&outcome.session_id)
            .fetch_optional(&admin_pool)
            .await
            .expect("status");
    assert_ne!(status.as_deref(), Some("ACCEPTED"));
    let active: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar(
        "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM sync_device WHERE fingerprint = ?1 AND device_status = 'ACTIVE'",
    )
    .bind(&outcome.fingerprint)
    .fetch_one(&admin_pool)
    .await
    .expect("count");
    assert_eq!(active, 0, "joiner must not be enrolled without Accept");

    // Staff directory is not available to an unaccepted joiner either.
    let staff = fetch_staff_directory("127.0.0.1", port, &identity, &outcome.session_id).await;
    assert!(
        staff.is_err(),
        "staff directory must require an accepted session"
    );

    std::env::remove_var("MEDOC_CLUSTER_DEVICE_SECRET");
}

#[tokio::test]
#[serial_test::serial]
async fn sas_confirm_rejected_while_still_join_requested() {
    std::env::set_var(
        "MEDOC_CLUSTER_DEVICE_SECRET",
        "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef",
    );

    let admin_pool = fresh_pool().await;
    let addr: IpAddr = "127.0.0.1".parse().unwrap();
    let listener = bind_cluster_listener(addr, 0).await.expect("bind");
    let port = listener.local_addr().unwrap().port();
    let pool_clone = admin_pool.clone();
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        loop {
            match listener.accept().await {
                Ok((stream, _)) => spawn_cluster_connection_handler(stream, pool_clone.clone()),
                Err(_) => break,
            }
        }
    });

    let identity = DeviceIdentity::load_or_create().expect("identity");
    let outcome = join_admin_endpoint("127.0.0.1", port, &identity, SeatRole::Member)
        .await
        .expect("join");
    save_admin_license(&admin_pool).await;
    let digits = derive_sas_from_transcript(&outcome.handshake_transcript);
    let res =
        confirm_sas_on_admin("127.0.0.1", port, &identity, &outcome.session_id, &digits).await;
    assert!(
        res.is_err(),
        "JOIN_REQUESTED (not accepted) must not confirm"
    );

    std::env::remove_var("MEDOC_CLUSTER_DEVICE_SECRET");
}

#[tokio::test]
#[serial_test::serial]
async fn sas_confirm_uses_transcript_when_stored_hash_is_wrong() {
    std::env::set_var(
        "MEDOC_CLUSTER_DEVICE_SECRET",
        "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef",
    );

    let admin_pool = fresh_pool().await;
    let signing_key = save_admin_license(&admin_pool).await;
    let addr: IpAddr = "127.0.0.1".parse().unwrap();
    let listener = bind_cluster_listener(addr, 0).await.expect("bind");
    let port = listener.local_addr().unwrap().port();
    let pool_clone = admin_pool.clone();
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        loop {
            match listener.accept().await {
                Ok((stream, _)) => spawn_cluster_connection_handler(stream, pool_clone.clone()),
                Err(_) => break,
            }
        }
    });

    let identity = DeviceIdentity::load_or_create().expect("identity");
    let outcome = join_admin_endpoint("127.0.0.1", port, &identity, SeatRole::Member)
        .await
        .expect("join");
    let digits = derive_sas_from_transcript(&outcome.handshake_transcript);
    let wrong_hash = hash_sas(&outcome.session_id, "0000", &signing_key);
    SqliteClusterRepos { pool: &admin_pool }
        .update_state(
            &outcome.session_id,
            PairingStatus::AwaitingSas,
            Some(&wrong_hash),
        )
        .await
        .expect("stale hash");
    confirm_sas_on_admin("127.0.0.1", port, &identity, &outcome.session_id, &digits)
        .await
        .expect("displayed/handshake SAS must win over a stale HMAC");

    std::env::remove_var("MEDOC_CLUSTER_DEVICE_SECRET");
}

#[tokio::test]
#[serial_test::serial]
async fn staff_directory_and_lan_token_after_sas() {
    std::env::set_var(
        "MEDOC_CLUSTER_DEVICE_SECRET",
        "fedcba9876543210fedcba9876543210fedcba9876543210fedcba9876543210",
    );
    std::env::set_var(
        "MEDOC_PAIRING_MASTER_SECRET",
        "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef",
    );

    let admin_pool = fresh_pool().await;
    save_admin_license(&admin_pool).await;

    let addr: IpAddr = "127.0.0.1".parse().unwrap();
    let listener = bind_cluster_listener(addr, 0).await.expect("bind");
    let port = listener.local_addr().unwrap().port();
    let pool_clone = admin_pool.clone();
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        loop {
            match listener.accept().await {
                Ok((stream, _)) => spawn_cluster_connection_handler(stream, pool_clone.clone()),
                Err(_) => break,
            }
        }
    });

    let identity = DeviceIdentity::load_or_create().expect("identity");
    let outcome = join_admin_endpoint("127.0.0.1", port, &identity, SeatRole::Member)
        .await
        .expect("join");
    let digits = derive_sas_from_transcript(&outcome.handshake_transcript);
    mark_admin_accepted(&admin_pool, &outcome.session_id, &digits).await;
    let lan = confirm_sas_on_admin("127.0.0.1", port, &identity, &outcome.session_id, &digits)
        .await
        .expect("sas");
    assert!(
        lan.activation_token
            .as_deref()
            .unwrap_or("")
            .starts_with("mt2."),
        "SAS confirm must return a master activation token"
    );

    let json = fetch_staff_directory("127.0.0.1", port, &identity, &outcome.session_id)
        .await
        .expect("staff directory after SAS");
    assert!(
        json.contains("staff") || json.contains('{'),
        "owner must return a staff directory payload, got {json}"
    );

    std::env::remove_var("MEDOC_CLUSTER_DEVICE_SECRET");
    std::env::remove_var("MEDOC_PAIRING_MASTER_SECRET");
}

#[tokio::test]
#[serial_test::serial]
async fn sas_brute_force_is_locked_after_five_attempts() {
    std::env::set_var(
        "MEDOC_CLUSTER_DEVICE_SECRET",
        "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef",
    );

    let admin_pool = fresh_pool().await;
    let addr: IpAddr = "127.0.0.1".parse().unwrap();
    let listener = bind_cluster_listener(addr, 0).await.expect("bind");
    let port = listener.local_addr().unwrap().port();
    let pool_clone = admin_pool.clone();
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        loop {
            match listener.accept().await {
                Ok((stream, _)) => spawn_cluster_connection_handler(stream, pool_clone.clone()),
                Err(_) => break,
            }
        }
    });

    let identity = DeviceIdentity::load_or_create().expect("identity");
    let outcome = join_admin_endpoint("127.0.0.1", port, &identity, SeatRole::Member)
        .await
        .expect("join");
    let digits = derive_sas_from_transcript(&outcome.handshake_transcript);
    arm_admin_sas(&admin_pool, &outcome.session_id, &digits).await;

    let wrong = if digits == "0000" { "1111" } else { "0000" };
    for _ in 0..5 {
        assert!(
            confirm_sas_on_admin("127.0.0.1", port, &identity, &outcome.session_id, wrong)
                .await
                .is_err()
        );
    }
    assert!(
        confirm_sas_on_admin("127.0.0.1", port, &identity, &outcome.session_id, &digits)
            .await
            .is_err(),
        "after 5 wrong entries even the right SAS must be refused"
    );

    std::env::remove_var("MEDOC_CLUSTER_DEVICE_SECRET");
}

================================================================================
FILE: tests/engine_http_tests.rs
================================================================================
//! HTTP-path tests for `SyncEngine` push/pull/mesh (T-U1) using wiremock.

use ed25519_dalek::SigningKey;
use medoc_core::infrastructure::database::connection::{run_migrations, test_memory_pool};
use medoc_sync::deployment::{DeploymentMode, DeviceRole, SyncDeploymentConfig};
use medoc_sync::engine::SyncEngine;
use medoc_sync::master_keys;
use medoc_sync::repo::{
    append_outbox, ensure_local_device, list_pending_outbox, mark_applied, save_deployment,
};

async fn deploy(
    pool: &sqlx::SqlitePool,
    cfg: &SyncDeploymentConfig,
) -> Result<(), medoc_core::error::AppError> {
    let mut cfg = cfg.clone();
    if cfg.master_cert_sha256.is_empty() {
        cfg.master_cert_sha256 = "ab".repeat(32);
    }
    save_deployment(pool, &cfg).await
}

async fn seed_peer_tls_pin(pool: &sqlx::SqlitePool, device_id: &str) {
    sqlx::query(
        "INSERT INTO sync_device (device_id, display_name, role, is_local, peer_cert_sha256)
         VALUES (?1, ?1, 'REPLICA', 0, ?2)
         ON CONFLICT(device_id) DO UPDATE SET peer_cert_sha256 = excluded.peer_cert_sha256",
    )
    .bind(device_id)
    .bind("ab".repeat(32))
    .execute(pool)
    .await
    .expect("peer pin");
}
use medoc_sync::schema::ensure_sync_tables;
use wiremock::matchers::{method, path};
use wiremock::{Mock, MockServer, ResponseTemplate};

async fn fresh_pool() -> sqlx::SqlitePool {
    let pool = test_memory_pool().await.expect("pool");
    run_migrations(&pool).await.expect("migrate");
    ensure_sync_tables(&pool).await.expect("sync schema");
    pool
}

async fn save_replica(pool: &sqlx::SqlitePool, master_url: &str, token: &str, label: &str) {
    deploy(
        pool,
        &SyncDeploymentConfig {
            schema_version: 1,
            mode: DeploymentMode::ServerlessPeer,
            role: DeviceRole::Replica,
            master_base_url: master_url.into(),
            master_access_token: token.into(),
            master_cert_sha256: "ab".repeat(32),
            device_label: label.into(),
            ..Default::default()
        },
    )
    .await
    .expect("deployment");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn push_to_master_empty_outbox_skips_http() {
    let pool = fresh_pool().await;
    let server = MockServer::start().await;
    save_replica(&pool, &server.uri(), "tok", "empty-push").await;
    let result = SyncEngine::push_to_master(&pool).await.expect("push");
    assert_eq!(result.accepted, 0);
    assert_eq!(
        server
            .received_requests()
            .await
            .as_ref()
            .map(|r| r.len())
            .unwrap_or(0),
        0
    );
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn push_to_master_delivers_pending_and_clears_outbox() {
    let pool = fresh_pool().await;
    let server = MockServer::start().await;
    save_replica(&pool, &server.uri(), "tok-push", "push-test").await;
    let device = ensure_local_device(&pool, "push-test").await.unwrap();
    append_outbox(
        &pool,
        &device,
        "app_kv",
        "http.push.key",
        "INSERT",
        r#"{"key":"http.push.key","value":"42"}"#,
    )
    .await
    .expect("outbox");

    Mock::given(method("POST"))
        .and(path("/api/v1/sync/push"))
        .respond_with(ResponseTemplate::new(200).set_body_json(serde_json::json!({
            "accepted": 1,
            "lastSeq": 1
        })))
        .mount(&server)
        .await;

    let result = SyncEngine::push_to_master(&pool).await.expect("push ok");
    assert_eq!(result.accepted, 1);
    assert_eq!(result.last_seq, 1);
    assert!(list_pending_outbox(&pool, &device, 500)
        .await
        .unwrap()
        .is_empty());
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn pull_from_master_applies_remote_app_kv() {
    let pool = fresh_pool().await;
    let server = MockServer::start().await;
    save_replica(&pool, &server.uri(), "tok-pull", "pull-test").await;
    let _device = ensure_local_device(&pool, "pull-test").await.unwrap();

    let remote_entry = serde_json::json!({
        "id": "pull-e1",
        "deviceId": "master-device",
        "seq": 1,
        "entityTable": "app_kv",
        "entityId": "pull.key",
        "op": "INSERT",
        "payloadJson": r#"{"key":"pull.key","value":"from-master"}"#,
        "createdAt": "2099-01-01T00:00:00Z"
    });

    Mock::given(method("POST"))
        .and(path("/api/v1/sync/pull"))
        .respond_with(ResponseTemplate::new(200).set_body_json(serde_json::json!({
            "entries": [remote_entry]
        })))
        .mount(&server)
        .await;

    // Seed master device id for vector lookup
    sqlx::query(
        "INSERT INTO sync_device (device_id, display_name, role, is_local)
         VALUES ('master-device', 'Master', 'MASTER', 0)",
    )
    .execute(&pool)
    .await
    .expect("master row");

    let result = SyncEngine::pull_from_master(&pool).await.expect("pull");
    assert_eq!(result.applied, 1);
    let val: String = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT value FROM app_kv WHERE key = 'pull.key'")
        .fetch_one(&pool)
        .await
        .expect("app_kv");
    assert_eq!(val, "from-master");
}

    deploy(
        &pool,
        &SyncDeploymentConfig {
            schema_version: 1,
            mode: DeploymentMode::ServerlessPeer,
            role: DeviceRole::Replica,

#[tokio::test]
async fn run_mesh_sync_fetches_signed_peer_list() {
    let pool = fresh_pool().await;
    let server = MockServer::start().await;
    let sk = SigningKey::from_bytes(&[7u8; 32]);
    let pubkey = master_keys::pubkey_b64(&sk);
    let master_id = "master-mesh-1";

    let peers_json = serde_json::json!([{
        "deviceId": "peer-other",
        "slaveLabel": "Peer B",
        "slavePubkey": "AAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAA=",
        "peerBaseUrl": format!("{}/", server.uri())
    }]);
    let canonical = serde_json::json!({
        "masterDeviceId": master_id,
        "peers": peers_json
    });
    let body_bytes = serde_json::to_vec(&canonical).unwrap();
    let signature = master_keys::sign(&sk, &body_bytes);

    Mock::given(method("GET"))
        .and(path("/api/v1/pairing/peers"))
        .respond_with(ResponseTemplate::new(200).set_body_json(serde_json::json!({
            "masterDeviceId": master_id,
            "peers": peers_json,
            "signature": signature
        })))
        .mount(&server)
        .await;

    Mock::given(method("POST"))
        .and(path("/api/v1/sync/push"))
        .respond_with(ResponseTemplate::new(200).set_body_json(serde_json::json!({
            "accepted": 0,
            "lastSeq": 0
        })))
        .mount(&server)
        .await;

    deploy(
        &pool,
        &SyncDeploymentConfig {
            schema_version: 1,
            mode: DeploymentMode::ServerlessPeer,
            role: DeviceRole::Replica,
            master_base_url: server.uri(),
            master_access_token: "mesh-tok".into(),
            master_pubkey: pubkey,
            master_device_id: master_id.into(),
            unstable_mesh: true,
            device_label: "mesh-on".into(),
            ..Default::default()
        },
    )
    .await
    .expect("deploy");
    let _local = ensure_local_device(&pool, "mesh-on").await.unwrap();

    let report = SyncEngine::run_mesh_sync(&pool).await.expect("mesh");
    assert!(report.attempted >= 1);
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn push_to_master_prefers_activation_token_over_jwt() {
    let pool = fresh_pool().await;
    let server = MockServer::start().await;
    deploy(
        &pool,
        &SyncDeploymentConfig {
            schema_version: 1,
            mode: DeploymentMode::ServerlessPeer,
            role: DeviceRole::Replica,
            master_base_url: server.uri(),
            master_access_token: "legacy-jwt".into(),
            activation_token: "activation-bearer".into(),
            device_label: "act-token".into(),
            ..Default::default()
        },
    )
    .await
    .expect("deploy");
    let device = ensure_local_device(&pool, "act-token").await.unwrap();
    append_outbox(
        &pool,
        &device,
        "app_kv",
        "act.key",
        "INSERT",
        r#"{"key":"act.key","value":"1"}"#,
    )
    .await
    .expect("outbox");

    Mock::given(method("POST"))
        .and(path("/api/v1/sync/push"))
        .respond_with(ResponseTemplate::new(200).set_body_json(serde_json::json!({
            "accepted": 1,
            "lastSeq": 1
        })))
        .mount(&server)
        .await;

    SyncEngine::push_to_master(&pool).await.expect("push");
    let reqs = server.received_requests().await.expect("requests");
    assert!(reqs[0]
        .headers
        .get("authorization")
        .is_some_and(|version| { version.to_str().unwrap_or("").contains("activation-bearer") }));
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn run_replica_sync_push_and_pull_success() {
    let pool = fresh_pool().await;
    let server = MockServer::start().await;
    deploy(
        &pool,
        &SyncDeploymentConfig {
            schema_version: 1,
            mode: DeploymentMode::ServerlessPeer,
            role: DeviceRole::Replica,
            master_base_url: server.uri(),
            master_access_token: "full-sync".into(),
            master_device_id: "master-device".into(),
            device_label: "replica-full".into(),
            ..Default::default()
        },
    )
    .await
    .expect("deploy");
    let device = ensure_local_device(&pool, "replica-full").await.unwrap();
    append_outbox(
        &pool,
        &device,
        "app_kv",
        "full.key",
        "INSERT",
        r#"{"key":"full.key","value":"sent"}"#,
    )
    .await
    .expect("outbox");

    Mock::given(method("POST"))
        .and(path("/api/v1/sync/push"))
        .respond_with(ResponseTemplate::new(200).set_body_json(serde_json::json!({
            "accepted": 1,
            "lastSeq": 1
        })))
        .mount(&server)
        .await;

    let remote_entry = serde_json::json!({
        "id": "full-pull",
        "deviceId": "master-device",
        "seq": 1,
        "entityTable": "app_kv",
        "entityId": "pulled.key",
        "op": "INSERT",
        "payloadJson": r#"{"key":"pulled.key","value":"down"}"#,
        "createdAt": "2099-01-01T00:00:00Z"
    });
    Mock::given(method("POST"))
        .and(path("/api/v1/sync/pull"))
        .respond_with(ResponseTemplate::new(200).set_body_json(serde_json::json!({
            "entries": [remote_entry]
        })))
        .mount(&server)
        .await;

    let report = SyncEngine::run_replica_sync(&pool).await.expect("sync");
    assert!(report.error.is_none(), "{:?}", report.error);
    assert_eq!(report.push.as_ref().map(|p| p.accepted), Some(1));
    assert_eq!(report.pull.as_ref().map(|p| p.applied), Some(1));
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn pull_from_master_counts_skipped_already_applied() {
    let pool = fresh_pool().await;
    let server = MockServer::start().await;
    save_replica(&pool, &server.uri(), "tok-skip", "pull-skip").await;
    let _device = ensure_local_device(&pool, "pull-skip").await.unwrap();

    let remote_entry = serde_json::json!({
        "id": "skip-e1",
        "deviceId": "master-device",
        "seq": 1,
        "entityTable": "app_kv",
        "entityId": "skip.key",
        "op": "INSERT",
        "payloadJson": r#"{"key":"skip.key","value":"version"}"#,
        "createdAt": "2099-01-01T00:00:00Z"
    });
    mark_applied(&pool, "master-device", 1).await.unwrap();

    Mock::given(method("POST"))
        .and(path("/api/v1/sync/pull"))
        .respond_with(ResponseTemplate::new(200).set_body_json(serde_json::json!({
            "entries": [remote_entry]
        })))
        .mount(&server)
        .await;

    deploy(
        &pool,
        &SyncDeploymentConfig {
            schema_version: 1,
            mode: DeploymentMode::ServerlessPeer,
            role: DeviceRole::Replica,
            master_base_url: server.uri(),
            master_access_token: "tok-skip".into(),
            master_device_id: "master-device".into(),
            device_label: "pull-skip".into(),
            ..Default::default()
        },
    )
    .await
    .expect("deploy");

    let result = SyncEngine::pull_from_master(&pool).await.expect("pull");
    assert_eq!(result.applied, 0);
    assert_eq!(result.skipped, 1);
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn run_mesh_sync_pushes_pending_outbox_to_peer() {
    let pool = fresh_pool().await;
    let master_server = MockServer::start().await;
    let peer_server = MockServer::start().await;
    let sk = SigningKey::from_bytes(&[9u8; 32]);
    let pubkey = master_keys::pubkey_b64(&sk);
    let master_id = "master-mesh-push";

    let peers_json = serde_json::json!([{
        "deviceId": "peer-target",
        "slaveLabel": "Peer Target",
        "slavePubkey": "AAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAA=",
        "peerBaseUrl": format!("{}/", peer_server.uri())
    }]);
    let canonical = serde_json::json!({
        "masterDeviceId": master_id,
        "peers": peers_json
    });
    let signature = master_keys::sign(&sk, &serde_json::to_vec(&canonical).unwrap());

    Mock::given(method("GET"))
        .and(path("/api/v1/pairing/peers"))
        .respond_with(ResponseTemplate::new(200).set_body_json(serde_json::json!({
            "masterDeviceId": master_id,
            "peers": peers_json,
            "signature": signature
        })))
        .mount(&master_server)
        .await;

    Mock::given(method("POST"))
        .and(path("/api/v1/sync/push"))
        .respond_with(ResponseTemplate::new(200).set_body_json(serde_json::json!({
            "accepted": 1,
            "lastSeq": 1
        })))
        .mount(&peer_server)
        .await;

    deploy(
        &pool,
        &SyncDeploymentConfig {
            schema_version: 1,
            mode: DeploymentMode::ServerlessPeer,
            role: DeviceRole::Replica,
            master_base_url: master_server.uri(),
            master_access_token: "mesh-push-tok".into(),
            master_pubkey: pubkey,
            master_device_id: master_id.into(),
            unstable_mesh: true,
            device_label: "mesh-pusher".into(),
            ..Default::default()
        },
    )
    .await
    .expect("deploy");
    let local = ensure_local_device(&pool, "mesh-pusher").await.unwrap();
    append_outbox(
        &pool,
        &local,
        "app_kv",
        "mesh.peer.key",
        "INSERT",
        r#"{"key":"mesh.peer.key","value":"peer-bound"}"#,
    )
    .await
    .expect("outbox");
    seed_peer_tls_pin(&pool, "peer-target").await;

    let report = SyncEngine::run_mesh_sync(&pool).await.expect("mesh");
    assert_eq!(report.attempted, 1);
    assert_eq!(report.succeeded, 1);
    assert!(peer_server
        .received_requests()
        .await
        .as_ref()
        .is_some_and(|r| !r.is_empty()));
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn run_replica_sync_includes_mesh_when_enabled() {
    let pool = fresh_pool().await;
    let server = MockServer::start().await;
    let sk = SigningKey::from_bytes(&[11u8; 32]);
    let pubkey = master_keys::pubkey_b64(&sk);
    let master_id = "master-replica-mesh";

    let peers_json = serde_json::json!([]);
    let canonical = serde_json::json!({
        "masterDeviceId": master_id,
        "peers": peers_json
    });
    let signature = master_keys::sign(&sk, &serde_json::to_vec(&canonical).unwrap());

    Mock::given(method("GET"))
        .and(path("/api/v1/pairing/peers"))
        .respond_with(ResponseTemplate::new(200).set_body_json(serde_json::json!({
            "masterDeviceId": master_id,
            "peers": peers_json,
            "signature": signature
        })))
        .mount(&server)
        .await;

    Mock::given(method("POST"))
        .and(path("/api/v1/sync/push"))
        .respond_with(ResponseTemplate::new(200).set_body_json(serde_json::json!({
            "accepted": 0,
            "lastSeq": 0
        })))
        .mount(&server)
        .await;

    Mock::given(method("POST"))
        .and(path("/api/v1/sync/pull"))
        .respond_with(ResponseTemplate::new(200).set_body_json(serde_json::json!({
            "entries": []
        })))
        .mount(&server)
        .await;

    deploy(
        &pool,
        &SyncDeploymentConfig {
            schema_version: 1,
            mode: DeploymentMode::ServerlessPeer,
            role: DeviceRole::Replica,
            master_base_url: server.uri(),

            master_pubkey: pubkey,
            master_device_id: master_id.into(),
            unstable_mesh: true,
            device_label: "replica-mesh".into(),
            ..Default::default()
        },
    )
    .await
    .expect("deploy");

    let report = SyncEngine::run_replica_sync(&pool).await.expect("sync");
    assert!(report.mesh.is_some());
    assert!(report.error.is_none(), "{:?}", report.error);
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn run_mesh_sync_records_peer_push_failure() {
    let pool = fresh_pool().await;
    let master_server = MockServer::start().await;
    let peer_server = MockServer::start().await;
    let sk = SigningKey::from_bytes(&[12u8; 32]);
    let pubkey = master_keys::pubkey_b64(&sk);
    let master_id = "master-mesh-fail";

    let peers_json = serde_json::json!([{
        "deviceId": "peer-fail",
        "slaveLabel": "Peer Fail",
        "slavePubkey": "AAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAA=",
        "peerBaseUrl": format!("{}/", peer_server.uri())
    }]);
    let canonical = serde_json::json!({
        "masterDeviceId": master_id,
        "peers": peers_json
    });
    let signature = master_keys::sign(&sk, &serde_json::to_vec(&canonical).unwrap());

    Mock::given(method("GET"))
        .and(path("/api/v1/pairing/peers"))
        .respond_with(ResponseTemplate::new(200).set_body_json(serde_json::json!({
            "masterDeviceId": master_id,
            "peers": peers_json,
            "signature": signature
        })))
        .mount(&master_server)
        .await;

    Mock::given(method("POST"))
        .and(path("/api/v1/sync/push"))
        .respond_with(ResponseTemplate::new(503))
        .mount(&peer_server)
        .await;

    deploy(
        &pool,
        &SyncDeploymentConfig {
            schema_version: 1,
            mode: DeploymentMode::ServerlessPeer,
            role: DeviceRole::Replica,
            master_base_url: master_server.uri(),
            master_access_token: "mesh-fail-tok".into(),
            master_pubkey: pubkey,
            master_device_id: master_id.into(),
            unstable_mesh: true,
            device_label: "mesh-fail-replica".into(),
            ..Default::default()
        },
    )
    .await
    .expect("deploy");
    let local = ensure_local_device(&pool, "mesh-fail-replica")
        .await
        .unwrap();
    append_outbox(
        &pool,
        &local,
        "app_kv",
        "mesh.fail.key",
        "INSERT",
        r#"{"key":"mesh.fail.key","value":"x"}"#,
    )
    .await
    .expect("outbox");
    seed_peer_tls_pin(&pool, "peer-fail").await;

    let report = SyncEngine::run_mesh_sync(&pool).await.expect("mesh");
    assert_eq!(report.attempted, 1);
    assert_eq!(report.succeeded, 0);
    assert!(report.errors.iter().any(|e| e.contains("503")));
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn replica_push_is_visible_to_a_later_replica_pull() {
    use medoc_sync::merge::{apply_remote_entry, ConflictPolicy};
    use medoc_sync::repo::OutboxEntry;

    let master = fresh_pool().await;
    deploy(
        &master,
        &SyncDeploymentConfig {
            schema_version: 1,
            mode: DeploymentMode::ServerlessPeer,
            role: DeviceRole::Master,
            device_label: "Master".into(),
            ..Default::default()
        },
    )
    .await
    .expect("master deployment");
    let local = ensure_local_device(&master, "Master")
        .await
        .expect("master id");
    let anchor = append_outbox(
        &master,
        &local,
        "app_kv",
        "relay.anchor",
        "INSERT",
        r#"{"key":"relay.anchor","value":"1"}"#,
    )
    .await
    .expect("anchor");

    let patient_id = "00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000000ab01";
    let payload = serde_json::json!({
        "id": patient_id,
        "name": "From Replica A",
        "date_of_birth": "1990-05-05",
        "sex": "FEMALE",
        "insurance_number": "INS-RELAY-A",
        "phone": null,
        "email": null,
        "address": null,
        "status": "ACTIVE",
        "created_at": "2099-01-01 00:00:00",
        "updated_at": "2099-06-01T00:00:00Z",
    });
    let entry = OutboxEntry {
        id: "replica-a-1".into(),
        device_id: "replica-a".into(),
        seq: 1,
        entity_table: "patient".into(),
        entity_id: patient_id.into(),
        op: "INSERT".into(),
        payload_json: payload.to_string(),
        created_at: "2099-06-01T00:00:00Z".into(),
    };
    let pushed = SyncEngine::ingest_push(&master, "replica-a", std::slice::from_ref(&entry))
        .await
        .expect("push");
    assert_eq!(pushed.accepted, 1);

    let pulled = SyncEngine::collect_pull_for_replica(&master, "replica-b", anchor.seq, 0)
        .await
        .expect("pull");
    let relayed = pulled
        .entries
        .iter()
        .find(|e| e.entity_id == patient_id)
        .expect("replica B must receive replica A's patient");
    assert_eq!(relayed.device_id, local);

    let replica_b = fresh_pool().await;
    let replica_b_id = ensure_local_device(&replica_b, "Replica B")
        .await
        .expect("replica b");
    apply_remote_entry(
        &replica_b,
        &replica_b_id,
        false,
        ConflictPolicy::LastWriteWins,
        relayed,
    )
    .await
    .expect("apply on B");
    let name: String = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT name FROM patient WHERE id = ?1")
        .bind(patient_id)
        .fetch_one(&replica_b)
        .await
        .expect("patient on B");
    assert_eq!(name, "From Replica A");
    let phone: Option<String> = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT phone FROM patient WHERE id = ?1")
        .bind(patient_id)
        .fetch_one(&replica_b)
        .await
        .unwrap();
    assert!(phone.is_none());
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn pull_cursor_stops_before_a_failing_row_until_retry_limit() {
    use medoc_sync::engine::PULL_ROW_RETRY_LIMIT;

    let pool = fresh_pool().await;
    let server = MockServer::start().await;
    save_replica(&pool, &server.uri(), "tok-cursor", "cursor-test").await;
    let _device = ensure_local_device(&pool, "cursor-test").await.unwrap();
    sqlx::query(
        "INSERT INTO sync_device (device_id, display_name, role, is_local)
         VALUES ('master-device', 'Master', 'MASTER', 0)",
    )
    .execute(&pool)
    .await
    .expect("master row");

    let bad = serde_json::json!({
        "id": "bad-1",
        "deviceId": "master-device",
        "seq": 1,
        "entityTable": "patient",
        "entityId": "missing-patient",
        "op": "NOPE",
        "payloadJson": "{}",
        "createdAt": "2099-01-01T00:00:00Z"
    });
    let good = serde_json::json!({
        "id": "good-1",
        "deviceId": "master-device",
        "seq": 2,
        "entityTable": "app_kv",
        "entityId": "cursor.key",
        "op": "INSERT",
        "payloadJson": r#"{"key":"cursor.key","value":"kept"}"#,
        "createdAt": "2099-01-01T00:00:00Z"
    });
    Mock::given(method("POST"))
        .and(path("/api/v1/sync/pull"))
        .respond_with(ResponseTemplate::new(200).set_body_json(serde_json::json!({
            "entries": [bad, good]
        })))
        .mount(&server)
        .await;

    async fn master_seq(pool: &sqlx::SqlitePool) -> Option<i64> {
        sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT seq FROM sync_vector WHERE device_id = 'master-device'")
            .fetch_optional(pool)
            .await
            .expect("seq")
    }

    let first = SyncEngine::pull_from_master(&pool)
        .await
        .expect("first pull");
    assert_eq!(
        first.applied, 1,
        "the row after the failure is still applied"
    );
    assert!(master_seq(&pool).await.is_none());
    let val: String = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT value FROM app_kv WHERE key = 'cursor.key'")
        .fetch_one(&pool)
        .await
        .expect("app_kv applied behind the hole");
    assert_eq!(val, "kept");

    for _ in 1..PULL_ROW_RETRY_LIMIT - 1 {
        SyncEngine::pull_from_master(&pool).await.expect("retry");
        assert!(master_seq(&pool).await.is_none());
    }
    SyncEngine::pull_from_master(&pool).await.expect("give up");
    assert_eq!(master_seq(&pool).await, Some(2));
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn large_outbox_drains_in_pages() {
    let pool = fresh_pool().await;
    let server = MockServer::start().await;
    save_replica(&pool, &server.uri(), "tok-pages", "page-test").await;
    let device = ensure_local_device(&pool, "page-test").await.unwrap();
    append_outbox(
        &pool,
        &device,
        "app_kv",
        "huge.key",
        "INSERT",
        &format!(
            r#"{{"key":"huge.key","value":"{}"}}"#,
            "x".repeat(13 * 1024 * 1024)
        ),
    )
    .await
    .expect("huge");
    for i in 0..520 {
        append_outbox(
            &pool,
            &device,
            "app_kv",
            &format!("page.key.{i}"),
            "INSERT",
            &format!(r#"{{"key":"page.key.{i}","value":"v"}}"#),
        )
        .await
        .expect("row");
    }
    Mock::given(method("POST"))
        .and(path("/api/v1/sync/push"))
        .respond_with(ResponseTemplate::new(200).set_body_json(serde_json::json!({
            "accepted": 500,
            "lastSeq": 1
        })))
        .mount(&server)
        .await;
    let result = SyncEngine::push_to_master(&pool).await.expect("push");
    assert!(result.accepted >= 1);
    let remaining = list_pending_outbox(&pool, &device, 10_000)
        .await
        .unwrap()
        .len();
    assert!(
        remaining < 520,
        "paged push should drain most rows, leftover {remaining}"
    );
    let failed: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar(
        "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM sync_outbox WHERE fail_reason = 'ENTRY_TOO_LARGE'",
    )
    .fetch_one(&pool)
    .await
    .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(failed, 1);
    let requests = server.received_requests().await.unwrap();
    assert!(
        requests.len() >= 2,
        "expected more than one page, got {}",
        requests.len()
    );
}

//! Engine validation-path tests (T-U1) — no live HTTP required.

use medoc_core::error::AppError;
use medoc_core::infrastructure::database::connection::{run_migrations, test_memory_pool};
use medoc_sync::deployment::{DeploymentMode, DeviceRole, SyncDeploymentConfig};
use medoc_sync::engine::SyncEngine;
use medoc_sync::repo::save_deployment;
use medoc_sync::schema::ensure_sync_tables;
use sqlx::SqlitePool;

async fn fresh_pool() -> SqlitePool {
    let pool = test_memory_pool().await.expect("pool");
    run_migrations(&pool).await.expect("migrate");
    ensure_sync_tables(&pool).await.expect("sync schema");
    pool
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn push_to_master_rejects_practice_desktop_mode() {
    let pool = fresh_pool().await;
    let err = SyncEngine::push_to_master(&pool)
        .await
        .expect_err("desktop");
    assert!(matches!(err, AppError::Validation(_)));
}

            schema_version: 1,
            mode: DeploymentMode::ServerlessPeer,
            role: DeviceRole::Master,

}

#[tokio::test]
async fn set_deployment_empty_label_uses_default_display_name() {
    let pool = fresh_pool().await;
    SyncEngine::set_deployment(
        &pool,
        SyncDeploymentConfig {
            schema_version: 1,
            mode: DeploymentMode::ServerlessPeer,
            role: DeviceRole::Replica,
            device_label: String::new(),
            ..Default::default()
        },
    )
    .await
    .expect("set");
    let snap = SyncEngine::status(&pool).await.expect("status");
    assert!(snap.peers.iter().any(|p| p.display_name == "This device"));
    let is_local: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar(
        "SELECT is_local FROM sync_device WHERE display_name = 'This device' LIMIT 1",
    )
    .fetch_one(&pool)
    .await
    .expect("local row");
    assert_eq!(is_local, 1);
    let role: String = sqlx::query_scalar(
        "SELECT role FROM sync_device WHERE display_name = 'This device' LIMIT 1",
    )
    .fetch_one(&pool)
    .await
    .expect("role");
    assert_eq!(role, "REPLICA");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn run_mesh_sync_rejects_master_role() {
    let pool = fresh_pool().await;
    save_deployment(
        &pool,
        &SyncDeploymentConfig {
            schema_version: 1,
            mode: DeploymentMode::ServerlessPeer,
            role: DeviceRole::Master,
            unstable_mesh: true,
            master_access_token: "tok".into(),
            ..Default::default()
        },
    )
    .await
    .expect("deploy");
    let err = SyncEngine::run_mesh_sync(&pool).await.expect_err("master");
    assert!(matches!(err, AppError::Validation(_)));
}

================================================================================
FILE: tests/install_plan_apply_tests.rs

//! Integration: apply install_plan writes deployment + locale.

use medoc_core::infrastructure::database::connection::{run_migrations, test_memory_pool};
use medoc_core::infrastructure::database::repos::admin::app_kv as app_kv_repo;
use medoc_core::infrastructure::install_plan::{
    DiscoverMode, InstallPlan, InstallRole, APP_KV_INSTALL_PLAN_PROVISIONING,
};
use medoc_sync::cluster::services::apply_install_plan;
use medoc_sync::deployment::APP_KV_DEPLOYMENT_KEY;
use medoc_sync::schema::ensure_sync_tables;

async fn fresh_pool() -> sqlx::SqlitePool {
    let pool = test_memory_pool().await.expect("pool");
    run_migrations(&pool).await.expect("migrate");
    ensure_sync_tables(&pool).await.expect("sync schema");
    pool
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn apply_install_plan_sets_deployment_and_locale() {
    let pool = fresh_pool().await;

    let mut plan = InstallPlan::new_master("Integration Master");
    plan.locale = "de".into();
    plan.discover.window_minutes = 45;

    let result = apply_install_plan(&pool, &plan).await.unwrap();
    assert!(result.applied);
    assert_eq!(result.locale.as_deref(), Some("de"));

    let dep_raw = app_kv_repo::get(&pool, APP_KV_DEPLOYMENT_KEY)
        .await
        .unwrap()
        .unwrap();
    assert!(dep_raw.contains("serverless_peer"));
    assert!(dep_raw.contains("MASTER"));

    let prefs = app_kv_repo::get(&pool, "practice.preferences.v1")
        .await
        .unwrap()
        .unwrap();
    assert!(prefs.contains("\"locale\":\"de\""));

    let prov = app_kv_repo::get(&pool, APP_KV_INSTALL_PLAN_PROVISIONING)
        .await
        .unwrap();
    assert!(prov.is_some());
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn apply_install_plan_replica_fixed_url() {
    let pool = fresh_pool().await;

    let mut plan = InstallPlan::new_master("Replica");
    plan.role = InstallRole::Replica;
    plan.discover.mode = DiscoverMode::Fixed;
    plan.discover.address = "10.0.0.5".into();
    plan.discover.port = 8787;

    apply_install_plan(&pool, &plan).await.unwrap();
    let dep_raw = app_kv_repo::get(&pool, APP_KV_DEPLOYMENT_KEY)
        .await
        .unwrap()
        .unwrap();
    assert!(dep_raw.contains("https://10.0.0.5:8787"));
    assert!(dep_raw.contains("REPLICA"));
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn consume_sidecar_then_gone() {
    let pool = fresh_pool().await;
    let dir = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("medoc-sidecar-{}", uuid::Uuid::new_v4()));
    std::fs::create_dir_all(&dir).unwrap();
    let path = dir.join("install_plan.pending.json");

    let plan = InstallPlan::new_master("Sidecar Master");
    medoc_core::infrastructure::usb_vault::write_sidecar_plan(&plan, &path).unwrap();
    assert!(path.exists());

    let result = medoc_sync::cluster::services::consume_pending_sidecar_and_apply(&pool, &path)
        .await
        .unwrap()
        .expect("applied");
    assert!(result.applied);
    assert!(!path.exists());

    let dep_raw = app_kv_repo::get(&pool, APP_KV_DEPLOYMENT_KEY)
        .await
        .unwrap()
        .unwrap();
    assert!(dep_raw.contains("MASTER"));
    let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(dir);
}

================================================================================
FILE: tests/merge_apply_tests.rs
================================================================================
//! Unit tests for `medoc_sync::merge` apply paths (T-U1).

use medoc_core::error::AppError;
use medoc_core::infrastructure::database::connection::{run_migrations, test_memory_pool};
use medoc_sync::merge::{apply_remote_entry, apply_remote_entry_phased, ConflictPolicy};
use medoc_sync::repo::{mark_applied, was_applied, OutboxEntry};
use medoc_sync::schema::ensure_sync_tables;
use sqlx::SqlitePool;

const LOCAL_MASTER: &str = "local-master-id";
const LOCAL_REPLICA: &str = "local-replica-id";

async fn fresh_pool() -> SqlitePool {
    let pool = test_memory_pool().await.expect("pool");
    run_migrations(&pool).await.expect("migrate");
    ensure_sync_tables(&pool).await.expect("sync schema");
    pool
}

fn entry(device: &str, seq: i64, table: &str, id: &str, op: &str, payload: &str) -> OutboxEntry {
    OutboxEntry {
        id: format!("e{seq}"),
        device_id: device.into(),
        seq,
        entity_table: table.into(),
        entity_id: id.into(),
        op: op.into(),
        payload_json: payload.into(),
        created_at: "2099-01-01T00:00:00Z".into(),
    }
}

async fn seed_patient(pool: &SqlitePool, id: &str, name: &str, updated_at: &str) {
    sqlx::query(
        "INSERT INTO patient (id, name, date_of_birth, sex, insurance_number,
                              phone, email, address, status, created_at, updated_at)
         VALUES (?1, ?2, '1990-01-01', 'MALE', 'V-1', NULL, NULL, NULL, 'ACTIVE',
                 '2099-01-01 00:00:00', ?3)",
    )
    .bind(id)
    .bind(name)
    .bind(updated_at)
    .execute(pool)
    .await
    .expect("seed patient");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn apply_delete_removes_patient_row() {
    let pool = fresh_pool().await;
    let id = "00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001";
    seed_patient(&pool, id, "To Delete", "2099-01-01 00:00:00").await;
    let e = entry("remote", 1, "patient", id, "DELETE", r#"{"id":"x"}"#);
    assert!(
        apply_remote_entry(&pool, LOCAL_MASTER, true, ConflictPolicy::LastWriteWins, &e)
            .await
            .unwrap()
    );
    let n: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM patient WHERE id = ?1")
        .bind(id)
        .fetch_one(&pool)
        .await
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(n, 0);
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn apply_delete_patient_with_appointment_does_not_fk_fail() {
    let pool = fresh_pool().await;
    let id = "00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000aa";
    seed_patient(&pool, id, "Has Apt", "2099-01-01 00:00:00").await;
    sqlx::query(
        "INSERT INTO appointment (id, date, time, kind, status, patient_id, physician_id)
         VALUES ('apt-fk-1', '2099-01-02', '09:00', 'CHECKUP', 'PLANNED', ?1, 'seed-physician-001')",
    )
    .bind(id)
    .execute(&pool)
    .await
    .expect("appointment");
    let e = entry("remote", 9, "patient", id, "DELETE", r#"{"id":"x"}"#);
    apply_remote_entry(&pool, LOCAL_MASTER, true, ConflictPolicy::LastWriteWins, &e)
        .await
        .expect("patient delete with appointment");
    let n: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM patient WHERE id = ?1")
        .bind(id)
        .fetch_one(&pool)
        .await
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(n, 0);
}

#[tokio::test]

        .unwrap();

        .await

#[tokio::test]
async fn apply_skips_when_already_applied() {
    let pool = fresh_pool().await;
    let e = entry(
        "remote",
        5,
        "app_kv",
        "k5",
        "INSERT",
        r#"{"key":"k5","value":"version"}"#,
    );
    mark_applied(&pool, "remote", 5).await.unwrap();
    assert!(
        !apply_remote_entry(&pool, LOCAL_MASTER, true, ConflictPolicy::LastWriteWins, &e)
            .await
            .unwrap()
    );
    assert!(was_applied(&pool, "remote", 5).await.unwrap());
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn apply_inserts_new_patient_from_remote() {
    let pool = fresh_pool().await;
    let id = "00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000002";
    let payload = format!(
        r#"{{"id":"{id}","name":"Remote New","date_of_birth":"1991-02-02","sex":"FEMALE",
            "insurance_number":"V-2","status":"ACTIVE","created_at":"2099-01-01 00:00:00",
            "updated_at":"2099-02-01T00:00:00Z"}}"#
    );
    let e = entry("remote", 6, "patient", id, "INSERT", &payload);
    assert!(apply_remote_entry(
        &pool,
        LOCAL_REPLICA,
        false,
        ConflictPolicy::LastWriteWins,
        &e
    )
    .await
    .unwrap());
    let name: String = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT name FROM patient WHERE id = ?1")
        .bind(id)
        .fetch_one(&pool)
        .await
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(name, "Remote New");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn clock_two_minutes_ahead_overwrites_and_a_slow_clock_does_not() {
    let pool = fresh_pool().await;
    let ahead = "00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000015";
    seed_patient(&pool, ahead, "Local Noon", "2099-06-01 12:00:00").await;
    let payload = format!(
        r#"{{"id":"{ahead}","name":"Replica Two Minutes Fast","updated_at":"2099-06-01T12:02:00Z"}}"#
    );
    let e = entry("replica-fast", 15, "patient", ahead, "UPDATE", &payload);
    apply_remote_entry(&pool, LOCAL_MASTER, true, ConflictPolicy::LastWriteWins, &e)
        .await
        .unwrap();
    let name: String = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT name FROM patient WHERE id = ?1")
        .bind(ahead)
        .fetch_one(&pool)
        .await
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(name, "Replica Two Minutes Fast");

    let payload =
        format!(r#"{{"id":"{ahead}","name":"Replica Slow","updated_at":"2099-06-01T12:00:00Z"}}"#);
    let e = entry("replica-slow", 16, "patient", ahead, "UPDATE", &payload);
    apply_remote_entry(&pool, LOCAL_MASTER, true, ConflictPolicy::LastWriteWins, &e)
        .await
        .unwrap();
    let name: String = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT name FROM patient WHERE id = ?1")
        .bind(ahead)
        .fetch_one(&pool)
        .await
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(name, "Replica Two Minutes Fast");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_slow_wall_clock_wins_when_its_logical_stamp_is_newer() {
    let pool = fresh_pool().await;
    let id = "00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000017";
    seed_patient(&pool, id, "Master Noon", "2099-06-01 12:00:00").await;
    sqlx::query(
        "INSERT INTO sync_row_hlc (entity_table, entity_id, wall_ms, counter)
         VALUES ('patient', ?1, 1700000000000, 2)",
    )
    .bind(id)
    .execute(&pool)
    .await
    .unwrap();
    let payload = format!(
        r#"{{"id":"{id}","name":"Replica After Master","updated_at":"2099-06-01T11:00:00Z","syncHlc":"1700000000000:3"}}"#
    );
    let e = entry("replica-slow", 17, "patient", id, "UPDATE", &payload);
    apply_remote_entry(&pool, LOCAL_MASTER, true, ConflictPolicy::LastWriteWins, &e)
        .await
        .unwrap();
    let name: String = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT name FROM patient WHERE id = ?1")
        .bind(id)
        .fetch_one(&pool)
        .await
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(name, "Replica After Master");

    let behind = format!(
        r#"{{"id":"{id}","name":"Replica Behind","updated_at":"2099-06-01T13:00:00Z","syncHlc":"1700000000000:1"}}"#
    );
    let e = entry("replica-behind", 18, "patient", id, "UPDATE", &behind);
    apply_remote_entry(&pool, LOCAL_MASTER, true, ConflictPolicy::LastWriteWins, &e)
        .await
        .unwrap();
    let name: String = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT name FROM patient WHERE id = ?1")
        .bind(id)
        .fetch_one(&pool)
        .await
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(name, "Replica After Master");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn apply_replica_accepts_admin_pull_without_timestamps() {
    let pool = fresh_pool().await;
    let id = "00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000007b";
    seed_patient(&pool, id, "Replica Local", "2099-01-01 00:00:00").await;
    let payload = format!(r#"{{"id":"{id}","name":"Admin Authoritative"}}"#);
    let e = entry("master", 12, "patient", id, "UPDATE", &payload);
    assert!(apply_remote_entry_phased(
        &pool,
        LOCAL_REPLICA,
        false,
        true,
        ConflictPolicy::LastWriteWins,
        &e,
    )
    .await
    .unwrap());
    let name: String = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT name FROM patient WHERE id = ?1")
        .bind(id)
        .fetch_one(&pool)
        .await
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(name, "Admin Authoritative");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn apply_app_kv_upsert_replaces_existing_value() {
    let pool = fresh_pool().await;
    sqlx::query("INSERT INTO app_kv (key, value) VALUES ('upsert.me', 'old')")
        .execute(&pool)
        .await
        .unwrap();
    let e = entry(
        "remote",
        13,
        "app_kv",
        "upsert.me",
        "UPDATE",
        r#"{"key":"upsert.me","value":"new","updated_at":"2099-12-01T00:00:00Z"}"#,
    );
    assert!(
        apply_remote_entry(&pool, LOCAL_MASTER, true, ConflictPolicy::LastWriteWins, &e)
            .await
            .unwrap()
    );
    let val: String = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT value FROM app_kv WHERE key = 'upsert.me'")
        .fetch_one(&pool)
        .await
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(val, "new");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn apply_rejects_invalid_json_payload() {
    let pool = fresh_pool().await;
    let e = entry("remote", 14, "patient", "x", "INSERT", r#"{"id":}"#);
    let err = apply_remote_entry(&pool, LOCAL_MASTER, true, ConflictPolicy::LastWriteWins, &e)
        .await
        .expect_err("bad json");
    assert!(matches!(err, AppError::Validation(_)));
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn apply_update_with_only_id_is_noop_but_marks_applied() {
    let pool = fresh_pool().await;
    let id = "00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000008";
    seed_patient(&pool, id, "Unchanged", "2099-01-01 00:00:00").await;
    let payload = format!(r#"{{"id":"{id}"}}"#);
    let e = entry("remote", 15, "patient", id, "UPDATE", &payload);
    assert!(
        apply_remote_entry(&pool, LOCAL_MASTER, true, ConflictPolicy::LastWriteWins, &e)
            .await
            .unwrap()
    );
    let name: String = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT name FROM patient WHERE id = ?1")
        .bind(id)
        .fetch_one(&pool)
        .await
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(name, "Unchanged");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn apply_replica_accepts_master_tie_break() {
    let pool = fresh_pool().await;
    let id = "00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000009";
    seed_patient(&pool, id, "Replica Tie", "2099-05-01 00:00:00").await;
    let payload =
        format!(r#"{{"id":"{id}","name":"Master Wins Tie","updated_at":"2099-05-01T00:00:00Z"}}"#);
    let e = entry("master", 16, "patient", id, "UPDATE", &payload);
    assert!(apply_remote_entry_phased(
        &pool,
        LOCAL_REPLICA,
        false,
        true,
        ConflictPolicy::LastWriteWins,
        &e,
    )
    .await
    .unwrap());
    let name: String = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT name FROM patient WHERE id = ?1")
        .bind(id)
        .fetch_one(&pool)
        .await
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(name, "Master Wins Tie");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn apply_insert_coerces_json_scalar_types() {
    let pool = fresh_pool().await;
    let id = "00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000010";
    let payload = format!(
        r#"{{"id":"{id}","name":"Typed","date_of_birth":"1992-03-03","sex":"MALE",
            "insurance_number":"V-T","status":"ACTIVE","created_at":"2099-01-01 00:00:00",
            "updated_at":"2099-04-01T00:00:00Z","phone":null,"email":true}}"#
    );
    let e = entry("remote", 17, "patient", id, "INSERT", &payload);
    assert!(
        apply_remote_entry(&pool, LOCAL_MASTER, true, ConflictPolicy::LastWriteWins, &e)
            .await
            .unwrap()
    );
    let phone: Option<String> = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT phone FROM patient WHERE id = ?1")
        .bind(id)
        .fetch_optional(&pool)
        .await
        .unwrap()
        .flatten();
    assert!(phone.is_none() || phone.as_deref() == Some(""));
}

    );

#[tokio::test]
async fn apply_payment_without_updated_at_column() {
    let pool = fresh_pool().await;
    let patient_id = "00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000ab";
    seed_patient(&pool, patient_id, "Payer", "2099-01-01 00:00:00").await;
    let pay_id = "pay-no-updated-at";
    sqlx::query(
        "INSERT INTO payment (id, patient_id, amount, payment_method, status, created_at)
         VALUES (?1, ?2, 10.0, 'CASH', 'PAID', '2099-01-01 00:00:00')",
    )
    .bind(pay_id)
    .bind(patient_id)
    .execute(&pool)
    .await
    .expect("seed payment");
    let payload = format!(
        r#"{{"id":"{pay_id}","patient_id":"{patient_id}","amount":12.5,"payment_method":"CASH","status":"PAID","created_at":"2099-02-01 00:00:00","updated_at":"2099-02-01T00:00:00Z"}}"#
    );
    let e = entry("master", 21, "payment", pay_id, "UPDATE", &payload);
    assert!(apply_remote_entry_phased(
        &pool,
        LOCAL_REPLICA,
        false,
        true,
        ConflictPolicy::LastWriteWins,
        &e
    )
    .await
    .unwrap());
    let amount: f64 = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT amount FROM payment WHERE id = ?1")
        .bind(pay_id)
        .fetch_one(&pool)
        .await
        .unwrap();
    assert!((amount - 12.5).abs() < f64::EPSILON);
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn json_null_stays_sql_null() {
    let pool = fresh_pool().await;
    let staff_id = "seed-physician-001";
    let patient_id = "00000000-0000-0000-0000-0000000000cc";
    let patient_payload = format!(
        r#"{{"id":"{patient_id}","name":"Nullable","date_of_birth":"1991-01-01","sex":"FEMALE",
            "insurance_number":"V-NULL","phone":null,"email":null,"address":null,
            "status":"ACTIVE","created_at":"2099-01-01 00:00:00","updated_at":"2099-01-01T00:00:00Z"}}"#
    );
    let patient = entry(
        "remote",
        30,
        "patient",
        patient_id,
        "INSERT",
        &patient_payload,
    );
    apply_remote_entry(
        &pool,
        LOCAL_MASTER,
        true,
        ConflictPolicy::LastWriteWins,
        &patient,
    )
    .await
    .expect("patient insert with nulls");
    let phone: Option<String> = sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT phone FROM patient WHERE id = ?1")
        .bind(patient_id)
        .fetch_one(&pool)
        .await
        .unwrap();
    assert!(phone.is_none(), "JSON null must bind as SQL NULL, not ''");

    let pay_id = "pay-null-service";
    let pay_payload = format!(
        r#"{{"id":"{pay_id}","patient_id":"{patient_id}","amount":5.0,"payment_method":"CASH",
            "status":"PAID","service_item_id":null,"description":null,"created_at":"2099-01-01 00:00:00"}}"#
    );
    let payment = entry("remote", 31, "payment", pay_id, "INSERT", &pay_payload);
    apply_remote_entry(
        &pool,
        LOCAL_MASTER,
        true,
        ConflictPolicy::LastWriteWins,
        &payment,
    )
    .await
    .expect("payment with null service_item_id must satisfy the foreign key");
    let service_item_id: Option<String> =
        sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT service_item_id FROM payment WHERE id = ?1")
            .bind(pay_id)
            .fetch_one(&pool)
            .await
            .unwrap();
    assert!(service_item_id.is_none());

    let note_id = "note-unread";
    let note_payload = format!(
        r#"{{"id":"{note_id}","user_id":"{staff_id}","kind":"INFO","title":"Unread","body":"Body",
            "payload_json":null,"read_at":null,"created_at":"2099-01-01 00:00:00"}}"#
    );
    let note = entry(
        "remote",
        32,
        "in_app_notification",
        note_id,
        "INSERT",
        &note_payload,
    );
    apply_remote_entry(
        &pool,
        LOCAL_MASTER,
        true,
        ConflictPolicy::LastWriteWins,
        &note,
    )
    .await
    .expect("notification");
    let unread: i64 = sqlx::query_scalar(
        "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM in_app_notification WHERE id = ?1 AND read_at IS NULL",
    )
    .bind(note_id)
    .fetch_one(&pool)
    .await
    .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(unread, 1, "unread filter must still match a null read_at");
}

async fn ensure_seed_chart(pool: &SqlitePool) {
    sqlx::query(
        "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO patient (id, name, date_of_birth, sex, insurance_number, status)
         VALUES ('seed-patient-001', 'Seed Patient', '1980-01-01', 'FEMALE', 'SEED-INS-001', 'ACTIVE')",
    )
    .execute(pool)
    .await
    .expect("seed patient");
    sqlx::query(
        "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO patient_chart (id, patient_id) VALUES ('seed-chart-001', 'seed-patient-001')",
    )
    .execute(pool)
    .await
    .expect("seed chart");
}

async fn seed_examination(pool: &SqlitePool, id: &str, complaint: &str, updated_at: &str) {
    ensure_seed_chart(pool).await;
    sqlx::query(
        "INSERT INTO examination (id, chart_id, chief_complaint, updated_at)
         VALUES (?1, 'seed-chart-001', ?2, ?3)",
    )
    .bind(id)
    .bind(complaint)
    .bind(updated_at)
    .execute(pool)
    .await
    .expect("seed examination");
}

async fn seed_payment_row(
    pool: &SqlitePool,
    id: &str,
    patient_id: &str,
    amount: f64,
    updated_at: &str,
) {
    sqlx::query(
        "INSERT INTO payment (id, patient_id, amount, payment_method, status, updated_at)
         VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, 'CASH', 'OUTSTANDING', ?4)",
    )
    .bind(id)
    .bind(patient_id)
    .bind(amount)
    .bind(updated_at)
    .execute(pool)
    .await
    .expect("seed payment");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn later_edit_wins_on_both_devices_for_examination() {
    let pool = fresh_pool().await;
    let id = "exam-lww-1";
    seed_examination(&pool, id, "older", "2099-01-01T00:00:00Z").await;
    let payload = format!(
        r#"{{"id":"{id}","chart_id":"seed-chart-001","chief_complaint":"newer","updated_at":"2099-06-01T00:00:00Z"}}"#
    );
    let e = entry("replica-a", 40, "examination", id, "UPDATE", &payload);
    apply_remote_entry(&pool, LOCAL_MASTER, true, ConflictPolicy::LastWriteWins, &e)
        .await
        .unwrap();
    let complaint: String =
        sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT chief_complaint FROM examination WHERE id = ?1")
            .bind(id)
            .fetch_one(&pool)
            .await
            .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(complaint, "newer");

    let replica = fresh_pool().await;
    seed_examination(&replica, id, "older", "2099-01-01T00:00:00Z").await;
    apply_remote_entry_phased(
        &replica,
        LOCAL_REPLICA,
        false,
        true,
        ConflictPolicy::LastWriteWins,
        &e,
    )
    .await
    .unwrap();
    let complaint_b: String =
        sqlx::query_scalar("SELECT chief_complaint FROM examination WHERE id = ?1")
            .bind(id)
            .fetch_one(&replica)
            .await
            .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(complaint_b, "newer");
}

    seed_patient(&pool, patient_id, "Pay", "2099-01-01 00:00:00").await;

    );
    let e = entry("replica-a", 41, "payment", pay_id, "UPDATE", &payload);
    apply_remote_entry(&pool, LOCAL_MASTER, true, ConflictPolicy::LastWriteWins, &e)
        .await
        .unwrap();

        .bind(pay_id)
        .fetch_one(&pool)
        .await
        .unwrap();

}
